(4)	kottaṟuvāḷ.
iḵtu āṭṭukkaṟiyi aṟuppataṟkum kottuvataṟkum payaṉpaṭuvatu.
(5)	vīccaṟuvāḷ.
itu vīcip pakivarik kolvataṟku utavuvatu.
iṅṅaṉam palvēṟu aṟuvāṭkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭil toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅki varavum, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟikkūṭa aṟuvāḷ eṉak kuṟiyātu ellāvaṟṟiyum arivāḷ eṉṟē ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akara mutali kuṟittiruppatu varuntat takkatu. (vē.ka)
iṇippu
ar eṉpatu ōr olikkuṟippu. arittal1 ar eṉṉum oli tōṉṟa,  eli puḻu mutaliyavi oru poruḷit tiṉṉal allatu kuṟittal. ara - arā - aravu - aravam = oli, (iriyum) pāmpu. arittal2 nuṇṇitāyolittal. kā : arikkuraṟpēṭi. arittal3 aṟuttal. kā : arivāḷmaṇi (ari) - (aram) - araṅku. araṅkal aṟuttal. araṅku = aṟi. āṟṟiṭikkuṟi. araṅku - araṅkam. araṅku - arakku - arakkaṉ = aḻippavaṉ. arittal4 veṭṭutal. kā : kōṭari (kōṭu + ari) = marak kiḷiyi veṭṭuṅkaruvi. kōṭari  - kōṭāri - kōṭāli. arittal5 arāvutal. ari + am = aram - aravu - arāvu. arittal6 arāvutal pōla uṭampiluṇṭākum namaccal. arippeṭuttal, pullarittal eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka. arittal7 arippeṭukkumpōtu coṟital pōla, viralkaḷāl oru poruḷi vārit tiraṭṭal. arippatu arici. arici pōṟ ciṟiya pal aricippal. ciṟiya nelli arinelli. ari = nuṇmi, aḻaku. arittal8 aḻittal. ari = pakivaṉ, ciṅkam, aracaṉ, tēvarkkaracaṉākiya intiraṉ. kōḷari = kollum ciṅkam. ari = aḻippavaṉ. aḻippatu. kā : murāri, jvarahari (va.) ari yari (va.). ari - am = aram = aḻivu. tuṉpam; E. Ger. harw. aram - aranti. ari + aṉ = araṉ = aḻippavaṉ, tēvaṉ, civaṉ. iṉi aram = civappu. araṉ = civaṉ eṉṟumām. aḻipput teyvaṅkaḷē mutaṉmutal vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. accamē teyva vaḻipāṭṭiṟku mutaṟ kāraṇam. aṇaṅku eṉṉum colli nōkkuka. aramakaḷir = tēvamakaḷir. itaṟku aracamakaḷir eṉṟu vittuvāṉ vēṅkaṭarāculu  reṭṭiyār avarkaḷ kūṟiyiruppatu poruntātu. cūrara makaḷirō ṭuṟṟa cūḷē eṉpatu kuṟiñcit teyvap peṇkaḷiyē  nōkkiyatu. (aram) - (arampu) - arampi = tēvamakaḷ rampā (va.). araṉ - haraṉ (va.). ari (peṇpāṟpeyar) = tirumāl. a ri - hari (va.) arō (īṟṟaci nili.) arō arā - arōvarā - arōkarā. aram.
maraṅkaḷuḷ aracupōl uyarntatu aracu (Ficus religiosa). araca ilipōl kulikkāy vaṭivuḷḷa (cordate) ilikaḷi yuṭiyatāyp pūvōṭu kūṭiyatu pūvaracu.  (o.mo.)
arakku
arakku - rākṣā
ma. mu¡F, f., tu. araku.
el = katiravaṉ. el-er-eri = neruppu, civappu, er-eruvi = kuruti, cempu, cemparuntu. er-ir-iratti = cempaḻa ilanti.
ir-ar-araṉ, arakku, arattam, aruṇam.
arakku = iṅkulikam, civappu.
arakku - arakkam = avalarakku (patiṟṟup. 30:27).
arakkāmpal, arakkuc cāyam, arakku mañcaḷ eṉpaṉa cenniṟap poruḷkaḷik kuṟittal kāṇka.  (va.va.75.)
aracac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ
aracarkku avar takutikkēṟpap pala aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ uḷa. avi aracac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ eṉappeṟum. avi, kulam, peyar, muṭi, kōl, māli, kaṭṭil, kuṭi koṭi, muttiri, muracu, kaṭimaram, kutiri, yāṉi, tēr, maṉi eṉap patiṉintu vakiya. ivaṟṟuḷ muṭiyoṉṟum mūvēntarkkuc ciṟappu eṉṟum, piṟavellām muttiṟa varacarkkum potuveṉṟum, kūṟuvatu marapu.
(1) kulam : mūvēntarum, katiravaṉ tiṅkaḷ neruppu ākiya muccuṭarit taṅkula mutalākak kūṟi vantaṉar. pāṇṭiyar tiṅkaṭ kulam; cōḻar katiravakkulam; cērar neruppukkulam. (kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiya  kumari nāṭākiya pāṇṭi nāṭṭil katiravaṉ kaḻi peruṅ kaṭumiyāyk kāyntu makkaḷ tiṅkaḷiyē virumpumpaṭi ceytataṉāl,  pāṇṭiyar tammit tiṅkaḷ vaḻiyiṉarākavum, kuṇapulamākiya cōḻanāṭu katiravaṉ eḻunticiyiliruntatāl, cōḻar tammik katiravaṉ vaḻiyiṉarākavum mali nāṭākiya cēra nāṭṭil mūṅkilum piṟavum oṉṟōṭoṉṟuraci aṭikkaṭi peruneruppup paṟṟiyatāl, cērar tammi neruppu vaḻiyiṉa rākavum kūṟikkoṇṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. kumari nāṭṭu veppattiṉālēyē, vēṉil iṉṉiḻal tarum vēmpiṉ pūmāliyi aṭiyāḷa māliyākavum, nīr vāḻ mīṉ vaṭivi muttiriyākavum, pāṇṭiyaṉ koṇṭāṉpōlum!)
(2) peyar: aracar peyarkaḷ, kuṭippeyar, iyaṟpeyar, paṭṭappeyar, virutuppeyar, ciṟappuppeyar, uyarvuppeyar, varicippeyar, tiṇinilip peyar, iyaṉilip peyar eṉa oṉpāṉ vakippaṭum.
mūvēntar kuṭippeyarkaḷ palappala. avaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmi yāṉavi. cēraṉ (cēral, cēralaṉ) cōḻaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpaṉa. piṟa kuṭippeyarkaḷ: cēraṉukku maliyaṉ, (malināṭaṉ, poṟiyaṉ,) vāṉavaṉ (vāṉavarampaṉ,) villavaṉ, kōti, utiyaṉ, kuṭavaṉ, kuṭṭuvaṉ, koṅkaṉ, pūḻiyaṉ eṉpavum; cōḻaṉukkuc ceṉṉi, kiḷḷi, cempiyaṉ, vaḷavaṉ, puṉaṉāṭaṉ eṉpavum; pāṇṭiyaṉukkuc ceḻiyaṉ, māṟaṉ, vaḻuti, mīṉavaṉ, vēmpaṉ, pañcavaṉ, kitavaṉ, kuriyaṉ, tamiḻnāṭaṉ eṉpavum; ākum. pāṇṭiyaṉukkuc ceḻiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar, paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟuc ceḻippāl ēṟpaṭṭatupōlum!
iyaṟpeyarāvatu peṟṟōriṭṭa piḷḷip peyar. mutalām irācarācac cōḻaṉiṉ piḷḷip peyar aruṇmoḻit tēvaṉ eṉpatu.
paṭṭappeyar eṉpatu, aracaṉ muṭi cūṭumpōtu puṉintu koḷḷum peyar. piṟkālac cōḻavēntar oruvar viṭṭoruvar tāṅkiya parakēcari irāca kēcari eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, piṟkālap pāṇṭiya vēntar oruvar viṭṭoruvar. tāṅkiya māṟavarmaṉ caṭāvarmaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ vēṅki nāṭṭil eytiya viṣṇuvarttaṉaṉ eṉṉum peyarum, paṭṭap peyarām.
virutup peyarāvatu, aracaṉ taṉ pakivari veṉṟapōtu ataṟkaṟikuṟiyākac cūṭikkoḷḷum koṟṟappeyar. muṭivaḻaṅku cōḻaṉ cōḻa kēraḷaṉ muṭittali koṇṭa perumāṉ kōṉēriṉmi koṇṭāṉ ulakuyyavanta nāyaṉār mutaliyaṉa 3 ām kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉiṉ koṟṟap peyarākum.
ciṟappup peyarāvatu, nalla vakiyilō tīya vakiyilō ētēṉumoru ciṟappuppaṟṟi, makkaḷ aracarkku iṭṭu vaḻaṅkum peyar. mutaṟkulōttuṅkaṉukku vaḻaṅkiya cuṅkantavirtta cōḻaṉ eṉpatu icic ciṟappup peyarum, 2 ām kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉukku viṇavar vaḻaṅkiya kirumikaṇṭa cōḻaṉ eṉpatu vacic ciṟappup peyarum, ākum.
uyarvup peyarāvatu makkaḷ aracarik kaṇṇiyamākak kuṟikkum peyar. iṟivaṉ, aṭikaḷ, tēvar, nāyaṉār, uṭiyār, perumāḷ, ulakuṭiya perumāḷ, eṉpaṉa aracaṉukkum; pirāṭṭiyār, nāycciyār, āḻvār eṉpaṉa aracikkum; makkaḷ nāyaṉār piḷḷiyār eṉpaṉa iḷavaracaṉukkum; periyatēvar eṉpatu iṟantu pōṉa aracaṉukkum; vaḻaṅkiya uyarvup peyarkaḷām.
varicip peyarāvatu aracaṉiṉ tarattik kuṟikkum peyar. vēḷ, maṉṉaṉ, kōvaṉ (kōṉ, kō), vēntaṉ eṉpaṉa varicip peyarām.
tiṇinilip peyarāvatu, kuṟiñci mulli marutam neytal pāli eṉṉum aintiṇi paṟṟiya peyar. veṟpaṉ, kāṉaka nāṭaṉ, ūraṉ, tuṟivaṉ, viṭali mutaliyaṉa tiṇinilip peyarām. ivi perumpālum kuṟunila maṉṉarkku vaḻaṅkum.
iyaṉilip peyarāvatu ētēṉum ōr iyal paṟṟi vaḻaṅkum peyar. cemmal, vaḷḷal mutaliyaṉa iyaṉilip peyarām.
tamiḻakam muḻuvatiyum aṭippaṭuttiya piṟkālac cōḻap pēraracar. mummaṭic cōḻaṉ, mummuṭic cōḻaṉ, tiripuvaṉa tēvaṉ, tiripuvaṉac cakkaravartti mutaliya koṟṟap peyarkaḷik koṇṭiruntaṉar. tamiḻakattōṭu piṟa nāṭukaḷiyum veṉṟa cōḻa pāṇṭiyap pēraracar. ellāntaliyāṉa perumāḷ, cakala puvaṉac cakkaravartti, irācarācaṉ, irācākkaḷ tampirāṉ mutaliya koṟṟap peyarkaḷiyum; em maṇṭalamuṅ koṇṭa ruḷiṉa eṉpatu pōṉṟa koṟṟa aṭimoḻikaḷiyum koṇṭiruntaṉar.
aracar pōṉṟē araciyarum peyarāl perumi kuṟikkap peṟṟaṉar. aracaṉuṭiya paṭṭattut tēvikkuk kōpperuntēvi allatu peruṅkōppeṇṭu eṉpatu varicip peyarāka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. orē perunāṭṭaracaṉ tēvikku ulaka muḻutuṭiyāḷ, avaṉi muḻutuṭiyāḷ, ulaka makātēvi mutaliya peyarkaḷum; mupperunāṭṭup pēraracaṉ tēvikku mūvulakuṭiyāḷ, tiripuvaṉa makātēvi, mutaliya peyarkaḷum; palperunāṭṭup pēraracaṉ tēvikku ēḻulakuṭiyāḷ eṉṉum peyarum; koṟṟap peyarāka vaḻaṅkivantaṉa.
(3) muṭi : muṭi eṉpatu makuṭam. tamiḻakam muḻuvatum niliyāka mūvēntar āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭirunta paḻaṅkālamellām, ammūvarkkē muṭiyaṇiyum urimiyiruntatu. piṟkālattil mūvēntarukkaṭaṅkāta uḷ nāṭṭuc ciṟṟaracarum muṭiyaṇintu koṇṭaṉar. muṭi cūṭiyar allāta aracar paṭṭaṅkaṭṭiyar āvar.
ōr aracaṉ piṟa aracari veṉṟapiṉ, avar muṭipōṟ ceyta poṉṉurukkaḷi māliyākak kōttu aṇintu koḷva tuṇṭu. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum avaṉ muṉṉōrum, cōḻa pāṇṭi yariyum aimperu vēḷiriyum, veṉṟamik kaṟikuṟiyāka, eḻumuṭi māliyoṉṟaṇintiruntaṉar.
āḷum aracaṉippōl iḷavaracaṉum muṭiyaṇintiruntāṉ. āyiṉ aracaṉmuṭi nīṇṭu kuvintum, iḷavaracaṉ muṭi kuṭṭi yākavum irunta ataṉāl, piṉṉatu maṇṭi eṉappaṭṭatu.
(4) kōl :  kōl eṉpatu, aracaṉ atikāra muṟiyil vīṟṟirukkumpōtum cellum pōtum kiyiṟ piṭittirukkum taṇṭu. atu maṇiyiḻittu ōviya vēlippāṭaminta poṟṟakaṭṭāl potiyap peṟṟiruppatu. kōl āṭcik kaṟikuṟiyātalāl, aracaṉuṭiya āṭci nērmiyāṉatāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatik kuṟittaṟku, atu nērāṉatākac ceyyap paṭṭirukkum. ataṉāl ataṟkuc ceṅkōl eṉṟu peyar. cemmi - nērmi. koṭumiyāṉa āṭci koṭuṅkōl eṉṉum peyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum. koṭumi - vaḷivu. āṭciyiṉ nērmiyāvatu aṟaneṟivaḻi nērē cellutal; ataṉ koṭumiyāvatu aṟaneṟiyiṉiṉṟu tiṟampi vaḷital.
(5) māli : māli eṉpatu aṭiyāḷa māli. pāṇṭiya ṉukku vēppamāliyum, cōḻaṉukku ātti māliyum, cēraṉukkup paṉampūmāliyum, aṭiyāḷa māliyām.
kuṟunila maṉṉarkkum ovvōr aṭiyāḷa māliyiruntatu. āyaṇṭiraṉukkuc curapuṉṉi māliyum, ēṟikkōṉukkuk kāntaḷ  māliyum, aṭiyāḷa māliyāka iruntaṉa.
(6) kaṭṭil: kaṭṭil eṉpatu irukki. aracaṉatu kaṭṭil aracukaṭṭil eṉṟum aracirukki eṉṟum aḻikkappeṟum. ataṉ kālkaḷ ari (ciṅka) vaṭivāy amintiruntataṉāl, ataṟku ariyaṇi (ciṅkācaṉam) eṉṟum peyar. talinakarilum piṟa pāṭivīṭukaḷilum aracaṉukku ariyaṇiyuṇṭu. tali nakaril, aracaṉ aracu vīṟṟirukkum ōlakkamaṇṭapattilum (Durbar Hall). amiccaroṭu cūḻum cūḻviṉimaṇṭapattilum, aṟaṅkūṟum maṉṟattilum, ovvōr ariyaṇiyirukkum.
ariyaṇikaṭkuc ciṟappuppeyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvatu marapu. caṭāvarmaṉ kulacēkarapāṇṭiyaṉukku, maturiyil maḻavarāyaṉ kaliṅkarāyaṉ muṉiyatariyaṉ eṉa mūvariyaṇikaḷum; vikkiramapāṇṭiyaṉukku, maturiyil muṉiya tariyaṉ eṉṉum ariyaṇiyum, irācēntirattil maliyatariyaṉ eṉṉum ariyaṇiyum, iruntaṉa.
(7) kuṭi : kuṭi eṉpatu, aracaṉ kuṭikaḷit tuṉpamākiya veyiliṉiṉṟu kāttu, avarkaṭku iṉpamum pātukāppumākiya niḻalittarukiṉṟāṉ eṉṉuṅ kuṟippup paṭappiṭikkappaṭum. periya veṇpaṭṭuk kuṭi. atu, pakalāyiṉum iravāyiṉum, aracaṉ atikāra muṟiyil irukkumiṭam uṭaṉiruntu cellumiṭam uṭaṉ cellum. atu niṟattālum vaṭivālum taṇmik kuṟippālum matiyi ottiruttalāl, matikkuṭi allatu mativaṭṭakkuṭi eṉṟum; koṟṟattik kuṟittalāl, koṟṟakkuṭi allatu veṇkoṟṟakkuṭi eṉṟum; peyar peṟum.
kōl eṉṉum peyar pōṉṟē, kuṭi eṉṉum peyarum ākupeyarāy āṭciyik kuṟikkum. ataṉāl, ōr aracaṉ oru perunāṭṭit taṉināyakamāy āḷvati, ulakamellām oru kuṭikkīḻ āḷtal eṉpar.
(8) koṭi : koṭi eṉpatu, aracaṉatu aṭiyāḷa vaṭivam eḻutiya tuṇi allatu paṭṭukkoṭi. atu ataṉ aṭiyāḷa vaṭivāṟ peyar peṟum. cēraṉukku viṟkoṭi; cōḻaṉukkup pulikkoṭi; pāṇṭiyaṉukku mīṉkoṭi (mīṉakkoṭi).
(9) muttiri : muttiriyeṉpatu, aracaṉuṭiya tirumukattil, miyālum arakkālum maṇ eṉṉum cāntālum poṟikkap paṭum aṭiyāḷa vaṭivam. mūvēntarkkum avaravar koṭiyi leḻutappaṭṭa vaṭivamē, muttiriyākavumiruntatu.
ōr aracaṉ, taṉṉāl vellappaṭṭa aracariṉ muttiri kaḷiyum, taṉ muttiriyuṭaṉ cērttup poṟippatu vaḻakkam. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ mummuttirikaḷiyum, atiyamāṉ ēḻu muttirikaḷiyum, uṭiyavarāyiruntaṉar.
(10) muracu : muracāvatu, ētēṉumoṉṟi aṟivittaṟku, allatu yārēṉum oru cārāri aḻittaṟku, aṭikkappaṭum pēriki eṉṉum perum paṟi. atu koṭi (tiyāka) muracu, muṟi (niyāya) muracu, pōr (vīra) muracu eṉa mūvakippaṭṭatākak kūṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ ciṟantatu pōrmuracu. ataṟkut taṉiyāka oru kaṭṭil uṇṭu. atu muracukaṭṭil eṉappaṭum.
pōr muracu, paṭi tiraṭṭuvataṟku nakarattilum, porunari ūkkuvataṟkup pōrkkaḷattilum, veṟṟi kuṟittaṟku īriṭattilum, iṭiyōcipaṭa muḻakkappeṟum. atu puliyi veṉṟa kollēṟṟiṉ cīvāta tōlāl pōrkkappeṟuvatu marapu.
puṉimarup paḻuntak kuttip puliyoṭu porutu veṉṟa
kaṉikura lurumuc cīṟṟak kataḻviṭiyurivi pōrtta
tuṉikural muracam
eṉṟu cintāmaṇiyum (2899).
kollēṟṟup pintōl cīvātu pōrtta 
mākkaṇ muracam
eṉṟu maturikkāñciyum (732-3), kūṟutal kāṇka.
pōrmuracu teyvattaṉmiyuḷḷatākavum veṟṟiyit taruvatākavuṅ karutappaṭṭataṉāl, ataṟku māliyum pīliyu maṇivatum, pali yiṭuvatum, ati nīrāṭṭuvatum, vaḻakkam.
(11) kaṭimaram : kaṭimaramāvatu, ovvōr aracakuṭiyiṉarālum, avaravar kuṭiyoṭu toṭarpuḷḷatākavum teyvattaṉmi yuḷḷatākavuṅ karutappaṭṭu, toṉṟu toṭṭup pēṇappaṭṭu varum ētēṉumoru vakiyāṉa kāvalmaram. atu talimi yaracarkkum ciṟṟaracarkkum potuvākum. pāṇṭināṭṭilirunta paḻiyaṉ eṉṉum kuṟunilamaṉṉaṉ, oru vēmpik kāval maramāka vaḷarttu vantāṉ. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ pakivaruḷ oruvaṉ kaṭappamarattik kāvalmaramākak koṇṭiruntāṉ.
kaṭimaram, cila aracarāl talinakaril maṭṭum taṉimaramākavum, cilarāl ūrtoṟum taṉimaramākavum, cilarāl cōlitoṟum taṉimaramākavum, vaḷarkkappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu.
(12) kutiri : aracaṉ ūruṅ kutiri paṭṭattuk kutiri eṉṟu aḻikkappeṟum. nalla maṇamum, vāḻippū maṭalpōṟ kātum, kāl mārpu kaḻuttu mukam mutuku ākiya aintuṟuppuk kaḷil veḷḷiyum, 82 viral (aṅkulam) uyaramum uḷḷa cēṅkutiri aracivuḷiyātaṟkut takkatu (piṅkalam).
kutirikaḷi, avaṟṟiṉ uṭaṟkūṟum naṭiyum kuralum iyalpum niṟamum tiṟamumpaṟṟi, paṟpala vakiyākap parinūl vakuttuk kūṟum. avaṟṟuḷ pāṭalam cēraṉatum, kōram cōḻaṉatum, kaṉavaṭṭam pāṇṭiyaṉatum, kantukam kuṟunila maṉṉaratum, ākum.
aracar kutirikaṭku iyaṟpeyar pōṉṟa ciṟappup peyarum iṭappeṟum, cilar tam peyariyē iṭuvatum uṇṭu. ōriyiṉ kutiri ōri eṉṟum, kāriyiṉ kutiri kāri eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa.
(13) yāṉi : aracaṉ ūrum yāṉi, aracuvā eṉṟum, paṭṭattiyāṉi eṉṟum, aḻikkappeṟum. kāl pōṉṟē vālum tutiyum nilattit toṭuvatum, pāl pōlum veḷḷi nakamuṭiyatum, kālālum vālālum tutiyālum kompālum uṭampāluṅ kolla vallatum, ēḻumuḻa vuyaramum oṉpatu muḻa nīḷamum patiṉmūṉṟu muḻac cuṟṟaḷavu muḷḷatum, ciṟiya kaṇṇum civanta puḷḷikaḷumuṭiyatum, uṭaliṉ piṉpuṟattilum muṉ puṟam ēṟṟamāyiruppatumāṉa, kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi aracuvāvātaṟkut takkatām (piṅkalam).
aracuvāviṟkum, iṭukuṟi pōṉṟa iyaṟpeyarum kāraṇak kuṟiyāṉa ciṟappup peyarum, iṭappeṟum.
(14) tēr : aracaṉ ūruntēr, maṟṟat tērkaḷiṉum uyarntum, ōviya vēlippāṭamintum, viliyuyarnta aṇikalaṅkaḷum ciṟanta kutirikaḷum pūṭṭap peṟṟum, irukkum.
potuvāka aracaṉ ūrum ūrtikaḷ mūṉṟum, aracamāṇpiṟ kēṟpa, oppuyarvaṟṟa āṭiyaṇi yalaṅkarippuṭiyaṉavāyirukkum.
caṅkakālattil, vēṭṭikkut tērilum kutiri mēlum celva tum, pōrum nakarvalamum nāṭukāvalum ākiya piṟavaṟṟukkut tērilum tēr cellāviṭattu yāṉimēlum celvatum, aracarkku vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. piṟkālattil tēr payaṉpaṭuttap peṟāmiyāl, vēṭṭi tavirap piṟavaṟṟiṟ kellām yāṉimītē aracar ceṉṟaṉar.
(15) maṉi : aracaṉ vāḻum maṉi araṇmaṉi eṉappaṭum. matilum kōpuramuñ curuṅkiyumākiya araṇuṭimipaṟṟi, atu araṇmaṉiyeṉappaṭṭatu. ataṟkuk kōyil paḷḷi nakar māḷiki eṉap piṟa peyarkaḷumuṇṭu.
paṭiyuṅ koṭiyuṅ kuṭiyum muracum
naṭinavil puraviyuṅ kaḷiṟun tērum
tāru muṭiyum nērvaṉa piṟavum
terivukoḷ ceṅkōl aracark kuriya
eṉṉuñ cūttirattil (1571). tolkāppiyar paṭiyiyum aracar ciṉṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ cērttuk kūṟiṉār.
aracac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ : muṭi, kuṭi, kavari, tōṭṭi, muracu, cakkaram, yāṉi, koṭi, matil, tōraṇam, nīrkkuṭam, pūmāli, caṅku, kaṭal, makaram, āmi, iṇikkayal, ciṅkam, tīpam, iṭapam, ācaṉam eṉa 21 vakippaṭuvatākac cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭu kūṟum. ivaṟṟuṭ pala, maṅkalap poruḷkaḷum araṇum uvamiyumām. (pa.ta.ā.)
aracar iyalpu
aracaṉukkum kuṭikaṭkumuḷḷa toṭarpu tantikkum makkaṭku muḷḷa toṭarpākum. kuṭikaḷi aravaṇittuk kāppavaṉē yaṉṟi, atikārattōṭu āḷpavaṉ ciṟanta aracaṉallaṉ. aracaṉik kuṟikkum peyarkaḷuḷ, kāvalaṉ eṉpatu oṉṟākum. aracaṉukkariya toḻil kuṭikaḷit tiṟamiyāyk kāppatē eṉṉum uṇmi, iccollāl viḷaṅkum. ōr aracaṉuṭiya kuṭikaḷi avaṉ aṭi niḻalār eṉṟu kūṟuvatum ikkaruttup paṟṟiyē.
mānilaṅkā valaṉāvāṉ maṉṉuyirkāk kuṅkālit 
tāṉataṉuk kiṭiyūṟu taṉṉāltaṉ paricaṉattāl
ūṉamiku pakittiṟattāl kaḷvarāl uyirtammāl
āṉapayam aintun tīrttu aṟaṅkāppāṉ allaṉō
eṉṟār cēkkiḻār.  - (tiru ārūr - 36).
aracaṉ kuṭikaḷit tuṉpaveyiliṉiṉṟu kāttaṟku aṭiyāḷa mākavē, veṇkoṟṟakkuṭi tāṅkiyuḷḷāṉ.
kaṇpora viḷaṅkumniṉ viṇporu viyaṉkuṭi
veyiṉmaṟik koṇṭaṉṟō vaṉṟē varuntiya
kuṭimaṟip patuvē kūrvēl vaḷava!  (puṟam - 35 : 19 - 21)
eṉṟār veḷḷik kuṭinākaṉār.
palakuṭi nīḻalun tamkuṭik kīḻk kāṇpar
alakuṭi nīḻa lavar  (kuṟaḷ. 1034)
eṉṟu aracaṉuṭiya nāṭṭik kuṭi niḻal, eṉṟu vaḷḷuvar kūṟiyatum ikkaruttup paṟṟiyē.  (col. 104.)
aracar illaṟa vāḻkki
mūvēntarum, perunila maṉṉar kuṭiyilum kuṟunila maṉṉar kuṭiyilum vēḷir kuṭiyilum peṇ koṇṭaṉar. avarukkup pala tēviyar iruntaṉar. avaruḷ talimiyāṉa kōpperun tēvikku aracaṉukkuḷḷa matippiruntatu. ōlakka maṇṭapattilum cūḻviṉik kūṭṭattilum nakarvalattilum, kōpperun tēviyum aracaṉoṭu kūṭa amarntiruppatu vaḻakkam.
tēvimāruḷ aracaṉāl ciṟappākak kātalikkappaṭupavaḷ kāmakkiḻatti eṉappaṭuvāḷ. oruvarkkē urimi pūṇuṅ kulapparattimakaḷāyk kāmaṅkāraṇamākat talimakaṉāl varintu koḷḷappaṭṭavaḷ kāmakkiḻatti eṉa, nampiyakap poruḷ kūṟum. utayakumaraṉ maṇimēkaliyi ivvakiyil maṇakka virumpiyirukkalām.
caṅkakālattaracar cilar cērip parattiyaroṭum toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. parattiyar cēriyiluḷḷa makaḷirkkuc cēripparattiyar eṉṟum, avaruḷ aracaṉāl kātalikkappaṭupavaṭkuk kātaṟparatti yeṉṟum, avaṉāl varintu koḷḷappaṭṭa parattikku iṟparattiyeṉṟum peyar. aracaṉ toṭarpu koḷḷum parattiyarellām, oru varkkē yurimi pūṇuṅkulap parattiyarākavē yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
aracaṉāṟ kātalikkappaṭum parattiyarellām, avaṉurimi yeṉa avaṉ peṟṟōrālēyē otukkappaṭṭu iḷamimutal vaḷarkkappaṭṭavar eṉṟu, iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuri yāciriyar kūṟuvar (pak 226-228).
iṉi, aracaṉ vēṭṭikkum ulāviṟkum ceṉṟaviṭattu yārēṉum ōr aḻakiya aracakaṉṉiyit taṉiyiṭattuk kāṇiṉ, avaḷik kaḷavumaṇam purivatumuṇṭu. neṭumuṭikkiḷḷi nākanāṭṭaracaṉ makaḷākiya pīlivaḷiyik kaṭaṟkariyiṟ kaṇṭu kaḷavumaṇam purintatāka, maṇimēkali kūṟum. iyalumiṭa mellām kaḷavu kaṟpākat toṭarum. aracaṉ maṇattiṟku ittuṇiyeṉa oru varampillātiruntatu.
araṇmaṉiyil makaḷir uṟivataṟkut taṉiyiṭamuṇṭu. atu uvaḷakam eṉappaṭum. atil aracaṉiyum iḷavaracariyun tavirap paruvamaṭinta āṭavar vēṟu yārum pukuvatilli. kuṟaḷaruñ cintarum peṇṭirum cōṉakap pēṭiyarum vāḷēnti uvaḷakattik kākkumāṟu amarttappaṭṭiruntaṉar. kaṉṉiyar uṟivataṟkut taṉimāṭam iruntatu. atu kaṉṉikimāṭam eṉappaṭṭatu. aracaṉ uvaḷakañ cellumpōtellām makaḷir parivāramē uṭaṉcellum. tēviyarukkut taṉittaṉi māḷiki iruntatu.
aracaṉ vēṟoruttiyik kātalittāṉ eṉṟō vēṟu kāraṇampaṟṟiyō, kōpperuntēvi ūṭiyirukkumpōtu, pulavar pāṇar mutaliyōr vāyilāy avaḷ ūṭalit tīrppatu aracaṉ vaḻakkam.
aracaṉukkum aracikkum, aṭimiyarum tōḻiyarum kuṟṟēvalarum parivārattārum meykāvalarumāka, paṟpala ūḻiyar iruntaṉar. araci cellumiṭamellām parivāra makaḷir eṇmaṅkala (aṭṭamaṅkala)p poruḷkaḷi ēntic celluvatu vaḻakkam.
nakiccuvi yuṇarccimikka aracar, tamconta iṉpattiṟ keṉṟu oru nakiyāṇṭiyi amarttikkoḷvatu muṇṭu. avaṉukkuk kōmāḷi allatu kōṇaṅki eṉṟu peyar.
araṇmaṉi vēlikkeṉṟu taṉiyāka, aivakik kammiyarum, mayirviṉiñaṉ vaṇṇāṉ cemmāṉ mutaliya kuṭimakkaḷum, piṟatoḻilāḷarum, amarttap peṟṟiruntaṉar. avari araṇmaṉit taṭṭāṉ araṇmaṉi mayirviṉiñaṉ araṇmaṉik kuyavaṉ eṉa aḻippatu vaḻakkam. araṇmaṉip poṟkollaṉ caṭṭiyaṇintiruntāṉ eṉṟu cilappatikāraṅ kūṟuvatāl, aracaṉoṭu neruṅkip paḻakupavarkkellām acciṟappaḷikkap paṭṭiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
aracaṉukku uṭpakiyum aṟipōku viṉiñarum paṟṟiya accamirukkum pōtu, araṇmaṉi maruttuvar avaṉ uṇavi aṉṉam cakkaravākam karuṅkuraṅku mutaliya uyirkaḷik koṇṭu nōṭṭañceyvar. aracaṉ uṇṭapiṉ avaṉukku veṟṟili maṭittuk koṭuppavaṉukku aṭippiyāṉ (aṭippikkāraṉ) eṉṟu peyar.
araṇmaṉic celavellām araciyaṟ celavākavē karutap paṭṭatu. araciyaṟ celavum araṇmaṉic celavum pōka eñciya potuvaki (cātāraṇa)p poruḷkaḷellām, koṭikkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. aracarkkē takum aruviliyaṇikalaṉ kaḷum, perumaṇikaḷum, peruntoki yāṉa poṟkācukaḷum, aracaṉ conta uṭimiyākap pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. kuṟippiṭṭa niṟikku mēṟpaṭṭa maṇikkaṟkaḷik kuṭikaḷ vittirukka vāvatu nāṭu kaṭattavāvatu kūṭāteṉṟum. aracaṉiṭattil oppu vittu vilipeṟṟuk koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, kaṇṭippāṉa aracavitiyiruntatu. aracaṉukkuc contamāka irunta nilattiṟkuk kaṇṭuḻavu eṉṟu peyar. araciyarkkuc ciṟu pāṭṭuc celaviṟ keṉṟu nilaṅkaḷum ūrkaḷum viṭappaṭṭiruntatākavum terikiṉṟatu.
aracaṉ iṉpamākap poḻutupōkkuvataṟkup pala vaḻikaḷ iruntaṉa. kaṇpōlum naṇparōṭu aḷavaḷāvalum, ōṅkiya ciṟappiṉ uyarnta kēḷvip pulavaroṭu payilalum, nakiyāṇṭi coṟkēṭṭalum, vaṭṭāṭṭu vallāṭṭu puṉalāṭṭu mutaliya viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷi yāṭalum, ilavantikic cōlikkum ceykuṉṟiṟkum neytalaṅkāṉalukkum ulāp pōtalum, pāṇar pāṭiṉiyar kaṇikiyariṉ icikēṭṭalum, viṟaliyar kaṇikiyar cākkiyariṉ kūttuk kāṇṭalum, vēṭṭi yāṭalum malivaḷaṅ kāṇalum, piṟavum, aracaṉ iṉpamākap poḻutupōkkum vaḻikaḷām. aracaṉ ulāppōkum pōtu tōḻiyaroṭu celvatu vaḻakkam. (aracaṉ tōḻiyaroṭu cellum iṉpavulāvum, nakarvalam varum veṟṟiyulāvum, vēṟupaṭṭavi) avarukku aracaṉoṭu meytoṭṭup payila vum viḷiyāṭavum urimiyuṇṭu.
viyamuñ civikiyu maṇikkā lamaḷiyu
muyyā ṉatti ṉuṟutuṇi makiḻcciyuñ
cāmarik kavariyun tamaṉiya vaṭippiyuṅ
kūr nuṉi vāḷuṅ kōmakaṉ koṭuppap
peṟṟa celvam piṟaḻā vāḻkkip
poṟṟoṭi maṭantiyar
eṉṉum cilappatikārap pakutiyāl (14: 126-131), aracaṉ parattiya roṭu ulāppōtalum, avarkku avaṉ paṟpala variciyaḷit talum, uṇṭeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
aracar, paruvamvanta putalvarit tuṇiyariyarāka allatu maṇṭalat talivarāka, vevvēṟiṭattamartti vippatu perum pāṉmi. avar tam putalviyari araca kuṭiyiṉarkkaṉṟip piṟarkkuk koṭuppatilliyeṉpatu, 3 ām kulōttuṅkaṉ ampikāpatiyik koṉṟatiṉāl aṟiyappaṭum.
nāṭukāval, cantu (pakitaṇiviṉi), pōr, naṭparacar viḻā mutaliya kāraṇampaṟṟi, aracaṉ taṉ tēviyariṭattiṉiṉṟu pirintu pōvaṉ. pirivukkālattil, iṉṟiyamiyum aṇikaḻaṟṟalum, meyyalaṅkariyāmiyum, aḷavāka vuṇṭalum, nāḷeṇṇik kaḻittalum, makiḻātiruttalum teyvatti vēṇṭalum; mīṭcikkālattil, varavu kaṇṭu makiḻtalum, viruntayartalum; tēviyar mēṟ koḷḷuñ  ceyalkaḷām.
aracaṉ iṟappiṉ, tēviyarum uṭaṉ iṟappar; allatu uṭaṉkaṭṭiyēṟuvar; allatu kimmi pūṇuvar. avar muṉṉiruvakiyil iṟappatē perumpāṉmi.  (pa.ta.ā.)
aracar kāla moḻi nilimi
mutaṟkālattil, tamiḻ ellāt tuṟikaḷilum taṉiyāṭci celutti  muḻuttūymiyāy vaḻaṅki vantatu. āyiṉ, iṭik kālattil vaṭamoḻi tēvapāṭi eṉṟu mūvēntarālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭataṉāl, purōkitar naṭattum pūci karaṇaṅkaṭkellām tamiḻ vilakkappaṭṭatu. vaṭacol vaḻaṅkuvatu uyarvu eṉṉuṅ karuttum nāḷaṭivil eḻuntatu. tamiḻaracarum oru cila tamiḻppulavarum tamiḻip puṟakkaṇikkavun talippaṭṭaṉar.
toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻccaṅkam irīit tamiḻi vaḷarttut tamiḻnāṭaṉ eṉṟu ciṟappikkappeṟṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ kuṭiyiṉarē, ki.pi.3 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil caṅkattik kulittuviṭṭaṉar. ataṉ piṉ, 11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil atip putuppikkumāṟu poyyā moḻippulavar ceyta perumuyaṟciyum vīṇāyp pōyiṟṟu.
vaṭacoṟkaḷ tolkāppiyar kālattilēyē, tamiḻil iṭam peṟat toṭaṅki viṭṭaṉa. āyiṉ, avar kālattil taṟpavamāka vum citivākavum orōvoṉṟāy arukiyē vaḻaṅkiṉa. piṟkālattilō, avi paṭippaṭiyāyp peruki vantatuṭaṉ, kaṭiccaṅkattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa kālattil, potu makkaṭkeṉ ṟēṟpaṭṭa kalveṭṭukkaḷil taṟcamamākavum kirantaveḻuttilum tōṉṟi, kalveṭṭut tamiḻiyē tamiḻarkku viḷaṅkātatum piḻi malintatumāṉa maṇippavaḷak kalavi moḻiyāka māṟṟi viṭṭaṉa. aracar peyar (aracarkaḷum avarkaḷiccārnta uṟaviṉarum) akattil kārikiḷḷi irukkuvēḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa paḻantamiḻppeyar tarittiruttal vaḻakkam. āyiṉ, uttiyōka muṟimiyil parāntakaṉ irājarājaṉ irājātittaṉ eṉṉum vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷit  tarippārkaḷ - cō. pak.71.) teyvap peyar iṭappeyar mutaliya palvakip peyarkaḷum, nāḷaṭivil vaṭacoṟkaḷāy māṟiṉa.

(taṭitta eḻuttuḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷ avaṟṟukku nērāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppākum). iṉi, cila teṉ coṟkaḷ tam teṉmoḻi vaṭiviḻantu vaṭamoḻi vaṭivil vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭaṉa. e-ṭu:

	paṭi				pirati
makkaḷit tākkiya kulappiriviṉi nāḷaṭivil ilakkiyat tiyum  tākkiṟṟu. veṇpā antaṇarpā eṉṟum, āciriyappā aracar pā eṉṟum, kalippā vaṇikarpā eṉṟum, vañcippā vēḷāḷar pā eṉṟum, vakukkappaṭṭatuṭaṉ; kalampakac ceyyuṭkaḷ tēvarkku nūṟum, antaṇarkkut toṇṇūṟ ṟintum, aracarkkut toṇṇūṟum, amiccarkku eḻupatum, vaṇikarkku aimpatum, vēḷāḷarkku muppatum pāṭappaṭum eṉavum ilakkaṇam vitikkappaṭṭatu. pāṇarit tīṇṭāmi tākkiyatāl. icittamiḻum nāṭakat tamiḻum 12 ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟkumēl vaḻakkoḻintaṉa. avaṟṟiṟkuriya nūlkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻ kaṟpārkkup pōtiya araciyalūkkuvippiṉmiyāl, mutaliru caṅkakālattum muttamiḻāyirunta ilakkaṇa nūl, kaṭiccaṅka  kālattil oru tamiḻāyum, 11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku mēl atuvum niṟiviṉṟi eḻuttuc collaḷavāyuṅ kuṟuki viṭṭatu.
tamiḻ vaṭamoḻik kiḷiyeṉak karutappaṭṭu, 11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇan taḻuvi vīracōḻiyam eṉṉum tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlum iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu.
kaṭiccaṅka kālattiliruntu āriyak karuttukkaḷum tamiḻilak kiyattiṟ pukuttappaṭṭataṉāl, tamiḻ varalāṟṟilum tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟilum purāṇakkaruttukkaḷ pukuntaṉa. piṟkālattu nūlkaḷellām perumpālum kalittuṟi paṟṟātu matattuṟiyē paṟṟiṉa.
14 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṭappaṭṭa villipāratattil, vaṭacoṟkaḷ varituṟiyiṉṟi āḷappaṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻppulavariyuṅ kaliñariyum pōṟṟuvāriṉmiyāl, nūṟṟukkaṇak kāṉa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. tūyatamiḻ tāḻttappaṭṭōr moḻi eṉat tāḻttappapaṭṭataṉāl nūṟ ṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷum maṟintoḻintaṉa.  (pa.ta.ā.)
aracar pākupāṭu
ciṟṟaracar talimiyaracar pēraracar eṉat tamiḻaracar muttiṟap paṭṭiruntaṉar. im muttiṟattārum, muṟiyē, kuṟunilavaracarum perunilavaracarum palanilavaracarum āvar. ciṟṟaracarkku maṉṉaṉ eṉṉum peyarum, talimiyaracarkku vēntaṉ kō (kōṉ) eṉṉum peyarum, marapākavuriya. iti,
kōkkaṇṭu maṉṉar kurikaṭal pukkilar
eṉṉum taṉippāṭaṟ ceyyuḷaṭiyālum,
vēntiṭi teritalvēṇṭi ēntupukaḻp
pōntē vēmpē āreṉa varūum
eṉṉum tolkāppiya aṭikaḷālum (1006), aṟiyalām.
pēraracarip peruṅkō allatu māvēntaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl aḻikkalām.
kō eṉṉum peyar vēntarukku maṭṭumaṉṟi, avarukkaṭaṅki avarnāṭṭuḷ ovvoru pakutiyi āḷum avar tāyattārkkum, ciṟṟaracaruḷ periyavarkkum vaḻaṅkum. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ tāyattārāṉa irumpoṟi marapiṉaruḷ oruvaṉāṉa cēramāṉ kuṭakkō iḷañcēralirumpoṟiyum, ciṟṟaracaruḷ oruvaṉāṉa viccikkōvum, kōveṉṟu peyar koṇṭiruntami kāṇka. vēntaṉ eṉṉum peyarō, eṉṟum talimiyaracaṉiyē kuṟikkum.
tamiḻakac ciṟṟaracar, eyiṉar vēṭṭuvar mutaliya pālinilak kuṭikaḷiṉ talivarākiya kuṟumparacarum, ciṟumalik kiḻavarum, uḻuvittuṇṇum vēḷāṇ kuṭiyiṉarāṉa vēḷirum; paṭit talivarum, araca kuṭumpattiṉarum, araciyaṟ pēratikārikaḷu mākap pala vakiyar; tolvaraviṉar putiyar eṉa irupālār; ūrkkiḻavar mutal maṇṭalat talivarvari pala tiṟattār.
koṅkar, kōcar, kaṅkar, kaṭṭiyar, paṅkaḷar, tuvari vēḷir, atikar, āviyar eṉṉum palakuṭic ciṟṟaracar cēraṉuk kaṭaṅkiya varākavum; pottappirāyar, kāṭavarāyar, pallavar (pallava tariyar), toṉṭiyar, campuvarāyar, ilāṭarāyar, maliya māṉar (cētirāyar), vāṇakōvariyar, muṉiyariyar (muṉiya tariyar), ōymāṉar, muttariyar, maḻavarāyar, paḻuvēṭṭa riyar, irukku vēḷir mutaliya palakuṭic ciṟṟaracar cōḻaṉuk kaṭaṅkiyavarākavum; āyar (āykuṭiyar), kaḷappāḷar (kaḷappirar) mutaliya cilakuṭic ciṟṟaracar pāṇṭiyaṉuk kaṭaṅkiyavarākavum; pala talimuṟiyiṉarā yiruntuvantaṉar. koṅkumaṇṭalam cōḻap pēraraciṟkuṭpaṭṭa, piṟkālattil, koṅkar kaṅkar nuḷampar mutaliyōr cōḻarukkaṭaṅkiyavarāyiṉar.
iṉi, ciṟṟaracarkkaṭaṅkiya kīḻc ciṟṟaracarumuṇṭu. vēṅkaṭa maliyic cārnta oru ciṟu nāṭṭut talivaṉāṉa karumpaṉūr kiḻāṉum, vēṅkaṭamalit talivaṉāṉa pulliyum, palkuṉṟak kōṭṭat talivaṉāṉa naṉṉaṉum, toṇṭi māṉukkaṭaṅkiya kīḻc ciṟṟaracar.
aṭaṅkā maṉṉari yaṭakku
maṭaṅkā viḷiyuḷ nāṭukiḻa vōyē  (puṟam 199)
eṉṟu viccikkō kapilarāṟ pāṭappeṟṟiruppatāl avaṉkīḻp  pala maṉṉariruntami aṟiyappaṭum.
iṉi, kaṭiccaṅka kālattil, akuti, eyiṉaṉ, erumiyūraṉ, ēṟikkōṉ, ēṉātitirukkiḷḷi, ōri, kaṭiyaneṭuvēṭṭuvaṉ, kumaṇaṉ, koṇkāṉaṅkiḻāṉ, ciṟukuṭikiḻāṉpaṇṇaṉ, taḻumpaṉ, tāmāṉtōṉṟikkōṉ, titiyaṉ, naḷḷi, nāli kiḻavaṉ nākaṉ, nāñcil vaḷḷuvaṉ kantaṉ, paḻiyaṉ, pāri, piṭṭaṅkoṟṟaṉ, mallikiḻāṉ kāriyāti, mūvaṉ, vallār kiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ, veḷimāṉ, vēṅkimārpaṉ eṉa aracakuṭi kūṟappaṭāta paṟpala ciṟṟaracarum kīḻcciṟṟaracarum tamiḻnāṭu muḻumiyumiruntaṉar.
tamiḻaracaruḷ, cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarākiya mukkuṭiyaracarum, vēntar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum talimiyaracarāvar. mutaṟkālattil avarkkē muṭiyaṇiyum urimiyiruntatu. itaṉālēyē, avar vēntar eṉappaṭṭār. vēntaṉ eṉṉum peyar vēyntōṉ. (koṉṟi vēyntōṉ eṉṉum peyar koṉṟi vēntaṉ eṉa maruviyiruttal kāṇka) eṉpataṉ marūu. vēytalāvatu muṭiyaṇital. muṭiyaṇiyum muttamiḻaracariyum muṭiyuṭi mūvēntar eṉṟu kūṟuvatu marapu. orē kālattil, ōr araca kuṭiyiṉar palar. māṟṟāntāy makkaḷum maṟṟat tāyattārumākac camavurimiyāḷarāka viruntu, oru perunāṭṭiṉ vevvēṟu pakutiyit taṉittaṉi āṇṭuvariṉ, avaraṉivarkkum muṭiyaṇi yum urimiyum kōveṉṉum peyarum uṇṭu. piṟ kālattil, oru perunāṭṭiṉiṉṟu taṉiyaracāyp pirintupōṉa ciṟṟaracarum ivvurimikaḷit tāmē aṭintu viṭṭaṉar.
mūvēntaruḷ oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉi veṉṟu aṭippaṭutti ṉavaṉāyiṉ, avaṉip pēraracaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉatu aracip pēraracu eṉṟum aḻikkalām. tamiḻvēntaruḷḷēyē oruvariruvari veṉṟu irumuṭip pēraracum mummuṭip pēraracum, avaruṭaṉ īḻavaracaṉ vēṅki (kīḻiccaḷukki) yaracaṉ pōṉṟāri veṉṟu paṉmuṭip pēraracum, niṟuviya cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyap pēraracar palarāvar. vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ, karikāl vaḷavaṉ, cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉpavar paṉmuṭip pēraracarkkuc ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭāvar.
mūvēntarum talimiyaracareṉṟum, piṟarellām ciṟṟaraca reṉṟum, marapum perumpāṉmiyum paṟṟip potuvākak kūṟappaṭiṉum; kālac cakkarattiṉ cuḻaṟciyāl, iṭiyiṭi, ciṟṟaracak kuṭiyiṉar pēraracarum pēraracak kuṭiyiṉar ciṟṟaracarum āyiṉar eṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum. vēntar kuṭiyic cērnta cōḻar, iṭik kālattil, tāyappōrāl pukārccōḻarum uṟiyūrc cōḻarumākap pirintu kōkkaḷ niliyaṭintu, piṉpu atuvumiṉṟip pallavaruk kaṭaṅkiya maṉṉarākat tāḻntaṉar. maṉṉar niliyilirunta pallavarō, itaṟku nērmāṟāka, pēraraca nilikkuyarntaṉar. innilimi piṉpu māṟi, cōḻar mīṇṭum vēntarum māvēntaru māyiṉar. iṅṅaṉam aracak kulaṅkaḷiṭi māṟimāṟi nērnta ēṟṟiṟakkam palappala.  (pa.ta.ā.)
aracar muṭivu
tamiḻaracar muṭivu taṉippaṭṭatum kuṭippaṭṭatum eṉa iruvaki.
aracar taṉimuṭivu : tamiḻaracar, pōril iṟattaliyē, perum pēṟākavum viṇṇulakam pukum vaḻiyākavuṅ koṇṭiruntaṉar. ataṉāl, iyalum pōtellām avarum cēṉiyuṭaṉ pōrukkuc celluvatu vaḻakkam. pōrilaṉṟi nōyālum mūppālum iṟakkum aracar vāḷpōḻntaṭakkap peṟuvar. ataṉ iyalpi,
nōyppāl viḷinta yākki taḻīik
kātaṉ maṟantavar tītumaruṅ kaṟumār
aṟampuri koḷki nāṉmaṟi mutalvar
tiṟampuri pacumpuṟ parappiṉar kiṭappi
maṟaṅkan tāka nallamar vīḻnta
nīḷkaḻaṉ maṟavar celvuḻic celkeṉa
vāḷpōḻn taṭakkal
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭup pakutiyāl (93:5-11) aṟika. caṅkakālattiṟkup piṟkālattil, vāḷpōḻn taṭakkal viṭappaṭṭatu.
pōriṟ peṟṟa puṟappuṇ nāṇiyum, putalvaṉ taṉṉoṭu poravaruki nāṇiyum, piṟavakiyil māṉakkēṭu nērnta viṭattum, ulakappaṟṟit tuṟantaviṭattum, aracar ūrukku vaṭakkil uṇṇānōṉpiruntu uyir tuṟappar. atu vaṭak kiruttal eṉappaṭum. pakivarāl ciṟippaṭṭiruntu uṇṇā tiṟattalum vaṭakkiruttaliṉ pāṟpaṭum.
vāḻnāḷiṟutiyil, aracāṭciyi iḷavaracaṉiṭam oppuvittu viṭṭuk kāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu tavañceytal, orōvōr aracaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa ceyalākum.
aracarkkup piṟappiṉum iṟappē ciṟappātalāl, koṟṟa aṭi moḻiyillāta varacarellām, avar iṟanta iṭappeyarāl vēṟupaṭuttik kūṟappaṭṭaṉar.
aracariṟantapiṉ, avaruṭampit tāḻiyāṟ kavittalum, tāḻiyāṟ kavittup putittalum, veṟumaṉē putittalum, muṟkāla vaḻakkam; erittal piṟkāla vaḻakkam. aracarukku iṟutic caṭaṅkiyaṟṟal paḷḷipaṭuttal (paḷḷi paṭi) eṉṟum, avar niṉivukkuṟiyāka eḻuppappaṭuṅ kaṭṭiṭam paḷḷippaṭik kōyil eṉṟum, peyarpeṟum.
aracakkuṭi muṭivu : muttamiḻaracarum tamiḻālum maṇavuṟavālum iṇikkappaṭṭu oṟṟumiyāyirunta vari, acōkap pēraracaṉālum avari acikkamuṭiyavilli. caṅkakālat tiṟkup piṟkālattil, tamiḻ pēṇāmi oṟṟumiyiṉmi kulappiriviṉi mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl, muttamiḻ nāṭum mūvēṟu pirintu valimi kuṉṟippōyc cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷ mutalāvatu 14 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tilli makamatiyap paṭikaṭkum piṉpu 16 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vicayanakara vaṭukap paṭikaṭkum, eḷitāy iriyāyiṉa. pāṇṭi nāṭṭu valimi ataṉ tāyap pōrkaḷālum tēyntu māyntatu. iṟutip pāṇṭiyaṉ ki.pi.1652 vari iruntāṉ.
cērar, kuṭiyil iṟutiyāka virunta aracar valimiyaṟṟavarā yiruntatiṉāl, cēranāṭāṉatu, vēṇāṭu (tiruvataṅkōṭu) kocci kōḻikkōṭu kōlattu nāṭu mutaliya pala ciṟu taṉiyaracu kaḷākap pirintu pōṉatuṭaṉ, 17 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭacol kalappāl maliyāḷa nāṭākavum tirintuviṭṭatu.
iṅṅaṉam, paṭippuk kālantoṭṭu mēmpaṭṭu iruntavi yeṉap pārāṭṭappeṟṟa mūvēntar kuṭikaḷum, 17ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṭiyil muṭivuṟṟaṉa.  (pa.ta.ā.)
aracar vaki
kuṟiñcit talivaṉ - maliyaṉ, veṟpaṉ, cilampaṉ, poruppaṉ kāṉaka nāṭaṉ (veṟpu, cilampu, poruppu eṉpaṉa maliyiṉ potup peyarkaḷ).
mullit talivaṉ - kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ, aṇṇal, tōṉṟal.
marutat talivaṉ - ūraṉ, makiḻnaṉ, kiḻavaṉ.
pālittalivaṉ - viṭali, mīḷi, kāḷi.
neytal talivaṉ - koṇkaṉ, cērppaṉ, tuṟivaṉ, mellam pulampaṉ.
nāṭṭut talivar - kuricil - pirapu, vēḷ - kuṟunilamaṉṉaṉ; kuṟumpaṉ - koḷḷit talivaṉ; maṉṉaṉ - ciṟṟaracaṉ; kōṉ allatu kō - aracaṉ; vēntaṉ - muṭivēynta pēraracaṉ, intiraṉ,  (col 39.)
aracar vāḻvil aṉṟāṭa nikaḻcci
aracar vāḻkkiyil aṉṟāṭa nikaḻccikaḷ potuvākap piṉvarumāṟu nikaḻntuvantaṉa.
vikaṟiyil, akavar (cūtar) aracaṉiṉ paḷḷi māṭattiṟku veḷiyē vāyilaruku niṉṟu, avaṉ muṉṉōriṉ pukaḻi iṉitākap pāṭi avaṉit tuyiluṇarttuvar.
aracaṉ tuyiluṇarntu, aṉṟu ceyya vēṇṭiyavaṟṟic cintittup pularum vēḷiyiṟ paḷḷiviṭṭeḻuvaṉ. avaṉ eḻuntamik kaṟikuṟiyākak kāli muracam eṉṉum paḷḷiyeḻucci muracam aṭikkappeṟum.
aracaṉ eḻuntapiṉ, paṭikkalap payiṟciyiruppiṉ atic ceytuviṭṭuk kālikkaṭaṉkaḷi muṭittu, nīrāṭi iṟivaḻipāṭu muṟṟik kāliyuṇavuṇṭu, ōlakka maṇṭapattiṟkuc celvaṉ.
aṅkuk koṭi muracu aṭikkappaṭum, palvēṟiṭattiṉiṉṟum vantirukkum pulavar pāṇar kūttar mutaliya paricilar, pāṭiyum āṭiyum tattam tiṟamikāṭṭi, ataṟkut taka aracaṉiṭam paricu peṟuvar.
koṭi nikaḻtti muṭintavuṭaṉ, muṟimuracu aṭikkappaṭum. vaḻakkāḷikaḷ tattam vaḻakkic colvataṟku muṉṉē vantiruppar. muracaṭittavuṭaṉ vaḻakkuk kēṭki toṭaṅkum.
aracaṉ ōlakkamaṇṭapattiliruntu koṭi nikaḻtti muṭintapiṉ, avvakkāla viṉinilimikkēṟpac cūḻviṉi maṇṭapattiṟ kēṉum aṟa maṉṟattiṟkēṉum celliṉum cellalām; aṉṟi ōlakkamaṇṭapattilēyē iruntu taṉṉāṭṭeḻuttup pōkkuvarattiyum ayalnāṭṭeḻuttup pōkkuvarattiyum kavaṉippiṉum kavaṉikkalām.
iv viṉikaḷellām muṟpakal nikaḻccikaḷākum.
naṇpakalil aracaṉ uṇavuṇṭapiṉ, iraṇṭoru nāḻiki allatu  veyil taṇiyumvari, iḷippāṟalām. ataṉpiṉ mīṇṭum ōlakkamaṇṭapam ceṉṟu ciṟitu nēram araciyaṟ kāriyaṅkaḷik kavaṉikkalām.
piṟpakal nikaḻccikaḷ potuvāka, aracarkkuk kuṭumpakkāriyak kavaṉippākavum iṉpap poḻutupōkkākavumē yirukkum.
irāvuṇavu uṇṭapiṉ, ciṟitunēram uriyāṭiyiruntu, ūraṭaṅkum vēḷiyil aracaṉ paḷḷikoḷvaṉ. paḷḷi māṭat tiṟkup paḷḷimaṇṭapam paḷḷiyampalam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
kārkālattilum, pōrkkālattilum, viḻānāḷilum, nāṭu kāvaṟ cuṟṟuppōkku nāṭkaḷilum, piṟa ciṟappu viṉi nikaḻcci yiṉ pōtum, mēṟkūṟiya aṉṟāṭa nikaḻcci viṉikaḷ māṟiyum nikaḻum.
potuvāka, aṟatti uyirnāṭiyākak koṇṭirunta ceṅ kōlaraca rellām. evviṭattum ennērattum, perumuṟi kēṭāṉa kāriyaṅkaḷik kavaṉittaṟku āyattamāyiruntatākavē terikiṉṟatu.
talimakaṉāl taliniṉṟu oḻukappaṭuvaṉa tarumam aruttam kāmam eṉa mūṉṟu; am mūṉṟiṉiyum oru pakali mūṉṟu kūṟiṭṭu mutaṟkaṭ pattunāḻiki yum aṟattoṭu paṭaccellum, iṭiyaṉa pattu nāḻikiyum aruttattoṭu paṭaccellum; kaṭiyaṉa pattu nāḻikiyum kāmattoṭu paṭaccellum; ātalāṉ talimakaṉ mutaṟkaṇ nāḻiki tarumattoṭu paṭuvāṉ; talimakaḷum vēṇṭavē tāṉum vēṇṭippōntu attāṇi pukuntu aṟaṅkēṭpatum aṟattoṭu paṭaccolvatuñ ceyyum, nāḻiki aḷantukoṇṭu iṭiyaṉa pattu nāḻikiyum iṟiyum muṟiyuṅkēṭṭu aruttattiṉoṭu paṭṭaṉavē ceytu vāḻvāṉām. avvaruttattu nīkkattuk kaṭiyaṉa pattu nāḻikiyil talimakaḷuḻip pōtarum, eṉpatu iṟi yaṉārakap poruḷuri (pak. 226).
aracarkkuk kālikkaṭaṉ kaḻittalum, ulakattiliruntu nāṭu kāvaṟṟoḻil celuttalum, viruntuṭaṉ aṭicil kitoṭalum, nāvalaroṭu kalvi payiṟalum, āṭal pāṭalkaḷil kaḷittalum, maṭavāroṭu kūṭalum, tuyiṟalum, tuyiluṇarntu tēvarpparā valumākiya kālavari eṭṭu, eṉpatu tañcivāṇaṉ kōviyuri (pak. 248)  (pa.ta.ā.)
aracar viḻākkaḷ
aracar viḻākkaḷ potuvum ciṟappum eṉa irupāṟpaṭum. avaṟṟuṭ potuvāvaṉa :
(1) maṇṇu maṅkalam : aracaṉ mutaṉ muṟiyāka muṭicūṭum pōtum, āṇṭutoṟum varum muṭicūṭiya nāḷilum, koṇṭāṭappeṟum viḻā maṇṇumaṅkalam ākum. atu nīrāṭi muṭicūṭum maṅkala viṉiyātalāl maṇṇumaṅkalam eṉappaṭṭatu. maṇṇutal - nīrāṭutal. tolkāppiyar itaṉic ciṟanta cīrtti maṇṇumaṅkalam eṉpar (1037).
uḻiñip pōril, matiṟkaṇ ōraracaṉ maṟṟōraracaṉik koṉṟu avaṉ muṭikkalam mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu, paṭṭavēntaṉ peyarāl muṭipuṉintu nīrāṭum maṅkalam, kuṭumi koṇṭa maṇṇumaṅkalam (tol. 1014) eṉappaṭum. atu akattōṉ maṇṇumaṅkalamum puṟattōṉ maṇṇumaṅkalamum eṉa iruvaki.
māṟṟaracaṉ matiliyaḻittuk kaḻutiyērāl uḻutu veḷḷi varakuṅ koḷḷuṅ kavaṭiyum vitti, amaṅkalamāyiṉa ceyta veṟṟivēntaṉ. avaṟṟiṟkuk kaḻuvāyāka nīrāṭum maṅkalam. maṉṉeyil aḻitta maṇṇu maṅkalam (tol. 1037) eṉappaṭum.
(2) perumaṅkalam : āṇṭutoṟuṅ koṇṭāṭap peṟum aracaṉ piṟantanāṭ koṇṭāṭṭam perumaṅkalam ākum. atu perunāḷ eṉavumpaṭum. atil, aracaṉ uyirkaḷiṭattuk kāṭṭum aruṭkaṟikuṟiyākat tūya veḷḷaṇi yaṇintu, ciṟip paṭṭavari viṭutali ceytu, koliyuñ ceruvum oḻintu, iṟitavirtalum tāṉañ ceytalum piṟavum mēṟkoḷvatu vaḻakkam.
(3) veṟṟi viḻā : aracaṉ pōril peṟṟa veṟṟiyit taṉṉakari lāyiṉum, māṟṟāṉ nakarilāyiṉum, īriṭattumāyiṉum, koṇṭāṭuvatu veṟṟiviḻā vākum. itiyoṭṭi, ampalam poṉvēytal tiruvītiyamittal mutaliya tiruppaṇikaḷum, tulāpāram iraṇiya karuppam mutaliya tāṉaṅkaḷum, ceyvatu vaḻakkam.
(4) makappēṟṟu viḻā : aracaṉukkup piḷḷi piṟantapōtu koṇṭāṭappaṭum viḻā makappēṟṟu viḻāvākum. peṇ makappēṟṟiṉum āṇ makappēṟum, āṇ makappēṟṟilum paṭṭattiṟkuriya mutaṉ makaṟpēṟum, ciṟappākak koṇṭāṭap peṟum.
(5) araṅkēṟṟu viḻā : muttamiḻum ōriru tamiḻum paṟṟi nūliyaṟṟiya āciriyar, araṅkēṟṟiyatik koṇṭāṭum viḻā araṅkēṟṟu viḻāvākum.
araṅkēṟiya pulavari veṇpaṭṭaṇivittu yāṉi mēlēṟṟi nakarvalam varuvittu. avarukkuc ciṟanta paricum ciṉṉamum muṟṟūṭṭum aḷippatu aracar vaḻakkam.
(6) naṭukal viḻā : pōrilpaṭṭa ciṟanta maṟavarkkuk kalnaṭum viḻā naṭukal viḻāvākum.
(7) pattiṉi viḻā : ciṟanta pattiṉip peṇṇukku, avaḷ iṟanta piṉ kal allatu cili nāṭṭum viḻā pattiṉi viḻāvākum.
iṉi, ciṟappāvaṉa : paḵṟuḷi neṭiyōṉ eṭutta munnīr viḻāvum, mucukuntaṉum neṭumuṭikkiḷḷivarippaṭṭa avaṉ vaḻiyiṉarum koṇṭāṭiya intira viḻāvum, mutalām irācarācaṉ piṟanta nāḷiṟ koṇṭāṭappaṭṭa catayat tiruviḻāvum, pōṉṟavi ciṟappu viḻāvākum.  (pa.ta.ā.)
aracaṉ1
ciṟukuḻantikaṭku eṅṅaṉam tāyum tantiyum teyvamō aṅṅaṉamē paḻaṅkāla makkaṭku aracaṉ teyvam. uṇavaḷittum pakiyaḻittum makkaḷik kātta kāvalaṉ teyvamāka allatu kaṇkaṇṭa teyvamākak karutappaṭṭum vaṇaṅkappaṭṭum vantāṉ. maṇavaṟiyil aracāṇik kompu naṭṭum allatu kaṭṭum vaḻakkam paṇṭikkālattil araca vaṇakkam paṟṟit tōṉṟiya tākat terikiṉṟatu  (col. 24.)

aravaṇittal eṉpatu taḻuvutaliyaṉṟit taḻuvikkāttaliyē kuṟittalāl, aravaṇi eṉṉum kūṭṭuc colliṉ mutaluṟuppu, pāmpik kuṟiyātu kākkum valimiyuḷḷa uyartiṇiyāṉa oruvaṉiyē kuṟittal vēṇṭum. akaram pala coṉ mutalil ukarattiṉ tiripāyiruttalāl, aravu eṉpatu uravu eṉpataṉ tiripeṉṟu koḷḷutal takkatām. uravu valimi. vali eṉpatu paṇpākupeyarāy valimiyuḷḷavaṉiyuṅ kuṟikkum.

aracaṉ - aricaṉ - ariyaṉ. aracu - aricu.

māvariyaṉ - mārāyaṉ - mārāyam.
mārāyam peṟṟa neṭumoḻi yāṉum  (tol. po. 63).
intiyarkkuḷ vaḻaṅkivarum rāy eṉṉum paṭṭap peyar, ariyaṉ eṉpatit taḻuviyatākum. rāv eṉpatu aravaṉ (aracaṉ) eṉpatit taḻuviyatu pōlum!
mārāyam eṉpatu tolkāppiyar kālattil māvariyam eṉṟē vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. piṟkālattil eḻutiṉavaṉ paḻamoḻippaṭi ēṭṭik keṭuttatākat terikiṉṟatu.
ariyaṉ - va. rāya. yakaram peṟṟa iccol vaṭiviṟkut tamiḻil tavira vēṟemmoḻiyilum viḷakkamiṉmi kāṇka.
rāja eṉṉum colli raj eṉṟu kuṟukki āḷ (to rule) eṉṟum, oḷir (to shine) eṉṟum, ceyaṟki muṟiyil vaṭavar poruḷ kaṟpippatu eḷḷaḷavum poruntātu.  (va.va.)
iṉi, attiru, attu(ka) māṉi, ilaṇi, kaṇavam, cuvali, tirumaram, paṇi eṉap pala peyarkaṇṭa aracamarattiṟku, appeyar toṉṟu toṭṭu ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅki varuvatum, accolliṉ tamiḻmikku oru cāṉṟākum.
ma. aracu, ka. arace, te. rāy.
nāṭṭu maraṅkaḷuḷ aracamaram mika ōṅki vaḷarvatāl, maravaracu eṉṉum poruḷil atu appeyar peṟṟatu.
attipōl tuḷirttu, ālpōṟ paṭarntu, aracupōlōṅki, aṟukupōl vērūṉṟi, mūṅkilpōṟ cuṟṟam muciyāmal vāḻntiruppīr. eṉṉum maraput toṭarmoḻiyiyum nōkkuka.  (va.va. 76-78.)
aracaṉ - va. rājaṉ, rājā - i. rāj, te. rāju.

aracaṉ - aracu, ariyaṉ - aricu.
aracaṉ - Gk. archon = ruler, king.
L. re#x, re#gis, OIr. re#g.

ariyaṉ - rāyaṉ - rāy - OF. roy, F. roi, ONF. rei, E. ray, roy.
mēliyāriya moḻikaḷil ic coṟkaṭku mūlamilli. camaṟkirutattil rājaṉ eṉṉum coṟku raj (oḷir, to shine) eṉpati mūlamākak kāṭṭi, raṅ (niṟam) eṉṉuñ colloṭu toṭarpupaṭuttuvar. āḷutaṟ poruḷil, arkho allatu arkh-ein eṉṉum kirēkkac colliyum, rāj eṉṉum camaṟkirutac colliyum, rego eṉṉum ilattīṉ colliyum, āḷutal piṟpaṭṭa vaḻakkeṉa aṟika.
araci = va. rājñī, L. regina. rājñī eṉṉum vaṭa collē iṉṟu rāṇi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
aṇittal = taḻuvutal, taḻuvik kāttal.
o.nō. taḻuvutal = aṇittal, aṇittuk kāttal.

taḻuvu - taḻiñci = pōriṟ paṭikkalaṅkaḷāl tākkuṇṭu cēta muṟṟa paṭiyāḷarip pōṟṟiyurittum poruḷ koṭuttum aracaṉ taḻuvik kōṭal.
aḻipaṭi taṭṭōr taḻiñciyoṭu tokii  (tol. po. 63).
aravaṇittal = aracaṉ taḻuvutal, aracaṉpōl taḻuvik kāttal.
aiyaṉ puriyum aravaṇippum  (paṇaviṭu. 27).
aravaṇittuk kākka vēṇṭum eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkiyum nōkkuka. itil, aravu eṉpatu aracaṉiyaṉṟip pāmpik kuṟikkātu. pāmpaṇittal eṉpatu puṇarcciyik kuṟikkumē yaṉṟip pātukāttalik kuṟikkātu.  (tamiḻar va.)
aracaṉ takutikaḷ
ōr aracaṉukkup piṉ ariyaṇi yēṟaṟ kuriyavaṉ, avvaracaṉu ṭiya  kōpperuntēviyiṉ mūttamakaṉāvaṉ. mūttamakaṉ iṟantuviṭiṉ iḷiyamakaṉum, āṇmakkaḷē illātapōtu makaḷvaḻip pēraṉmāruḷ mūppuṭiyavaṉum, pēraṉmārum illātapōtu kōpperuntēvikkaṭutta tēviyiṉ putalvaruḷ mūppuṭiyavaṉum, avarum illātapōtu piṟa neruṅkiya uṟaviṉaruḷ takutimikkavaṉum, ariyaṇiyēṟuvar.
aracaṉātaṟkuriyavaṉ, iḷamiyilēyē pala kalikaḷiyuṅ kaṟṟu, palvakip paṭippayiṟciyum peṟṟu, vēṭṭi yāṭuvatilum tērcciyuṟṟu, aṟivu āṟṟal aṉpu aṟam maṟam mutaliya kuṇaṅkaḷi oruṅkē uṭiyavaṉāy, pala aruñ ceyalum aṟacceyalum āṟṟik kuṭikaḷiṉ uḷḷattik kavarntavaṉāy, iruttal vēṇṭum.
makkaḷ yākki -ciṟappāka aracar yākki - niliyaṟṟa tātaliṉ, āḷum aracaṉ, taṉakkup piṉṉāl araciyaṟ kuḻappamum iṭarppāṭum nērātavāṟu, taṉ kālattilēyē mūpput takutiyuḷḷa taṉ makaṉukku allatu pēraṉukku, maṇṭi kavittu iḷavaracup paṭṭaṅkaṭṭi, āṭcit tuṟiyilum pōrviṉi yilum piṟavaṟṟilum payiṟci aḷippatu vaḻakkam.
aracaṉ yārukku iḷavaracup paṭṭam kaṭṭiṉum, āṭcittiṟaṉum araciyaṟpoṟuppuc cuṟṟattiṉ uṭaṉpāṭum kuṭikaḷiṉ iṇakkamum iruntāloḻiya, āṭciyik kaṭipōkak koṇṭu celutta muṭiyātu.
paḻaviṟal tāyam putuviṟal tāyam eṉṉum iruvakit tāyaṅkaḷuḷ, mūvēntar tāyam paḻaviṟal tāyamākavum, kuṟunila maṉṉar tāyam iruvakiyākavum iruntu vantaṉa.  (pa.ta.ā.)
aracaṉun tōṭṭamum - viḷiyāṭṭu
aracaṉum cēvakaṉum tōṭṭakkāraṉum tōṭṭamum (atāvatu pūcaṇikkoṭi kaḷum) eṉa āṭuvār mutalāvatu pirintu koḷvar.
aracaṉ caṟṟu eṭṭattiliruntu koṇṭu, pūcaṇit tōṭṭam pōṭumpaṭi cēvakaṉ vaḻit tōṭṭakkāraṉi ēvuvaṉ. tōṭṭakkāraṉ vēli toṭaṅkuvaṉ.
aracaṉ aṭikkaṭi taṉ cēvakaṉi ēvit tōṭṭattiṉ nilimi yip pārttuviṭṭu varac colvaṉ. cēvakaṉ vantu kēṭkum ovvoru taṭaviyum, muṟiyē, ovvoru payirttoḻil viṉi nikaḻntuḷḷatākat tōṭṭakkāraṉ colvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam uḻutal, vitittal, nīrpāyccal, muḷittal, ōrili mutaṟ palilivari viṭal, koṭiyōṭal, kaḷiyeṭuttal, pūppūttal, piñcuviṭal, kāy ātal, muṟṟutal ākiya pala viṉikaḷum collappaṭum. uḻutal mutaṟ kāy ātal vari ovvoru viṉiyic collumpōtum, tōṭṭatti nikarkkum piḷḷikaḷ avvav viṉiyik kiyāl naṭittuk kāṭṭuvar.
kāykaḷ muṟṟiyatic collumuṉ, tōṭṭakkāraṉ kāykaḷit taṭṭippārppatupōl ovvoru piḷḷiyiṉ taliyiyum kuṭṭi muṟṟiviṭṭatā? eṉṟu taṉṉittāṉē kēṭṭu, cila talikaḷi muṟṟiviṭṭatu eṉṟum, cila talikaḷi muṟṟa villi eṉṟum, colli; muṟṟiviṭṭateṉṟu coṉṉa piḷḷikaḷi vēṟāka vippāṉ. kāy koṇṭuvarumpaṭi aracaṉāl ēvappaṭṭa cēvakaṉ cila kāykaḷik koṇṭu pōyt taṉ vīṭṭil vittiruppatupōl, cila piḷḷikaḷik koṇṭupōyc caṟṟut tolivil vittiruppāṉ. akkāykaḷ kaḷavu pōvatupōl, appiḷḷikaḷ tām muṉpirunta iṭattiṟku vantuviṭuvar. iṅṅaṉam iraṇṭoru muṟi nikaḻnta piṉ, cēvakaṉ aracaṉiyē aḻittu vantu, ellāk kāykaḷiyuṅ koṇṭupōvatu pōl ellāp piḷḷikaḷi yuṅ koṇṭupōvaṉ. itōṭu āṭṭam muṭiyum, ivviḷi yāṭṭiṉ tōṟṟam veḷippaṭi.   (ta.nā.vi.)
araciya luṟuppukaḷ
oru nāṭu allatu cīmi (State)
(1)	āḷnilam (Territory)
(2)	kuṭikaḷ (Population)
(3)	araciyal (Government)
(4)	kōṉmi (Sovereignty)
eṉa nāluṟuppukkaḷi uṭiyatu. innāṉkum uḷḷa nāṭṭi niṟināṭu eṉṟum, ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟum palavuṅ kuṟintatik kuṟināṭu eṉṟum aḻikkalām.
(1) āḷnilam : cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar eṉṉum muttamiḻaracarum, taṉittaṉi āṇṭa cēranāṭu cōḻanāṭu pāṇṭiyanāṭu eṉṉum munnāṭum cērnta tamiḻakam mutalāvatu (taliccaṅkak kālattil) vaṭapeṇṇiyāṟṟiṟkum, (kiruṣṇā kōtāvari māvaṭṭaṅ kaḷik koṇṭa kīḻic caḷukkiya nāṭu vēṅkināṭu eṉṟum, ataṟkumēṟkē pampāy mākāṇat teṉ pakutiyilirunta mēlic caḷukkiyanāṭu vēḷpulam eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟirunta tiṉāl, taliccaṅka kālat tamiḻaka vaṭavelli kiruṭṭiṇi yāṟu eṉṟu koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu.) teṟkil amiḻntupōṉa paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum iṭippaṭṭatāyiruntatu; piṉpu (iṭic caṅkak kālattilum kaṭiccaṅkak kālattilum), vēṅkaṭa (tiruppati) malikkum kaṭal koṇṭa kumari (kaṉṉi) yāṟṟiṟkum iṭippaṭṭatāyiruntatu. ataṉpiṉ, kumariyāṟuṅ kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭuk kumarimuṉi (Cape Comorin) teṟkelli yāyiṟṟu.
tamiḻakattil cēranāṭu mēṟkiliruntamiyāl kuṭapulam eṉṟum, cōḻanāṭu kiḻakkiliruntamiyāl kuṇapulam eṉṟum, pāṇṭiya nāṭu teṟkilirun tamiyāl teṉpulam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. im  munnāṭum tamiḻ nāṭēyāyiṉum, pāṇṭiya nāṭē ciṟanta tamiḻ vaḻakkup paṟṟiyum tamiḻccaṅka virukki paṟṟiyum. tamiḻnāṭeṉac ciṟappittuk kūṟappeṟṟatu. cōḻanāṭu pāṇṭiya nāṭu eṉṉum peyarkaḷ. muṟiyē cōṇāṭu pāṇṭināṭu eṉa maruvum. cēranāṭu neṭumalit toṭaruṭimiyālum, kuṟiñcinila mikutiyālum malināṭeṉappaṭṭatu.
taliccaṅkak kālattil ippōtuḷḷa tiruvāṅkūr kocci kuṭaku micūr eṉṉum cīmikaḷum, malapār teṉkaṉṉaram kōyamputtūr cēlam eṉṉum māvaṭṭaṅkaḷum, cēravēntaṉ āḷnilamāyiruntaṉa tiruccirāppaḷḷi, tañcāvūr, teṉṉārk kāṭu, vaṭārkkāṭu, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu, cittūr, nellūr, kaṭappi, aṉantapuram eṉṉum māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ cōḻavēntaṉ āḷnilamā yiruntaṉa. putukkōṭṭi, maturi, irāmanātapuram, tirunelvēli eṉṉum māvaṭṭaṅkaḷum, tiruvāṅkūrc cīmiyiṉ teṉ pakutiyilirunta vēṇāṭum, teṟkē intumāvāriyil (Indian Ocean) amiḻntu pōṉa perunilap pakutiyum, pāṇṭiya vēntaṉ āḷnilamāyiruntaṉa. ippōtuḷḷa cīmiyellikaḷum māvaṭṭap piriviṉiyum perumpālum āṅkila āṭciyil ēṟpaṭṭaviyātaliṉ, mēṟkūṟiya muttamiḻnāṭṭellikaḷ matippākak kūṟappaṭṭavi eṉa aṟika.
oruvāṟu curuṅkaccoṉṉāl, potiyamaliyiliruntu vaṭa peṇṇiyāṟu nōkki vaṭakiḻakkāka irupatu pāki (Degree) cāyttu iḻukkappaṭum kōṭṭāl pirikkappaṭum irunilap pakutikaḷuḷ, mēlippakuti muṟṟum cēra nāṭākum; kīḻip pakutiyil, putukkōṭṭikku vaṭakkiluḷḷatu cōḻanāṭum, ataṟkut teṟkiluḷḷatu pāṇṭi nāṭum ākum.
teṟkil amiḻntupōṉa kumariyāṟṟukkum paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟukkum iṭippaṭṭa tolivu eḻunūṟu kātam eṉṟu, cilappatikāra virivuriyāciriyarākiya aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟiyiruppatāl pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ paḻamparappi oruvāṟu uṇaralām. kātam eṉpatu pattukkal koṇṭateṉṟum, mūṉṟu kal koṇṭa teṉṟum, iruvēṟākak kūṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ kuṟinta aḷavik koḷḷiṉ, teṟkē amiḻntupōṉa pāṇṭināṭṭu nilam 1100 kal toliviṟkuk kuṟiyātatākum. anniliyil, paḵṟuḷiyāṟu cāvakattīviṟku nēr mēṟ kāyamiyum.
akkālattu avarnāṭṭut teṉpālimukattiṟku vaṭavelli yākiya paḵṟuḷiyeṉṉum āṟṟiṟkum kumariyeṉṉum āṟṟiṟku miṭiyē. eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatavāṟum; ivaṟṟiṉ nīrmalivāṉeṉa malinta ēḻteṅka nāṭum, ēḻmaturi nāṭum, ēḻmuṉpāli nāṭum, ēḻpiṉpāli nāṭum, ēḻkuṉṟanāṭum, ēḻkuṇakāri nāṭum, ēḻ kuṟumpaṉināṭum, eṉṉum inta nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭum; kumari kollam mutaliya paṉmalināṭum kāṭum, natiyum, patiyum; taṭanīrkkumari vaṭaperuṅkōṭṭiṉ kāṟum kaṭal koṇṭoḻitalāl, eṉṉum aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟṟāl (cilappatikāram (cāminātiyar patippu), pakkam 230) amiḻntu pōṉa pāṇṭināṭṭuppakuti aimpattoṉṟiṟkuk kuṟiyāta nāṭukaḷik koṇṭiruntateṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷ teṉkōṭi yilirunta teṉpālimukam paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkut teṟkilirunta teṉpatum, aṟiyappaṭum.
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi  (cilampu. 11: 19-22)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟiyiruppatāl, paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kaṅkipōlum pēriyāṟu eṉṟum, kumarikkōṭu paṉimalik kaṭutta perumali eṉṟum, ūkikkalām. iṭiccaṅkak kālattil, kumariyāṟṟiṟkut teṟkuppaṭṭa pāṇṭināṭṭup pakuti mūḻkiyataṉṟi, vēṟu oru māṟutalum nērntatilli.
paḵṟuḷi mūḻkiyapiṉ tamiḻakap pēriyāṟāyiruntatu kumariyāṟē.
teṉāa turukeḻu kumari  (puṟam. 6)
eṉṟu kārikiḻārum,
kumariyam peruntuṟi  (puṟam. 67)
eṉṟu picirāntiyārum pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka. kaṭiccaṅkak kālattiṟkup piṉ kumariyāṟum mūḻkiṟṟu. paḵṟuḷiyuṅ kumariyum mūḻkiya piṉṉarē,
taṇṇīruṅ kāviriyē tārvēntaṉ cōḻaṉē
maṇṇāva tuñcōḻa maṇṭalamē
eṉṟu kampar pāṭumāṟu kāviri tamiḻakattiṟ ciṟantu, cōḻanāṭu puṉal nāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu.
kaṭiccaṅkak kālattilēyē, cēranāṭum cōḻanāṭum ivviru pākaṅkaḷākap pirintiruntaṉa. cēranāṭṭil, kuṭa (mēṟkut toṭarcci) malikku mēṟkiluḷḷatu kaṭaṉmalināṭu eṉṟum, kiḻakkiluḷḷatu koṅkunāṭu eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. kaṭaṉ malināṭṭiṉ vaṭapākam, kuṭṭam kuṭam tuḷuvam koṅ kaṇam eṉṉum pala pakutikaḷik koṇṭiruntatu. koṅkunāṭu, kuṭakoṅku (mēlmaṇṭalam), kuṇakoṅku (kīḻmaṇṭalam) eṉa irupākamākap pirintu, avaṟṟuḷ kuṭakoṅku, mīṇṭum kuṭaku, karunāṭu, kaṅkanāṭu, kaṭṭiyanāṭu mutaliya palanāṭukaḷākap pirintu pōyiṟṟu. avaṟṟuḷ kuṭakiyāṇṭa kōcar koṅ kiḷaṅkōcar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kaṅkanāṭṭiyāṇṭa kaṅkar tammik koṅkunāṭṭaracar (koṅkutēcarājākkaḷ) eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭaṉar. akkālattuk kaṭṭiyanāṭu tamiḻaka vaṭa velliyā yiruntateṉpatu.
kullik kaṇṇi vaṭukar muṉiyatu
palvēṟ kaṭṭi naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar
moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum  (11)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟuntokic ceyyuḷāl aṟiyalākum. kuṇakoṅ kiṉ teṉpākam pūḻināṭu eṉap pirintiruntatu. atu potiṉi eṉṉum paḻaṉimaliyiyum uṭkoṇṭiruntatu. paḻaṉimali cēranāṭṭic cērntatu eṉpatu,
cērar koṅkuvi kāvūr naṉṉāṭu
eṉṉum aruṇakirinātar kūṟṟālum, vaṭakkuttici paḻaṉi eṉṉum kampar kūṟṟālum aṟiyalākum. kuṭakoṅku pala nāṭukaḷāyp pirintu pōṉapiṉ, koṅku nāṭu eṉac ciṟappāka aḻikkappeṟṟatu,  karuvūrit talinakarākak koṇṭirunta kuṇakoṅkē, ataṉiṉṟum pūḻināṭu pirinta piṉ, koṅku nāṭeṉa eñciyiruntatu kōvi cēla māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ kūṭiya pakutiyēyākum. iṅṅaṉam kaṭaṉmali nāṭum koṅkunāṭum palapala pakutikaḷākap pirintupōyiṉum, aviyāvum ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pōṉṟa cēraṉ āṭciyil avaṉukkaṭaṅkiyē iruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ kuṭanāṭum kuṭṭa nāṭum koṅkunāṭum pūḻināṭum nīṇṭa kālamākac cēranāṭṭup pakutikaḷākavē yiruntu, cērar kuṭumpattiṉarāl āḷappaṭṭu vantaṉa.
cōḻanāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākam toṇṭināṭu (vaṭa cōḻam) eṉṟum, teṉpākam puṉalnāṭu (teṉcōḻam) eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa.
kaṭiccaṅka kālattiṟutiyilēyē, cēranāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākat tiluḷḷa tuḷuvam koṅkaṇam kuṭaku karunāṭakam mutaliya nāṭukaḷ, moḻipeyar tēyamākat tiriyat toṭaṅki viṭṭaṉa.
muttamiḻ nāṭukaḷuḷ aṭaṅkiya uḷnāṭukaḷi vevvēṟu ciṟṟaracarum tuṇiyariyarum (Viceroys) āṇṭu vantaṉar. talimiyaracar valikuṉṟiyapōtu, atikāra vācimikka ciṟṟaraca rum tuṇiyariyarum taṉiyaracarāki viṭuvatu vaḻakkam. ivvaki yil, cēranāṭṭiṉiṉṟu koṅkunāṭum, cōḻanāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu toṇṭināṭum, pirintupōyiṉa. piṟkālattup pēraracar tam peruvaliyāl avaṟṟi mīḷa aṭippaṭuttiṉum, avi vellap paṭṭa puṟanāṭukaḷ pōl karutappaṭṭaṉavē yoḻiya, paṇṭu pōl cēracōḻa nāṭṭuppakutiyāṉa uḷnāṭākak karutappeṟa villi. āyiṉum, tamiḻakatti vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil eṉṟu kūṟum ilakkiya marapu neṭukalum iruntuvantatu.
pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ perumpakutiyic ciṟitum peritumāyp paṉ muṟiyiṟ kaṭalkoṇṭu viṭṭataṉālum, cēra cōḻa nāṭukaḷiṉ ellip puṟattuc ciṟṟaracar aṭikkaṭi taṉiyaracarākik koṇṭum tam nāṭṭi virivupaṭuttik koṇṭum iruntatiṉālum, tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭakōṭiyum vaṭamēṟkup pakutiyum moḻipeyar tēyamākat tirintu vantatiṉālum, kaṭiccaṅkak kālattiṟkup piṉ muttamiḻ nāṭukaḷum mika oṭuṅki viṭṭaṉa.
cēranāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākattic cērnta karunāṭum kaṅkamum kaṭṭiyamum, 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē muḻukkaṉṉaṭa nāṭāki, 11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil hoycaḷa āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa. 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyilirunta viṣṇu varttaṉaṉ eṉṉum hoycaḷa maṉṉaṉ. kēraḷa (cērala) aracaṉi veṉṟu nīlamaliyik (Nilgris) kippaṟṟik koṇṭatāka avaṉ kalveṭṭuk kūṟuvatāl, cēranāṭṭiṉ vaṭavelli 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil mikatteṟkē taḷḷi viṭṭateṉa aṟiyalām.
koṅkunāṭu, mūvēntarum muṭṭip poruṅkaḷamāyiruntu, oru niliyilillāmal, aṭikkaṭi am mūvaruḷḷum oruvar kiyiṉiṉṟu oruvar kikkuk kaṭantu koṇṭum ellimāṟik koṇṭum iruntatiṉāl. oru taṉi nāṭṭiṟkuriya taṉmiyi muṟṟum iḻantuviṭṭatu. ataṉāl, 3 ām kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉ (1178-1218) avikkaḷap pulavarāyirunta kampar, cēra cōḻa pāṇṭi toṇṭi nāṭukaḷiṉ ellikaḷi maṭṭum piṉvarumāṟu pāṭiyuḷḷār.
veḷḷā ṟatuvaṭakkā mēṟkup peruvaḻiyām
teḷḷār puṉaṟkaṉṉi teṟkākum - uḷḷāra
āṇṭa kaṭalkiḻakkā mimpat taṟukātam
pāṇṭināṭ ṭellip pati
kaṭalkiḻakkut teṟkuk kariporuveḷ ḷāṟu
kuṭaticiyiṟ kōṭṭik kariyām - vaṭaticiyil
ēṉāṭṭup paṇṇi yirupattu nāṟkātam
cōṇāṭṭuk kelliyeṉac col
mēṟkup pavaḷamali vēṅkaṭa nērvaṭakkām 
ārkku muvari yaṇikiḻakku - pārkkuḷuyar
teṟkup piṉāki tikaḻirupa tiṉkātam
naṟṟoṇṭi nāṭeṉavē nāṭṭu
vaṭakkut ticipaḻaṉi vāṉkīḻteṉ kāci
kuṭakkut ticikōḻik kōṭām - kaṭaṟkariyiṉ
ōramō teṟkāku muḷḷeṇ patiṉkātam
cēranāṭ ṭelliyeṉac ceppu.
ivvellikaḷum nīṭittu niṟkavilli, muttamiḻaracarum piṟakum oruvariyoruvar porutu veṉṟu koṇṭum tattam nāṭṭi virivākkikkoṇṭu miruntatiṉāl, avar nāṭukaḷum ataṟkēṟpac curuṅkiyum virintum vantu koṇṭiruntaṉa.
caṅkakālattiṟkup piṟkālattil, kaṅka kaṭṭiya nāṭukaḷiṉ vaṭapākattil, taṭikipāṭi nuḷampapāṭi eṉac cila ciṟunāṭukaḷ putitākat tōṉṟiṉa.
(2) kuṭikaḷ : cēracōḻa pāṇṭiyam eṉa muppāṟpaṭṭa tamiḻakat tup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ yāvarum, tiraviṭap pēriṉattic cērnta tamiḻar eṉṉum iṉattārāvar. avar nakara vāḻnarum nāṭṭu vāḻnarum kāṭṭu vāḻnarum mali vāḻnarumāy, nālvakip paṭṭiruntaṉar. avaruḷ orucār mali vāḻnarum, orucār kāṭṭu vāḻnarum, nākarikamiṉṟik kāṭṭu vilaṅkāṇṭikaḷāy (kāṭṭu vilaṅkāṇṭi - kāṭṭu mirukāṇṭi kāṭṭumirāṇṭi) vāḻntu vantaṉar.
cittar muṉivar mutaliya ciṟanta tuṟavu vakuppār nāṭṭiṭip piṟantu vaḷarntavarēyāyiṉum, tam tuṟavu nilipaṟṟi maliyil uṟintu vantaṉar.
muḻu anākarikarum maliyuṟi tuṟaviyarum aracaṉāṭcik kuṭpaṭṭilar.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar eṉappaṭṭa pārppār (pirāmaṇar) tol kāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻnāṭu pukuntu nakarilum nāṭṭilum kōyil viṉiñarākavum araciyal viṉiñarākavum pulavarākavum perumakkaḷ tūtarākavum amarn tiruntaṉar.
marutam eṉṉum akanāṭṭilum neytal eṉṉuṅkari nāṭṭilumuḷḷa kōnakaraṅkaḷil, varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉpiruntē, yavaṉar (kirēkkar, rōmar) cōṉakar (arapiyar, turukkar, milēccar) mutaliya mēṉāṭṭārum, avantiyar, makatar, kuccarar marāṭṭar mutaliya vaṭanāṭṭārum, īḻavar, nākar mutaliya kīḻ nāṭṭārum, kammiyam vaṇikam aracavūḻiyam mutaliya paṟpala viṉi mēṟkoṇṭu, avaravarkkuriya cēriyil pativāyiruntu vantaṉar .
(3) araciyal : araciyal eṉpatu,
(1)	caṭṭam amippōr (Legislature)
(2)	karumam āṟṟuvōr (Executive)
(3)	naṭuttīrppōr (judiciary)
eṉṉum muttuṟi atikāra vakuppukkaḷi uṟuppākak koṇṭatu.
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil, im muttuṟi atikāraṅkaḷum pirikkappaṭavilli. ataṉāl, caṭṭam amittaṟkeṉṟu ikkālattiṟ pōl oru taṉi avi iruntatilli, aracaṉum avaṉuṭiya tuṇiyatikārikaḷumē, im mūvaki viṉikaḷiyum purintu vantaṉar. ciṟṟaracaṉāyiṉum pēraracaṉāyiṉum caṭṭam amikkum atikāram aracaṉiṭattilēyē iruntatu. aracaṉukkum kuṭikaḷukkum tīṅku viḷikkāta paḻimiyāṉa marapu vaḻakkaṅ kaḷellām appaṭiyē taḻuvap peṟṟaṉa. avvappōtu tēvip paṭṭa tiruttaṅkaḷum putu vitikaḷumē aracaṉāl ākkappaṭṭaṉa.
(4) kōṉmi : kōṉmiyāvatu, oru nāṭu piṟa nāṭṭic cārātu, taṉakkuriya caṭṭaṅkaḷit tāṉē amittuk koḷḷum urimi. ivvurimi mūvēntarukkum taṉittaṉi yiruntatāl, ammūvar nāṭum muḻuniṟi vuṟṟaviyākum.
kōṉmi eṉpatu oru nāṭṭiṟkuriya taṉiyuṟuppākap paṇṭikkālattil uṇarappaṭāmiyāl, tiruvaḷḷuvar ēṉi mūṉṟuṟuppukkaḷiyum āṟāka vakuttu.
paṭikuṭi kūḻamiccu naṭparaṇ āṟum
uṭiyāṉ aracaruḷ ēṟu
eṉṟu kūṟiṉār (381). avar kūṟum āṟaṉuḷ; kuṭi eṉpatu kuṭikaḷ; kūḻ (poruḷ); araṇ eṉṉum iraṇṭum āḷnilat tiṉ pakutikaḷ; aracaṉ paṭi amiccu naṭpu eṉṉum nāṉkum araciyaliṉ pakutikaḷ.
cila tamiḻ nūlāciriyar.
mummaliyu munnāṭu munnatiyu muppatiyu
mummuracu muttamiḻu mukkoṭiyu - mummāvun
tāmuṭiya maṉṉar taṭamuṭimēl tāraṉṟō
pāmuṟitēr vaḷḷuvarmup pāl
eṉṉum tiruvaḷḷuvamālic ceyyuḷi yoṭṭi, peyar, mali, nāṭu, āṟu, nakar, muracu, tamiḻ, koṭi, kutiri, māli eṉṉum pattum aracavuṟuppukkaḷ eṉak kūṟuvar.
pāṭṭiyal ilakkaṇiyar, ivaṟṟuḷ peyar tamiḻ eṉṉum iraṇṭi nīkki, avaṟṟukkup patilāka, yāṉi āṇi eṉṉum iraṇṭic cērppar.
ivviru cārārum kūṟupavaṟṟuḷ perumpālaṉa aracacciṉṉaṅ kaḷum āḷnilap pakutikaḷumātaliṉ, avi iṟṟi araciyal nūṟpaṭi araca vuṟuppuk kaḷākā eṉa aṟika.  (pa.ta.ā.)
araciyal viṉiñar
muttamiḻ nāṭṭilum, araciyal viṉiñar, perunāṭṭut talinakar  ciṟunāṭṭut talinakarkaḷ ūrkaḷ ākiya mūvaki yiṭaṅkaḷil citaṟiyiruntaṉar.
(1) perunāṭṭut talinakarilirunta viṉiñar : aracaṉuṭiya āṭcikku aṭippaṭit tuṇiyāyirunta atikāraccuṟṟam aimperuṅkuḻu eṉpatu, amiccar, purōkitar, paṭit talivar, tūtar, oṟṟar eṉṉum aintu kuḻuvāriṉ peruntokutiyē aimperuṅkuḻuvākum.
talimiyamiccaṉukku uttara mantiri allatu makāmantiri eṉṟu peyar.
tūtar, (tūtuvar) aracaṉ viṭutta ceytikaḷip piṟa araca riṭattuk koṇṭu celpavarum, aracaṉuṭiya āṇikaḷi nāṭṭati kārikaḷiṭattum ūratikārikaḷiṭattuṅ koṇṭu celpavarum eṉa iru pālar. araca tūtar caṭṭiyun talip pākiyu maṇintiruppar. ataṉāl avarkkuk kañcukamākkaḷ eṉṟum, caṭṭiyiṭṭa piratāṉikaḷ eṉṟum peyar. talimit tūtaṉ kañcukamutalvaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ.
cañcayaṉ mutalāt talikkīṭu peṟṟa 
kañcuka mutalvar īriñ ñūṟṟuvar
eṉṟu cilappatikāraṅ kūṟuvatāl (26: 137-8), tūtar perun toki yiṉarāyiruntaṉar eṉpataṟiyappaṭum.
aimperuṅ kuḻuviṟku aṭuttapaṭiyāy, ataṉiṉuñ caṟṟu virivāka irunta atikāraccuṟṟam eṇpērāyam eṉpatu, karaṇattiyalavar (kaṇakkar), karuma vitikaḷ (aracāṇiyi niṟivēṟṟum atikārikaḷ,) kaṉakac cuṟṟam (paṇṭāram eṉṉum pokkicacāli yatikārikaḷ), kaṭi kāppāḷar (araṇmaṉi vāyiṟ kāvalar), nakara māntar (nakarapperumakkaḷ allatu vaṇikap perumakkaḷ), paṭittalivar, yāṉimaṟavar, ivuḷi maṟavar (kutiric cēvakar) eṉṉum eṭṭuk kūṭṭattāriṉ perun tokuti eṇ pērāyamākum. 
aracaṉuṭiya naṉmiyiyum uṭal nalattiyum uṇmiyākak kavaṉittup pēṇutaṟku, uṟutic cuṟṟam eṉṟoru kuḻu iruntatu kuṟikkappaṭṭa paṭit talivar, nimittikar, maruttuvar, naṭpāḷar, antaṇar ākiya aintiṟattār cērnta kuḻu, uṟutic cuṟṟamākum.
aracaṉillāta allatu avaṉ kaṭunōyppaṭṭa camiyattil araciyalik kavaṉittaṟku, araciyaṟpoṟuppuc cuṟṟam oṉṟu iruntatu. atu ācāṉ (purōkitaṉ) peruṅkaṇi (kaṇiyar talivaṉ), aṟakkaḷattantaṇar (niyāya capiyār), kāviti (variyatikārikaḷ), mantirak kaṇakkar (aracavāṇi yeḻutuvōr) ākiya aintiṟattārik koṇṭatu. kōvalaṉik kolvitta āriyap paṭi kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉiṉ iṟappiṟkum, avaṉ makaṉ veṟṟivēṟceḻiyaṉiṉ muṭicūṭṭiṟkum, iṭippaṭṭa kālattil, pāṇṭip perunāṭṭaraciyalik kavaṉittatu araciyaṟ poṟuppuc cuṟṟamē. 
tali nakariluḷḷa attāṇi maṇṭapattil, aracaṉ muṉṉiliyil, cūḻviṉi yamiccarum, paṭittalivarum, palvēṟu āḷviṉit tiṇikkaḷat talivarum, peruṅkaṇiyum, ācāṉum, palvakip pulavarum piṟarum nāḷtōṟum kuḻumiyirukkum niliyāṉa kūṭṭattiṟku aracavi eṉṟu peyar. atu irukkumiṭam avikkaḷam eṉappaṭum. aracaviyil aracaṉukkaṭuttapaṭiyāka mutalamiccaṉē talimiyāyiruppāṉ.
aracavi, atiluḷḷāriṉ taṉmiyum aṟivum paṟṟi, nallavi tīyavi niṟiyavi kuṟiyavi eṉa nālvakiyāka vakuttuk kūṟappaṭum. aṟivu oḻukkam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ ciṟantu, naṭunilimi yākap pēcuvōr kūṭiya avi nallavi; ataṟku etirāṉatu tīyavi; ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum aṟintu, etirkālac ceytikaḷi muṉṉamāka aṟivikkum pēraṟiñarkūṭiya avi niṟiyavi; ataṟku etirāṉatu kuṟiyavi.
aracaṉ avvappōtu tiruvāy malarntaruḷum āṇikaḷi nēriṟ kēṭpavaṉukkut tiruvāyk kēḷvi eṉṟum, at tiruvāyk kēḷvi aṟivikka aṟintu aracavāṇiyi eḻutuvōrkkut tirumantira vōli eṉṟum. avveḻuttāḷar talivaṉukkut tirumantira vōli nāyakam eṉṟum peyar. aracavāṇi kaḷik kēṭṭeḻutum atikārac cuṟṟattiṟku vāyiṟ kēṭpōr (Secretarial Staff) eṉṟum, aracavāṇikku vāyk kēḷvi eṉṟum, iti niṟivēṟṟuvōrkku vāyk kēḷviyar eṉṟum, peyaruṇṭu. nāḷtōṟum nikaḻum araciyal nikaḻccikaḷi nikaḻccip puttakattil eḻuti vippavaṉ paṭṭōlip perumāṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ūrccapikaḷiṉiṉ ṟum nāṭṭatikārikaḷiṭattiruntum varum ōlikaḷip paṭit taṟintu, avaṟṟiṟkut takka viṭikaḷi yeḻutit tūtar vāyi lāy viṭuppōrkku viṭiyil eṉṟum, avar talivaṉukku viṭiyatikāri eṉṟum peyar.
ūrkaḷilum talinakarilum taṇṭappaṭṭu vanta varit toki kaḷik kaṇakkiṟ patintu koḷvōr, variyilār eṉappaṭuvar. patintukoḷḷappaṭṭa varittokiyi, iṉṉiṉṉa cela viṟku ivvaḷavivvaḷaveṉṟu kūṟu ceyvōrkku varikkūṟu ceyvār eṉṟu peyar. ovvōr ūriṉiṉṟum aracaṉukku varavēṇṭiya kāṇikkaṭaṉ eṉṉum nilavarikkuk kaṇakku vip pōr varippottakam eṉṟum, avar talivaṉ varip pottakak kaṇakku eṉṟum, peyar peṟuvar. nilavari nīṅkiya iṟiyili nilaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum ūrkaḷiṉiṉṟum varavēṇṭiya piṟavarikaṭkuk kaṇakku vippōr, puravuvari eṉappaṭuvar. avar tokuti puravuvarit tiṇikkaḷam eṉappaṭum. at tiṇik kaḷat talimik kaṇakkaṉukkup puravuvariccīkaraṇam eṉṟum, ataṉ talivaṉukkup puravuvarit tiṇikkaḷa nāyakam eṉṟum, peyar. ivarallātu, variyilīṭu eṉṟum, puravuvarit tiṇikkaḷa mukaveṭṭi eṉṟum, iruvari yatikārikaḷ iruntaṉar.
cēranāṭṭu araciṟiyatikāri pāṭṭamāḷaṉ eṉṟum, araciyaṟ kaṇakkatikāri mēleḻuttuk kaṇakku eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
attāṇimaṇṭapattilum araṇmaṉiyilum, avvappōtu nāḻikiyaṟintu aracaṉuk kaṟivippavar, nāḻikik kaṇakkar eṉappaṭuvar.
maṇanāḷ tirunāḷ porunāḷ ākiya mūvakip perunāḷilum, yāṉimēlēṟi muracaṟintu aracavāṇiyi nakarattārk kaṟivippavaṉ vaḷḷuvaṉ eṉṟum, aracarkkuriya vēttiyaṟ kūtti yāṭupavaṉ cākkiyaṉ eṉṟum, kūṟappaṭuvar. tivākaram ivviruvariyum uḷpaṭukarumattalivar eṉṟu iṇittuk kūṟum. cākkiyaṉukkuk kūttuḷpaṭuvōṉ eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
araṇmaṉik kāriyaṅkaḷi mēṟpārppavaṉukku māḷiki nāyakam (Seneschal or Chamberlin) eṉṟum, araṇmaṉik kaṇakkeḻutupavaṉukkut tirumukakkaṇakku eṉṟum peyar. araṇmaṉi vēliyāṭkaḷ akapparivāram eṉṟum, aracaṉoṭu neruṅkip paṇiceyvār aṇukkac cēvakam eṉṟum, aracaṉukkup pakkattil niṉṟu ēval kēṭpōr uḻiccuṟṟāḷar eṉṟum, aḻikkappeṟuvar. aracaṉukkuk kavari ālavaṭṭam mutaliyaṉa vīcuvārum, kuṭikoṭi mutaliyaṉa piṭippārum, aṭiyāḷakkārar eṉappaṭuvar.
aracaṉukku ūkkamum maṟamuṅ kiḷarumāṟu, avaṉuṭiya muṉṉōriṉ aruntiṟaṟ ceyalkaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟi, avaṉ kuṭiyip pukaḻvataṟku, ōvar allatu ēttāḷar eṉṟu collap paṭum pāṭakar palariruntaṉar. avar cūtar mākatar vētāḷikar (vitāḷikar) eṉap palvakiyar. cūtar niṉṟēttuvār eṉṟum, mākatar iruntēttuvār eṉṟum collappaṭuvar. vikaṟi yil aracaṉ paḷḷiyaṟikkup puṟampē niṉṟu, avaṉ kuṭiyip pukaḻntu pāṭi, avaṉi iṉitākat tuyiluṇarttupavar cūtar āvar. iṅṅaṉam pācaṟiyil aracaṉit tuyiluṇarttupavari, akavar eṉṟu maturik kāñci kūṟum.
aracaṉuṭiya āṇikaḷi avvappōtu kāḷamūtip paṭi kaṭkut terivippavaṉ paṭiyuḷpaṭuvōṉ allatu ciṉṉamūti eṉappaṭuvāṉ. avaṉukkup paṭikkiḻavaṉ ciṟukkaṉ, paṭic ciṟukkaṉ, paṭicciṟupiḷḷi eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
aracaṉ araṇmaṉikku veḷiyē nakarilum nāṭṭilum cellum pōtellām, avaṉuṭiya parivārattiṟku muṉ ceṉṟu, avaṉ varukiyik kūṟi makkaḷi vaḻiyiṉiṉṟum vilakkupavaṉukkuk kaṭṭiyaṅkāraṉ (Herald) eṉṟu peyar.
aracaṉ talinakarilirukkumpōtum nāṭukāvaṉ mēṟcellu miṭattum, avaṉoṭu kūṭavēyirukkum araciyal viṉik kūṭṭattārkku, uṭaṉ kūṭṭam eṉṟum uḻiyar eṉṟum peyar. avaruḷ atikārikaḷāyiruppār uṭaṉ kūṭṭattati kārikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. viṉiyāṟṟum vēḷiyil aracaṉic cūḻntirukkum parivāram, ēvaṟparivāram kāvaṟparivāram viṉipparivāram eṉa muttiṟappaṭṭirukkum.
araciyal, allatu araṇmaṉit toṭarpāṉa, uṇavu uṭi uṟiyuḷ uṇkalam aṇikalam ūrtikaḷ taṭṭumuṭṭukkaḷ mutaliya palvakip poruḷkaḷiyum, paṇiyāṭkaḷik koṇṭu ākkuvittu aracāṇiyi niṟivēṟṟum talivar, potuvākak karumikaḷ allatu kaṉmikaḷ eṉa aḻikkap peṟuvar. ivar ākkaviṉittuṟiyar. ivari ēvi naṭattuvōr karumavitikaḷ āvar. ivarallāta piṟarellām āḷviṉit tuṟiyar.
(2) mūvakic ciṟunāṭṭut talinakarkaḷilumirunta viṉiñar: maṇṭalam vaḷanāṭu nāṭu eṉṉum (allatu ivaṟṟiṟkotta) āḷnilappirivukaḷuḷ, ovvoṉṟiṟkum, ovvoru talivaṉ allatu atikāri iruntāṉ. maṇṭala atikārikku maṇṭalikaṉ allatu maṇṭala mutali eṉṟu peyar. nāṭṭatikārikku nāṭāḷvāṉ eṉṟum, nāṭṭu nāyakam eṉṟum, nāṭuṭiyāṉ eṉṟum, nāṭṭuviyavaṉ eṉṟum peyar. vaḷanāṭṭatikārikaḷum nāṭṭatikārikaḷ pōṉṟē aḻikkappaṭṭaṉarpōlum.
nāṭṭik kūṟupaṭa aḷappataṟku nāṭaḷappōr eṉṟum, nāṭṭiyaḷantatik kaṇkāṇittu uṇmi kāṇpataṟku nāṭukaṇkāṭci eṉṟum, kūṟupaṭa aḷanta nāṭṭiṟku varivitit taṟku nāṭukūṟu eṉṟum. nāṭṭik kāval ceytaṟku nāṭukāval eṉṟum, pala araciyal aluvalāḷar iruntaṉar.
(3) ūrkaḷilirunta viṉiñar : ovvōr (āḷnila) ūriyum āḷvataṟku, aracaṉāṇippaṭi ūrmakkaḷāl tērnteṭukkap paṭṭa oru capi iruntatu. accapiyārkku āḷuṅkaṇam eṉṟum, kaṇapperumakkaḷ eṉṟum, vāriyapperumakkaḷ eṉṟum, kaṇavāriyapperumakkaḷ eṉṟum peyar.
ūrccapiyāṉatu, ūrāṭci paṟṟiya palvēṟu kāriyaṅkaḷik kavaṉittaṟku, palvēṟu vāriyamākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. vāriyam eṉpatu mēṟpārvi allatu mēlāṇmi. ūrip paṟṟiya potukkāriyaṅkaḷiyum, aṟamuṟi kuṟṟattīrppu mutaliyavaṟṟiyum, kavaṉippatu āṭṭivāriyam (camvatcara vāriyam); tōṭṭam tōppu puṉcey mutaliyavaṟṟik kavaṉippatu tōṭṭa vāriyam; naṉceykaḷik kavaṉippatu kaḻaṉivāriyam; ēri kuḷam āṟu mutaliya nīrnilikaḷik kavaṉippatu ērivāriyam; ēri kuḷaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kaliṅkukaḷiyum matakukaḷiyuṅ kavaṉippatu kaliṅkuvāriyam; ūril vaḻaṅkum nāṇayaṅkaḷi nōṭṭañ ceytu naṟkācukaḷiyē celāvaṇi yākkuvatu poṉvāriyam; ūrccapik kaṇakkukaḷik kavaṉippatu kaṇakkuvāriyam; pañcakālattiṟ payaṉpaṭumpaṭi vaḷamikkālattil uṇavupporuḷkaḷit tokuttu vippatu pañcavāravāriyam (pañcavāriyam); ūrilum akkam pakkattilumuḷḷa peruvaḻikaḷik kavaṉippatu taṭivaḻivāriyam; ūriluḷḷa kuṭumpukaḷik kavaṉippatu kuṭumpu vāriyam.
ivaṟṟuḷ cila vāriyaṅkaḷ palavūrkaḷililli. ellā ūr kaḷilumiruntavi, āṭṭivāriyam, tōṭṭavāriyam, ēri vāriyam, poṉvāriyam, pañcavāravāriyam eṉṉum aintē. āṭṭi vāriyattiṟku ūrvāriyam tarumavāriyam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. kaḻaṉi mikutiyā yillāviṭattut tōṭṭa vāriyamē kaḻaṉikaḷiyum. ēri kuḷaṅkaḷ palavā yillāviṭattu ērivāri yamē kaliṅkukaḷiyum kavaṉittuk koḷḷum. ovvoru vāriya mum, taṉittaṉi capi allatu ciṟukuṟi eṉṟum, ellā vāriyamum cērntu makācapi allatu peruṅkuṟi eṉṟum, kūṟappeṟum.
ūrccapik kaṇakkeḻutupavaṉ karaṇattāṉ (mattiyataṉ) eṉappaṭuvāṉ; kaṇakku mikutiyāyillāta ciṟṟūrkaḷil kaṇakku vāriyam iruntirātu.
ūrik kāvalceypavarkkup pāṭikāval eṉṟu peyar. talimik kāvalaṉip perumpāṭikāval allatu taliyāri eṉṟum, avaṉukkuk kīḻppaṭṭavaric ciṟupāṭi kāval eṉṟum, kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam.
koṭikkālkaḷ tōṭṭantōppukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ vēlikaḷik kavaṉittaṟku, vēlināyakam eṉṟōr aluvalāḷaṉ iruntāṉ.
ūrccapi vēlikkāraṉukku veṭṭi (veṭṭiyāḷ veṭṭi yāṉ) eṉṟu peyar.
ūrāḷi eṉṟu ilakkiyattilum, ūrāḷaṉ ūruṭiyāṉ ūrmēl niṉṟa tiruvaṭikaḷ eṉṟu kalveṭṭilum, vantiruppatāl, ōriru perunāṭukaḷil ovvor āḷnila ūrukkum ōr araciyalatikāriyumiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
taṉiyūrkaḷil oruvakiyāṉa nakaraṅkaḷil, nakaramakkaḷāl tērnteṭukkappeṟṟa nakarattār eṉṉuñ capiyār iruntaṉar.
(4) perunāṭeṅkumirunta viṉiñar : talinakarilum, pērūr kaḷilum, vaḻakkāḷikaḷiṉ vaḻakkik kēṭṭu muṟiceyya aṟaṅ kūṟaviyamum; ellā āḷnila ūrkaḷilum, āvaṇaṅkaḷik kāppiṭuvataṟku āvaṇakkaḷariyum iruntaṉa. aṟaṅkū ṟaviyat tiṟku, aṟakkaḷam, maṉṟam, tarumācaṉam, niyāyacapi eṉṟum; ataṉ uṟuppiṉarkku, aṟakkaḷattār, maṉṟattār, tarumācaṉat tār, niyāyattār eṉṟum; peyaruṇṭu.
āṅkāṅkuḷḷa ūṭṭuppuri maṭam maruttuvacāli mutaliya aṟaniliyaṅkaḷi mēṟpārttaṟku, taṉit taṉik kuḻuvār amarttap peṟṟiruntaṉar. avarukku aṟappuṟaṅkāvalar eṉṟum, puṇṇiyakkaṇap perumakkaḷ eṉṟum peyar.
poṟkācukaḷiyum kācāka vaḻaṅkiṉa poṟkaṭṭikaḷiyum, niṟuttum urittum nōṭṭañceyyavum, cariyāṉavaṟṟiṉ mēl muttiriyiṭavum, vaṇṇakkar eṉṉum aluvalāḷar ellāp pērūrkaḷilumiruntaṉar.
araciṟiyum piṟavarikaḷum taṇṭuvataṟku ūrtoṟum taṇṭu vāṉ (taṇṭalāḷaṉ) eṉṉum araciyal viṉiñaṉ iruntāṉ. vari celuttāta kuṭikaḷit tuṉpuṟuttit taṇṭu pavaṉukkup pēṟāḷaṉ eṉṟu peyar.
(5) vēṟu cila viṉiñar : viṉiyaṟiyappaṭāta cila araciyaṟ pataviyāḷarum aluvalāḷarum atikārikaḷum, kalveṭṭiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷar. avar, aricumakkaḷ, mutalikaḷ, paḻaniyā yam, terippu, mukaveṭṭi. perumpaṇikkāraṉ, (naṭuvirukki eṉpavaṉ, aṟaṅkūṟaviyattāṉāy, allatu karaṇattāṉāy irukkalām) naṭuvirukki, ciṟutaṉam, peruntaṉam, ciṟutaram, peruntaram, peruntarattukku mēlnāyakam (ciṟutaram eṉpavaṉ nāṭṭatikāriyākavum, peruntaram eṉpavaṉ vaḷanāṭṭati kāriyākavum, peruntarattukku mēl nāyakam eṉpāṉ vaḷanāṭṭatikārikaḷ talivaṉākavum iruntirukkalām.) mutaliyōrāvar.
peruṅkōyiluḷḷa iṭamellām, atimēṟpārttaṟku oru taṉikkuḻu iruntatu. pa.ta.ā.
aracu vakikaḷ
1. oruvarāṭci
a. tāyāṭci - Matriarchy
ā. tantiyāṭci - Patriarchy
i. nāṭṭāṇmi - City State
ī. tēvāṭci - Theocracy
u. kōvaracu - Monarchy:
a. taṉṉaracu - Autocracy
ā. taṉmūppāṭci - Dictatorship
i. amiccaracu - Limited monarchy
ī. pārāḷumaṉṟak kōvaracu - Parliamentary Monarchy
2. palarāṭci
a. kurukkaḷāṭci - Hierocracy
ā. cīriyōrāṭci - Aristocracy
a. cilarāṭci - Oligarhcy
ā. palarāṭci - Polyarchy
i. celvarāṭci - Plutocracy
ī. paṭiyāṭci - Stratocracy
3. kuṭiyāṭci - Democracy
a. makkaḷāṭci - Republic
a. oṟṟiyāṭci - Unitary Government
ā. kūṭṭāṭci - Federal Government
ā. kūṭṭuṭimi - Socialism
i. potuvuṭimi - Communism
ī. kaṭṭuṭimi - Fascism
u. uḻippāḷarāṭci - Ergatocracy
ū. maṉpatāṭci - Mobocracy Ochlocracy
e. aṉivarāṭci - Pantisocracy
aracukaḷellām kīḻ varumāṟu vevvēṟu vakiyil ivviru tiṟappaṭum.
1. ceṅkōlāṭci Benign Govt. + koṭuṅkōlāṭci Despotic Government
2. taṉṉāṭci Home Rule x vēṟṟāṭci Xenocracy
3. oruvarāṭci Monocracy x palarāṭci Polyarchy
4. pēraracu Imperialism x ciṟṟaracu Feudalism
5. pārāḷumaṉṟa āṭci (Parliamentary Govt.) allāṭci (Non-Parliamentary Govt.)
6. āṭavarāṭci Androcracy x peṇṭirāṭci Gynecocracy Gynocracy.
7. mutalāḷiyar āṭci Capitalistic Govt. x  toḻilāḷiyar Proletarian Govt.  (ti.ta.ma.)
aracuṟuppu
palvakik kuṭiyaracu tōṉṟiyuḷḷa ikkālattu araciyal nūlār. araciyalamip pil nāṭṭi (State) uṭampākak koṇṭu āḷ nilam (Territory), kuṭikaḷ (Population), aracu (Government), kōṉmi (Sovereignty), oṟṟumi (Unity) eṉṉum ainti ataṉ uṟuppākkuvar.
ceṅkōl allatu koṭuṅkōl koṇṭa kōvaracē (Mon-archy) vaḻaṅkivanta muṟkālattil, pēraṟiñarāṉa tiruvaḷḷuvar araciyalamippil aracaṉiyē uṭampākak koṇṭu.
paṭikuṭi kūḻamiccu naṭpara ṇāṟu
muṭiyā ṉaracaru ḷēṟu  (kuṟaḷ. 381)
eṉṟu. paṭi kuṭi poruḷ amiccu naṭpu araṇ āṟiyum avaṉukku uṟuppākak kūṟiṉar. īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē avar kūṟiya araciṉ iyalvariyaṟi, iṉṟum poruttamāyum poruḷ potintatāyumiruppatu. avariṉ paltuṟik kalviyi yum paranta nōkkiyum āḻnta eṇṇattiyum kūrtta matiyiyum, kāṭṭap pōtiya cāṉṟākiṉṟatu.
aracuṟuppu āṟeṉṟu kuṟaḷiṟ kūṟiṉum, uṇmiyil ēḻeṉpatē āciriyar uḷḷak karuttu. nāṭillātu kuṭiyirukka muṭiyātatāka liṉ, nāṭṭik kuṭiyuḷ aṭakkiyē aracuṟuppu āṟeṉṟār. nāṭu eṉpatu oru taṉiyuṟuppākavē, nāṭu eṉṉun talippuk koṇṭa 74ām atikārattil avar kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
kuṭiyiṉṟi nāṭirukkalām. āyiṉ nāṭiṉṟik kuṭi irukka muṭi yātu. ivveḷiya vuṇmiyit tiruvaḷḷuvar aṟiyātirun tiruppiṉ, tirukkuṟaḷiyē iyaṟṟiyirukka muṭiyātu. mēlum, nāṭu eṉṟē oru taṉiyatikāram piṉṉar vakuttiruppatāl, muṉṉukkup piṉ muraṇākavum muṭiyum, ōratikāram muḻuvati yum talippuṭaṉ kavaṉiyātu pōvatum, iyaluñ ceyalaṉṟu.
tiruvaḷḷuvamāliyiṟ pōkkiyār pāṭal.
araciya liyin tamicciya līrin
turuval laraṇiraṇṭoṉ ṟeṉkū - ḻiruviyal
tiṇpaṭi naṭpup patiṉēḻ kuṭipatiṉmūṉ
ṟeṇporu ḷēḻā mivi
eṉṟē. potuvāka veḷiyiṭappeṟṟu vantirukkiṉṟatu. āṟum uṭiyāṉ aracaruḷ ēṟu eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar eṇ varampiṭṭuk kūṟiyiruppataṉālum. araṇ nāṭṭiṉ kūṟātalā lum, nāṭu muḻutum araṇamiyavuṅ kūṭumātalālum, nāṭṭi araṇuḷ aṭakkip pōkkiyār aṅṅaṉam pāṭiṉa reṉṟu. perumpālar karutalām.  āyiṉ, cēlaṅkallūri mēṉāḷ tamiḻ virivuriyāḷar tiru. naṭēca antaṇaṉār iyaṟṟiya tirukkuṟaḷ tiṟavu - poruṭpāl iṟimāṭci eṉṉum ariya ārāycci nūlil, pōkkiyār pāṭalāka.
araciya liyin tamicciya līrin
turinā ṭaraṇporu ḷovvoṉ - ṟuricāl
paṭiyiraṇṭu naṭpup patiṉēḻpaṉ mūṉṟu
kuṭiyeḻupāṉ ṟokkaporuṭ kūṟu
eṉṉum arumiyāṉa pāṭavēṟupāṭē kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ituvē cariyāṉa pāṭamāka iruttal vēṇṭum.
tirukkuṟaṭ patippukaḷellāvaṟṟilumuḷḷa pāṭattil. iru viyal eṉṉum taṉiccīr ilakkaṇappaṭi īriyal eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. atic ceyyuḷ tiripeṉṟu koḷḷiṉum, ak koḷki attuṇic ciṟantatākat tōṉṟa villi. ātalāl, tiru. naṭēca antaṇaṉār koṇṭa pāṭattiyē iṉip patippippōrellām mēṟkoḷvārāka.
tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟiya aracuṟuppukaḷuḷ. kūḻ (poruḷ). naṭpu eṉṉum iraṇṭum mika mutaṉmiyākak kavaṉikkattakkavi. intiyā ulakap perunāṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟāyiruppiṉum, taṉakkup pōtiya uṇavup poruḷiṉmiyāl, amerikkā viṟkum ēṉi nāṭukaṭkum ceṉṟu kiyēnta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. cila peru nāṭukaṭkup pōtiya uṇavup poruḷiruppiṉum, kittoḻiṟ karuvip poruṭkup piṟanāṭukaḷic cārntirukka vēṇṭiya nilimiyuḷḷatu.
iṉi, amerikkāvum iraciyāvum ettuṇi vallaracukaḷā yiruppiṉum, oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ (United Nations) eṉṉum amippu ēṟpaṭṭapiṉṉum,ataṟkuḷḷēyē; vaṭa attilāntiya uṭaṉpaṭikkiyamippu (N.A.T.O.) eṉṟum, teṉkiḻakku āciya uṭaṉpaṭikki yamippu (S.E.A.T.O.) eṉṟum, naṭuva uṭaṉ paṭikkiyamippu (C.T.O.) eṉṟum. iṉṉum piṟavāṟum, et tuṇiyō kūṭṭu naṭpukaḷit tōṟṟuvittuk koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉi, āppirikka nāṭukaḷum arapiya nāṭukaḷum piṟavum tammuṭ ceytukoṇṭirukkum naṭpuṭaṉpaṭikkikaḷip paṟṟic collavē vēṇṭuvatilli. iṅṅaṉam, oṟṟumikkāka ēṟpaṭṭa ulakamippu oṉṟā nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ (Disunited Nations) eṉṟu collumāṟē. ulaka nāṭukaḷiṉ pōkku iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
tiṅkaḷiyaṭintu taṉ aṟiviyal (Scientific), kammiyal (Technolo-gical) vaḷarcciyiṉ talittiṟattit terivittuk koṇṭirukkum amerikkāvum, evven nāṭṭoṭu evvevvakiyil naṭpup pūṇalāmeṉṟu cūḻvatilum, cūḻcci muṭipi niṟivēṟṟu vatilumē, kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu. iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum; oru māperu vallaracu.
viṉivaliyun taṉvaliyum māṟṟāṉ valiyum
tuṇivaliyum tūkkic ceyal  (kuṟaḷ 471)
eṉpataṟkēṟpa, taṉ vāḻviṟkut tuṇivaliyi iṉṟiyami yātatākak karutiṉ, īrāyiram āṇṭiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻaṅkālattil, naṭpu ettuṇic ciṟanta aracuṟuppāyiruntiruttal vēṇṭu meṉpati eṇṇik kāṇka.
iṉi, oru nāṭṭiṉ nilimi, cūḻnilikkēṟpa aṭikkaṭi māṟik koṇṭu varuvatāl, kōṉmi eṉpatu nilittavuṟuppaṉṟu.
palkuḻuvum pāḻceyyum uṭpakiyum vēntalikkum
kolkuṟumpum illatu nāṭu (kuṟaḷ 735)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟiyiruppatāl, oṟṟumi eṉpatum avarkkut teriyātataṉṟu.
iṉṟu makkaḷtoki maṭṭiṟku miñcip perukiyiruppatālum, tamiḻam eṉpatu moḻi ilakkiyam, paṇpāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟil taṉippaṭṭatātalālum. etirkālat tamiḻaka aracu puttam putiya muṟiyil amital vēṇṭum. (tamiḻam)

(agni) - L. ignis, Lith. ugnis, Russ. ogone, Slav. ognj. 
“From what root is Agni derived? He is the foremost leader, he is led foremost in sacrifices, he makes every things, to which it inclines, a part of himself. ‘He is a drying agent’ says Stha#ulas@t@hivi, it does not make wet, it does not moisten. ‘It is derived from three verbs’, says Sakarpuni, from going from shining or burning, and from leading.’ He, indeed, takes the letter a from the root i (to go), the letter g from the root an#j (to shine), or dah (to burn) with the root (ni) (to lead) as the last member.” eṉṟu lakṣmaṉ carūp eḻutiya The Nighantu & the Nirukta eṉṉum nūlil 120 ām pakkattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṟiñar itaṉ porut tattik kaṇṭukoḷka.

aruntati kāṭṭal
aruntati kāṭṭal eṉpatum āriyat toṭarpu karutiyatē. tamiḻa marapuppaṭi aruntati taliyāya kaṟparaciyallaḷ. avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉākiya vaciṭṭa muṉivaṉ oḻukkattip paṟṟi aiyuṟṟu, avaṉāl viṇmīṉākac cāvikkappaṭṭāḷ eṉṟu kati kūṟu kiṉṟatu. tamiḻap pattiṉip peṇṭirō, kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu coṟṟiṟam pāmi, eṉṉuṅ koḷkiyiṉar. ātalāl, āti mantiyār, pūtapāṇṭiyaṉ tēviyār, kaṇṇakiyār, āriyap paṭi kaṭanta neṭuṅceḻiyaṉ tēviyār, tilakavatiyār mutaliya ettuṇiyō taliyāya tamiḻakak kaṟput teyvaṅ kaḷirukkavum avari viṭṭuviṭṭu aruntatiyi niṉippittal kaṉiyiruppak kāy kavarntaṟṟē.  (ta.ti.35.36.)

col varalāṟu
ammutal = amuṅkutal, amukkutal.
am - (ammi) = amukki yarikkuṅ kal.

amukku - amukkal - amukkalāṉ = koppuḷaṅkaḷi amuṅkac ceyyum taḻi.
am - amiḻ - āḻ - aḻuntu - aḻuttu - aḻuttam.
amukkuvataṉālum aḻuttuvataṉālum ōriṭam paḷḷamākum; oru poruḷ taṭṭiyākum.
am - amal - aval = 1. paḷḷam. avaliḻiyiṉum mici yēṟiṉum. (puṟam. 102:3). 2. viḷinilam. 3. kuḷam. 4. taṭṭiyāka iṭitta nellarici allatu kamparici. paḷḷak karuttiṉiṉṟu kuḷḷak karuttun tōṉṟum.
o.nō. paḷ - paḷḷam. paḷ - paḷḷi = 1. kuḷḷam. 2. paḷḷiyāṭu. paḷḷiyaṉ = kuṟukit taṭittavaṉ. paḷḷiyāṭu = kuḷḷamāṉa āṭu. kuḷḷak karuttiṉiṉṟu cappik karuttut tōṉṟum.
k - t., pōli o.nō. cemmi - cevvi.
cuma - cumal - cuval = cumakkun tōṭpaṭṭi.
aval - (avali) - avari = cappiyāṉa allatu taṭṭiyāṉa kāyvaki.
o.nō. ayil - ayili - ayiri.
la - ra, pōli.
avari vakikaḷiṟ cila uruṇṭu tiraṇṭiruppiṉum, perumpāṉmi nōkki avi vilakkākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. mēlum, piñcu niliyilēyē camikkumāṟu maruttuva nūlkaḷ kūṟuvatāl, ellā vakikaḷum piñcu niliyil cappiyākavē yiruttali nōkkuka.
ovvoru poruḷum ataṉ ciṟappiyalpu paṟṟiyē peyar peṟuvatu marapu. vāḻi, kattari, muruṅki, veṇṭi, pūcaṇi, curi, pīrkku, puṭali, pākal mutaliya piṟa kāyvakikaḷ ellāvaṟṟōṭum oppunōkki, avari yoṉṟē taṭṭiyā yiruttalik kaṇṭaṟika.
paḷḷam eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ mēlmaṭṭattiṉ tāḻvu; kuṭṭi eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ uyarattiṉ tāḻvu; cappi eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ tiṇṇattiṉ allatu puṭippiṉ tāḻvu. im muk karuttum ōriṉappaṭṭaṉa. āḻamillāta kiṇṇam taṭṭam eṉap peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
poruḷvaki
(1)	āṭṭuk kompavari = āṭṭuk kompupōl vaṭivuḷḷa avari; kind of bean that resembles goat’s horn in shape.
(2)	ārāl mīṉavari = ārāl mīṉ pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷa avari; kind of bean that resembles sand - eel in shape.
(3)	āṉik kātavari = āṉik kātu pōl vaṭivuḷḷa avari; kind of bean that resembles elephant’s ear in shape.
(4)	kaṇuvavari = koṭiyiṉ kaṇukkaḷil kāykkum avari; kind of bean that brings forth fruits at joints also.
(5)	koḻuppavari = kuṭṭiyākak koḻutta catiyuḷḷa avari; kind of bean that is short and fleshy.
(6)	kōḻiyavari (kōḻikkā lavari) = kōḻi viral pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷa avari; canavalia gladiata.
(7)	civappavari - cevvavari = cenniṟa avari; a red variety of bean, lablab cultratus.
(8)	ciṟṟavari - maṇiyavari = a small variety of bean.
(9)	tīvāntara vavari = kīḻit tīviṉiṉṟu vanta avari;  a bean from the eastern islands (w.).
(10)	nakaravari - dolichos rugosus (W)
(11)	pālavari - veḷḷavari; a white variety of bean, 
	avarik koḻuṅkoṭi viḷarkkāy
	kōṭpata māka.  (puṟam 120. 10-11)
(12)	pēravari - a large variety of bean.
(13)	muṟukkavari = tirukal muṟukalākavuḷḷa avari; psopha carpus tetragonolobus.
iṉapporuḷkaḷ
(1)	kappal avari = french beans.
(2)	kāṭṭavari = koḻuppavari pōṉṟatāyuḷḷa mocci (cēlam vaḻakku); wild bean, lablab vulgaris.
vīṭṭup puṟattil viḷivikkappaṭum avariyi nōkki mocci kāṭṭavari eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, avarikku vīṭṭavari eṉa ōr aṭimoḻip peyar peṟappaṭum.
vīṭṭavarikkum kāṭṭavarikkum vēṟupāṭu
		vīṭṭavari		kāṭṭavari
(1)	vīṭṭup puṟattil 		kāṭṭup puṟattil
	viḷikkappaṭuvatu 	viḷikkappaṭuvatu
(2)	koṭiyākap paṭarvatu	kuttuc ceṭiyāka vaḷarvatu
(3)	iḷaṅkāy kaṟiyākac 	muṟṟiya kāy avittuc
	camikkappaṭuvatu		 tiṉṉappaṭuvatu
(4)	tōl melliyatu	tōl valliyatu.
(3) ayalnāṭṭu (cīmi) avari = marutaṅkāy pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷatum nīṇṭatumāṉa veḷināṭṭuk kāyvaki; a kind of foreign vegetable. ayal nāṭṭi allatu mēl nāṭṭip potu makkaḷ cīmi eṉṉum vaṭacollāṟ kuṟippar. iṅku vaṭacol eṉṟatu vaṭanāṭṭuc colli.
(4) cīṉiyavari - kottavari - kuttavari = kottuk kottākak kāykkum kāyvaki; a species of pulse whose fruits are in bunches.
(5) cuṭaliyavari = cuṭukāṭṭup pakkattil iyaṟkiyāka viḷivatum, cīṉiyavari pōṉṟatum, uṇṇap paṭātatumāṉa kāyvaki; a species of inedible bean growing wildly near burning grounds. itu pēyavariyeṉavumpaṭum.
(6) pāṭavari - vāḷavari - tampaṭṭavari - cāṭṭavari = vāḷ pōl nīṇṭu paṭṭiyāka virukkum kāyvaki; sword-bean, canavalia ensiformis.
