avarikkāy eṉpatu maliyāḷattil avarakka eṉat tirintuḷḷatu. amarakka eṉpatu avarakka eṉpataṉ tirivu.
tuḷuc coṟkaḷuḷ rakaramuḷḷavi oruvakiyiyum ṭakara muḷḷavi maṟṟoru vakiyiyum kuṟippaṉavākap parō emaṉō tiraviṭa akaramutaliyiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tuḷu akaramutaliyil aṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟikkappaṭa villi.
iṇimoḻi
avari tuvari.
ciṟappuk kuṟippu
avari koyyuna rāra māntum  (puṟam 215:5)
ciṟukoṭik koḷḷē poṟikiḷa ravariyoṭu  (puṟam. 335:5)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭaṭikaḷ, paṇṭik kālattil avariyum kāṭṭil, moccipōla viḷikkappaṭṭa teṉṟō, mocciyum  avariyeṉap paṭṭateṉṟō, karuta iṭantarum.
koṭṭiyavari yeṉṟu patārtta kuṇa cintāmaṇi kuṟittiruppatu. oru taṉi vakiyākat teriyavilli.
cīrtiruttam:
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali, kōḻi yavariyi vāḷavari (sword-bean) yeṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷatu.
ārāl mīṉavari, āṉikkātavari, koḻuppavari, cuṭali avari mutaliya peyarkaḷ atiṟ kuṟikkappeṟavilli.
mēl nāṭṭut tamiḻaṟiñar tam aṟiyāmiyāl avarikkāy eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa avarakka, amarakka eṉṉum coṟkaḷiyum avariyik kuṟikkum taṉic coṟkaḷākak kuṟittuḷḷaṉar.
aviyuṇavum ceviyuṇavum
māntaṉ uyirvāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta kāṟṟu, nīr, uṇavu eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉuḷ, mutalatu uṭal nalattiṟkēṟṟa iṭatti lellām irukku miṭattilēyē muyaṟciyiṉṟi ilavacamākap peṟakkūṭiyatu; iṭiyatu pañca millāta kālamellām ciṟu muyaṟciyāṟ peṟakkūṭiyatu; iṟutiyatē viḷivukkāla mel lām muyaṟciyiṉālō, vilikoṭuttō peṟakkūṭiya tākum.
nākarika vāḻkkikku vēṟu pala poruḷkaḷum vēṇṭu māyiṉum, uyir vāḻkkikku vēṇṭiyatē iṉṟiyamiyāta tātaliṉ, māntaṉ kavalippaṭṭut tēṭa vēṇṭiya mutaṉmi yāṉa poruḷ uṇavoṉṟē.
uṇaviṉālēyē uyir uṭampil niṟpatuṭaṉ, uṭampum uriya vaḷarcciyaṭikiṉṟatu. uṇaviṉ māṟṟamē uṭampu. uṭampiṉ naṉṉiliyē uyir nili.
nīriṉ ṟamiyā yākkik kellām
uṇṭi koṭuttō ruyirkoṭut tōrē
uṇṭi mutaṟṟē yuṇaviṉ piṇṭam
uṇaveṉap paṭuvatu nilattoṭu nīrē
nīrum nilaṉum puṇari yōrīṇ(ṭu)
uṭampu muyirum paṭittici ṉōrē.  (puṟam.18)
uṇavu peṟutaṟkuriya palvēṟu toḻilkaḷip payilvataṟkum, avaṟṟic ceytaṟkum, uriya paruvattil maṇam purivataṟkum, piṉṉar makkaḷip peṟṟu vaḷarttaṟkum, nīṇṭa kālam uṭampiṉ naṉṉili vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, ulaka vāḻkkiyi virumpuvōrellām uṭampip pēṇuvatu iṉṟiyamiyāta tākiṉṟatu.
iṉi, paṇpaṭṭa makkaḷ immi vāḻkkikku maṭṭumaṉṟi maṟumi vāḻkkik kum aṟivupeṟa vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, ataṉ poruṭṭum uṭampiṉ nīḷ naṉṉili iṉṟiyamiyāta tākum.
uṭampār aḻiyiṉ uyirār aḻivar
tiṭampaṭa meyññāṉañ cēravu māṭṭār
uṭampi vaḷarkkum upāyam aṟintē
uṭampi vaḷarttēṉ uyirvaḷart tēṉē.  (724)
uṭampiṉi muṉṉam iḻukkeṉ ṟiruntēṉ
uṭampiṉuk kuḷḷē uṟuporuḷ kaṇṭēṉ
uṭampuḷē uttamaṉ kōyilkoṇ ṭāṉeṉ(ṟu)
uṭampiṉi yāṉirun tōmpukiṉ ṟēṉē.  (725)
eṉpavi tirumūlar tirumantiraṅkaḷ.
eṉavē, māntaṉ irumi vāḻviṟkum iṉṟiyamiyātatu uṭampu eṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. ittakiya uṭampi uruvākku vatum iṟutivari nilip paṭuttuvatum uṇavē.
uṇavi viḷippatu uḻavuttoḻil. attoḻili makkaḷ aṉivarum mēṟkoḷḷiṉum, iru nilimiyāl uṇavut taṭṭu ēṟpaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu maḻiyiṉmi; iṉṉoṉṟu makkaṭ perukkam.
maḻiyiṉmi nīṭikkātu; ulakam oruṅkē aḻiyum ūḻiyiṟuti vari, pār muḻutum paravavuñ ceyyātu. ātalāl, pañcakālattil vaḷanāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vaṟaṭci nāṭṭiṟku vēṇṭiya uṇavi varuvittuk koḷḷalām. āyiṉ makkaṭ perukkattāl ēṟpaṭum uṇavuttaṭṭu nilittatum nīkka muṭiyātatumākum. maḻi ērāḷamākap peyyiṉum uṇavupporuḷ viḷivatu nilamē yātalālum, makkaṭ perukkattiṟkēṟpa nilamum virivuṟāmi yālum, nilavarampīṭum pakirntuṇṭalum, kuṭumpa maṭṭuppāṭum araciyalēṟpāṭāka amikiṉṟaṉa. ivvaki araciyalē kūṭṭuṭimi (Socialism) eṉpatu.
kūṭṭuṭimi eṉṉum āṭcimuṟi ikkālattatēyāṉālum, ataṉ uyirnāṭik kūṟāṉa pakirntuṇṭali, ēṟattāḻa īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē, teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvar.
pāttūṇ marīi yavaṉip paciyeṉṉum
tīppiṇi tīṇṭa varitu.  (227)
pakuttuṇṭu palluyi rōmputal nūlōr
tokuttavaṟṟu ḷellān tali.  (322)
tammi liruntu tamatupāt tuṇṭaṟṟāl
ammā arivi muyakku  (1107)
eṉṉum kuṟaḷmaṇikaḷāl neṟiyiṭṭuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
avar kālattil makkaṭ perukkamilli; nilakkuṟivumilli. avi yiruntiruppiṉ.
ilameṉ ṟacii yiruppārik kāṇiṉ
nilameṉṉum nallāḷ nakum  (1040)
eṉṟu kūṟiyirār.
ākavē, avar ikkālattil iruntārāyiṉ, nila varampīṭṭiṟkum, kuṭumpak kaṭṭuppāṭṭut tiṭṭattiṟkum neṟi vakuttēyiruppār.
avar iṉṟu illāviṭiṉum, avar pōṅkilēyē, intiyat talimi mantiriṉiyārāṉa tirumāṭṭiyār intirākānti yammiyār, irupāṉ kuṟippup poruḷāṭcit tiṭṭatti vakuttiruppatu, intiyar mītu iṟivaṉ koṇṭa pēraruḷeṉṟē karututal vēṇṭum.
makkaḷ āṟaṟivu paṭitta uyartiṇi eṉṉum uyarnta uyiriṉattic cērntavarātalāl. puṟavuṭampiṟkuriya aviyuṇavi maṭṭumaṉṟi akavuṭampiṟkuriya aṟivuṇaviyum arumpāṭu paṭṭēṉum tēṭi nukartal vēṇṭum. aviyuṇaveṉṟatu avitta allatu camitta uṇavi. nākarika makkaḷuṇavu avikkappaṭṭatē eṉpatic colla vēṇṭuvatilli. aṟivuṇavu cevivāyilākak kēṭṭu uṭkoḷḷap paṭutalāl ceviyuṇa veṉappaṭṭatu.
cevivā yāka neñcukaḷa ṉākak
kēṭṭavi kēṭṭavi viṭātuḷat tamittu  (naṉ. 40)
eṉṟu naṉṉūṟ pāyiraṅ kūṟutal kāṇka.
aviyuṇavi maṭṭum oruvaṉ virumpuvāṉāyiṉ, avaṉ uyar tiṇiyic cērntavaṉākāṉ. uṭampāl uyartiṇiyāyiṉum uḷattāl aḵṟiṇi yākavē karutappaṭuvāṉ. avaṉāl makkaḷiṉattiṟkē iḻivu uṇṭākum. ataṉāl,
cevikkuṇa villāta pōḻtu ciṟitu
vayiṟṟukkum īyap paṭum.  (412)
eṉṟu ceviyuṇaviṉ perum ciṟappi eṭuttukkūṟi.
ceviyiṟ cuviyuṇarā vāyuṇarviṉ mākkaḷ
aviyiṉum vāḻiṉum eṉ.  (420)
eṉṟu ceviyuṇavu koḷḷātavaṉi iṟappa iḻittum paḻittum kūṟiṉār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
ātalāl, tiraviṭattiṟkut tāyum, āriyattiṟku mūlamumāṉa ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyākiya oppuyarvaṟṟa tamiḻi, tāy moḻiyākak koṇṭiruntum, ataṉic caṟṟum pēṇātu. tamiḻ vantu vayiṟṟi niṟikkumā eṉṟu puṟakkaṇittuk kūṟuvatu, taṉṉit tamiḻaṉeṉṟu collik koḷpavaṉukkuc caṟṟum takātu. ceviyuṇavuṇṭu koṇṭē aviyuṇavum peṟa iyalumpōtu. oruvaṉ ēṉ tamiḻit taḷḷa vēṇṭum? tamiḻit tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭavaṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil tamiḻariṭit tamiḻiṟ pēcittāṉē vāḻavoṇṇum! atu karutiyēṉum kūṭṭuṭimik koḷkiyaṉ tamiḻip pōṟṟuka. tāṉ pōṟṟāviṭiṉum pōṟṟuvāri iyaṉṟavari pōṟṟuka.  (teṉmoḻi kumpam 1976.)
aviḻ
aviḻ = ventu malarnta cōṟṟup parukki, cōṟu.
aviḻ - aviḻtu - aviḻtam - amiḻtam = uṇavu
aṟucuvi nālvaki amiḻtam  (maṇi 28:116.)
aviḻtu - amiḻtu - amutu = cōṟu, uṇavu, nīr.
nīrum uṇavātalāl amuteṉap peṟṟatu.
amiḻtam - amutam = cōṟu, nīr.
marumam - (mammam) - ammam = muli, tāyppāl, kuḻanti yuṇavu  (ti.ma. 42.)
pālum oruvaki yuṇavātalālum, ammam amutu eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ orupuṭi oppumiyiṉālum, amutu eṉṉum collum pālik kuṟittatu. amutu = pāl. amutam, pāl, amiḻtu = pāl.  (ti.ma.43.)
avi - capā (sabha#) - i.vē.
amital = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal. ami = kūṭṭam.
ami -avi o.nō. ammi - avvi, kumi - kuvi.
aviyaṭakkiyal aviyalkiḷavi eṉpaṉa, tolkāppiyarkku muṉpē toṉṉūlkaḷil vaḻaṅkivanta ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷām.
vāyuṟi vāḻttē aviyaṭak kiyalē
ceviyaṟi vuṟūuveṉa aviyum aṉṉa.  (tol. 1367)
aviyal kiḷavi maṟittaṉar kiḷattal.  (tol. 925)
avi - aviyam.
tanti makaṟkāṟṟu naṉṟi yaviyattu
munti yiruppac ceyal.  (kuṟaḷ 67)
avi - cavi. o.nō. iḷi - ciḷi, uti - cuti.
cavi - capā (va.)
vaṭacol vaṭiviṟkup piṉvarumāṟu mūvēṟu vakiyil mūlaṅkāṭṭuvar.  (va.va.)
(1) sa = saha (kūṭa). sa-sapā = kūṭṭam.
(2) pā (bha#) = viḷaṅku (to shine). sapā = viḷakkattōṭu (pirakācattōṭu) kūṭiyatu, aṟiñaravi.
ipporuḷiṟ pā eṉṉum vaṭacol pāl eṉṉum teṉ colloṭu toṭarpuṭiyatāyirukkalām.
pāl = veṇmi. pāṟṟiru nīṟṟem paramaṉi tiruvāca (446).
(3) sip.
itu uṭaṉpiṟappik kuṟikkum mēliyāriyat tiyūttāṉiyac col. ākkacuppōrṭuc ciṟṟakaramutaliyil (C.O.D.) piṉvarumāṟu kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
sib = a brother or sister. sibship = the group of children from the same two  parents.
OE sib (b), MDu sib (be), OHG sippi, Goth sibjis.
im mūvaki mūlattiṉum tamiḻmūlamē poruttamāyiruttal kāṇka.  (va.va. 80-81)
avi : amital = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal. ami = kūṭṭam, ampalam, ami - avi. o.nō. ammi - avvi, kumi - kuvi.
avi - cavi, o.nō. amiyam - camiyam, eṭṭi - ceṭṭi. avi - aviyam. am oru perumip poruṭ piṉṉoṭṭu. e-ṭu: nili niliyam, kampu - kampam, cavi - capā (va.).  (ti.ma. 737.)
aḻintupōṉa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ
ēṉiya moḻikaḷilellām ilakkiyam varavara uyarntum mikuntum varavum, tamiḻilō varavarat tāḻntum kuṟintum vantirukkiṉṟatu. vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi yilakkiyaṅkaḷ iru peruṅkaṭalkaḷākat toṉṉūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, vaṭamoḻikkaṭal muṉṉuḷḷapaṭiyē iṉṟum kuṟiyātuḷatu. āṉāl, teṉmoḻikkaṭalō oru ciṟu kuḷamāka vaṟṟiyuḷḷatu.
akattiyarukku muntiya tamiḻnūlkaḷil, ceṅkōṉtaric celavu eṉṉum ciṟiya nūliṉ oru pakutiyē iṉṟu kiṭittuḷatu.
talikkaḻakattārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa ettuṇiyō paripāṭalum, mutunāriyum mutukurukum, kaḷariyāviriyumeṉa ittoṭak kattaṉavum iṭikkaḻakattārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa kaliyum, kurukum, veṇṭāḷiyum, viyāḻamāliyakavalumeṉa it toṭakkat taṉavum kaṭikkaḻakattārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa paripāṭaluṭpala vum, kūttum variyum ciṟṟiciyum, pēriciyum ippōtilli.
talikkaḻakakkālat tilakkaṇamākiya akattiyamum, iṭikkaḻakat tilakkaṇa mākiya māpurāṇamum icinūlum pūta purāṇamum ippōtilli.
iṉi, aṭi nūl, aṇiyiyal, avinayam, avinantamāli, āciriya māli, āciriyamuṟi, āṉantaviyal, iḷantiriyam, intira kāḷiyam, aintiram, ōviyanūl, kaṭakaṇṭu, kaṇakkiyal, kaliyāṇa kāti, kaḷavu nūl, kavimayakkiṟi, kalikkōṭṭut taṇṭu, kālakēci, kākkipāṭiṉiyam, kuṇṭalakēci, kuṇanūl, kōḷ nūl, caṅkayāppu, cayantam, cintam, caccapuṭaveṇpā, cātavākaṉam, ciṟpanūl, ciṟukākkipāṭiṉiyam, ciṟukurīiyuri, cuttāṉantap pirakācam, ceyaṉmuṟi, ceyiṟṟiyam, tantiravākkiyam, tumpippāṭṭu, takaṭūr yāttiri, tāḷacamuttiram, tāḷavaki yōttu, tēcikamāli, nākakumārakāviyam, nīlakēci, pañca pāratīyam, paratam, pañcamarapu, patiṉāṟupaṭalam, paratacēṉā patiyam, parinūl, palkāppiyam, palkāyam, paṉmaṇimāli, paṉṉiru paṭalam, pāvippāṭṭu, pāṭṭiyaṉ marapu. pāṭṭu maṭi, pāratam (peruntēvaṉār iyaṟṟiyatu), puṇarppāvi, putiyal nūl, purāṇacākaram, periyapammam, peruvallam, pōkkiyam, maṇiyāram, mativāṇar nāṭakattamiḻ nūl, mantira nūl, mayēccuraryāppu, mārkkaṇṭēyar kāñci, muṟuval, muttoḷḷāyiram, mūppeṭṭuc ceyyuḷ, mūvaṭi muppatu, mōtirappāṭṭu, yacōtarakāviyam, vaccattoḷḷāyiram, vañcippāṭṭu, vaḷiyāpati, vāyppiyam, viḷakkattār kūttu mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta nūlkaḷ kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilum piṟ kālattilumiruntavi yippōtilli.
pāṇṭiyaṉ tamiḻppāratam, tiruccirāppaḷḷiyantāti, srīrāja rāja vijayam, nāṭakanūl, vīraṇukka vijayam, kulōttuṅka cōḻa cariti, aṣṭātaca purāṇam, aruṇili vicākaṉ tamiḻp pāratam, peruvañci, attikirit tirumāl cintu, kāṅkēyaṉ piḷḷikkavi, vīramāli, tiruvatikik kalampakam. tiruvalli yantāti mutaliyavi, ituvariyaṟiyappaṭāta nūlkaḷākac cāsaṉat tamiḻkkavi caritattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kaṭikkaḻakattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa pulavarkaḷiyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷil pala ippōtu kiṭikkavilli. oṭṭakkūttariyaṟṟiya arumpit toḷḷāyiram, etirnūl mutaliyavi iṉṉum veḷivaravilli.
iṭikkaḻaka virukkiyākiya kapāṭapurattil, eṇṇāyirat teccam nūl kaḷiruntu piṉpu kaṭalkōḷuṇṭaḻintaṉaveṉ ṟoru vaḻimuṟi vaḻakkuḷḷatu.
paṟpala kalikaḷ paṇṭit tamiḻiliruntu piṉpaḻintu pōyiṉa veṉpati.
ēraṇa muruvam yōkam icikaṇak kiratam cālam
tāraṇa maṟamē cantam tampanīr nilamu lōkam
māraṇam poruḷeṉ ṟiṉṉa māṉanūl yāvum vāri
vāraṇaṅ koṇṭa tantō vaḻivaḻip peyaru māḷa
eṉṉum ceyyuḷālaṟiyalām.
mēṟkūṟiya nūlkaḷellām aḻintu pōṉamikkuk kaṭal kōḷkaḷum, āriyattiṉāl tamiḻar uyarnilikkalvi yiḻantamiyum, iruperuṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷākum.
tolkāppiyam oṉṟil vallavarē oru perum pulavarāka matikkappaṭukiṉṟār. tolkāppiyattiṟkuc camamum atiṉuñ ciṟantavumāṉa ettuṇiyō nūlkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.  (o.mo.)
aḻipaci
kolvatu pōla varuttuvataṉālum kuṭippiṟappu kalvi māṉam aṟivuṭimi mutaliya pēṟukaḷiyum, paṇpukaḷiyum aḻip pataṉālum kaṭumpaci aḻipaciyām.  (ti.ma. 136.)
aḻukkāṟu
aḻuṅkutal varuntutal allatu tuṉpuṟutal. aḻuṅkuvatu aḻukku. atu uṟu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi peṟṟu aḻukkuṟu eṉa niṟkum. aḻukkuṟutal, piṟarākkam kaṇṭu poṟātu varun tutal. nācamuṟu eṉṉum viṉi nācamaṟu eṉṟu ulaka vaḻakkil tirintāṟ pōṉṟu aḻukkuṟu eṉpatum aḻukkaṟu eṉa ilakkiya vaḻakkil tirintatu. aḻukkaṟ ṟakaṉṟārum illi (170) eṉa vaḷḷuvarē kūṟutal kāṇka. nācamuṟṟup pōvāṉ eṉpatu nācamaṟṟup pōvāṉ eṉṟē vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.  (ti.ma.102.)
aḷ
aḷ-ala (muḷ) - va.
o.nō. nuḷ - naḷ - naḷḷi = naṇṭu. naḷ-naḷi = tēḷ. vaṭamoḻi yil ali eṉṉuñ col tēḷiyuṅ kuṟikkum.  (va.va.82.)
aḷakam
aḷakam - alaka - aḷ = ceṟivu. aḷḷutal = ceṟital. aḷḷal = nerukkam. aḷam = ceṟivu. aḷam - aḷakam = 1. peṇmayir. (piṅ), ceṟinta kūntal. 2. mayirk kuḻaṟci (piṅ.).  (va.va.81.)
aḷavikaḷ
paḻantamiḻāṭciyiṟ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa aḷavikaḷ, perum pālum ikkālattuḷḷa tamiḻ aḷavikaḷē. avi eṇṇal, eṭuttal, mukattal, nīṭṭal eṉa nālvakippaṭum.
(1) eṇṇalaḷavi: eṇṇalaḷavi, ciṟṟilakkam pērilakkam eṉa iruvaki. ari kāl arikkāl vīcam (mākāṇi) mutaliya piṉṉaveṇkaḷ ciṟṟilakkamum, oṉṟu iraṇṭu mūṉṟu mutaliya muḻuveṇkaḷ pērilakkamum ākum. ciṟṟilakkattil, kīḻvāyilakkameṉṟum mēlvāyilakkameṉṟum iruvaki yuṇṭu. kīḻari (1/640), kīḻkkāl (1/1280), kīḻarikkāl (1/2560), kīḻvīcam (1/5120), mutaliyaṉa kīḻvāyc ciṟṟilakkamum, mēlari (ari). mēṟkāl (kāl), mēlarikkāl (arikkāl) mēlvīcam (vīcam) mutaliyaṉa mēlvāyc ciṟṟilakkamumākum. kīḻvāyc ciṟṟilakkattoṭu oppu nōkkiyē, ari, kāl  arikkāl mutaliyaṉa mēlvāyc ciṟṟilakkameṉappaṭum. mēlvāyc ciṟṟilakkattil aṭimaṭṭa eṇ mēl muntiri eṉṉum muntiri (1/320) yum, kīḻvāyc ciṟṟilakkattil aṭimaṭṭa eṇ kīḻmuntiri (1/102.400) yum, ākum.
(2) eṭuttalaḷavi: eṭuttal eṉpatu eṭuttu niṟuttal. eṭutta laḷaviyil, poṉṉum maṇiyum niṟukka oṉṟum, piṟa poruḷ kaḷi niṟukka oṉṟumāka. iruvakiyuṇṭu. poṉṉiṟi yaḷavikkup poṉṉilakkam eṉṟu peyar. poṉ ērāḷamā yiruppiṉ, atuvum piṟa poruḷpōl niṟukkappaṭum.
kīḻvaruvatu poṉṉaḷavi :
4 nel 		-	1 kuṉṟimaṇi
2 kuṉṟimaṇi 	-	1 mañcāṭi
2 mañcāṭi 	-	1 vallam (paṇattūkkam)
10 vallam 	-	1 kaḻañcu (16 avuṉcu)
kīḻvaruvatu piṟaporuḷaḷavi
4 kaḵcu 		-	1 palam (toṭi)
8 palam 		-	1 cēr
5 cēr 		-	1 vīci
2½ vīci	 -	1 tulām (kā, tūkku, niṟi)
8 vīci 		-	1 maṇaṅku
20 maṇaṅku 	-	1 kaṇṭi (pāram)
cilaviṭaṅkaḷil 5 vīci koṇṭatu oru tulāmāka viruntatu. araca muttiriyiṭṭa aḷavukkal, kuṭiñikkal, pāṭikkal, paṇṭārakkal eṉṉum peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu, nakaraṅkaḷil atu nakarakkal eṉappaṭṭatu. pēraḷavāṉa poṉṉi niṟukka āṇikkal eṉṉum niṟikal iruntatu. mikap pēraḷavāṉa poṉ tulām kaṇakkāka niṟukkappeṟṟatu.
(3) mukattalaḷavi: mukantaḷakkappaṭum poruḷkaḷuḷ, nel perumpāṉmiyākavum ciṟantatākavumiruttal paṟṟi, mukatta laḷavi nellilakkam eṉappaṭum.
kīḻvaruvatu mukattalaḷavi :
2 ceviṭu 		-	piṭi
5 ceviṭu 		-	1 āḻākku
2 āḻākku	-	1 uḻakku
2 uḻakku 		-	1 uri
2 uri 			-	1 nāḻi
8 nāḻi 		-	1 kuṟuṇi (marakkāl)
2 kuṟuṇi 		-	1 patakku
2 patakku		-	1 tūṇi (kāṭi)
3 tūṇi		-	1 kalam
400 kuṟuṇi 	-	1 karici (paṟi)
araca muttiriyiṭṭa aḷavi nāḻiyum marakkālum, araca paṇṭārattil aracaṉ peyariyum, kōyiṟ paṇṭārattil teyvap peyariyum tāṅkiyiruntaṉa. cōḻāntakaṉ nāḻi aruṇmoḻittēvaṉ marakkāl eṉpaṉa aracaṉ peyariyum, āṭavallāṉ marakkāl ceppukkāl tirucciṟṟampalamuṭi yāṉ marakkāl eṉpaṉa teyvap peyariyum, tāṅkiyavi yākum.
iṉi, ovvoru nāṭṭiṟkum ciṟappāṉa perumukattalaḷavum uṇṭu. pāṇṭi nāṭṭil kōṭṭi (21 marakkāl) eṉpatum vaṭacōḻanāṭṭil puṭṭi (40 marakkāl) eṉpatum perumukatta laḷavām.
(4) nīṭṭalaḷavi : nīṭṭalaḷavi, vaḻiyaḷaviyum nilavaḷavi yum eṉa iruvakippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ nilavaḷavi kuḻik kaṇakku eṉappaṭum.
vaḻiyaḷavi varumāṟu :
8 tōri (nel)	- 	1 viral
12 viral 		- 	1 cāṇ
2 cāṇ 			- 	1 muḻam
4 muḻam 		- 	1 pākam allatu taṇṭam
2000 taṇṭam		- 	1 kurōcam 2¼ mil
4 kurōcam 		- 	1 yōcaṉi
7½ nāḻikivaḻi 	- 	1 kātam (10 mil)
kuḻiyaḷavi varumāṟu
16 cāṇ 		- 	1 kōl
18 kōl 		- 	1 kuḻi
100 kuḻi 		- 	1 mā
240 kuḻi 		- 	1 pāṭakam
20 mā 			- 	1 vēli
cey eṉṟu oru nila aḷavu caṅkakālattiliruntatu.
nilavariyit tulliyamāy vitittaṟ poruṭṭu, cōḻap peru nāṭu muḻumiyum, mutalām irācarācaṉ kālattil orumuṟi yum, mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattil oru muṟiyum, mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattil oru muṟiyum aḷakkappaṭṭatu. av vaḷaviyi naṭattiṉa atikāri ulakaḷantāṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ kiyāṇṭa aḷavukōl ulakaḷanta kōl eṉappaṭṭatu.
elli māṟumpōtellām vaḷanāṭukaḷum nāṭukaḷum aḷakkappeṟum. avaṟṟi yaḷakkuṅ kōl iṟiyiṟukkuṅ kōl eṉappeṟum, kuṭi tāṅkik kōl eṉṟum ōr aḷavu kōl muṉkālattiruntatu.
nilaṅkaḷ mika nuṭpamāka aḷakkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu “āka iṟi yili nīṅku nilam mukkālē iraṇṭu mākkāṇi arikkāṇi muntirikikkīḻ ariyē iraṇṭumā mukkāṇik kīḻ mukkālē nāṉkumā arikkāṇi muntirik kīḻ nāṉ kumāviṉāl iṟi kaṭṭiṉa kāṇikkaṭaṉ” (cō., pak.58) eṉpataṉāl viḷaṅkum. itil kuṟikkappaṭṭa nila aḷavu 1/52,428,800,000 vēli. iṉi, kālavaḷavi varumāṟu :
60 noṭi 	- 	1 vināṭi
60 vināṭi 	- 	1 nāḻiki
7½ nāḻiki 	- 	1 cāmam
8 cāmam 	- 	1 nāḷ
7 nāḷ 		- 	1 vāram
15 nāḷ 		- 	1 pakkam
2 pakkam 	- 	1 mātam
6 mātam 	- 	1 ayaṉam
2 ayaṉam 	- 	1 āṇṭu.  (pa.ta.ā.)
aḷi
aḷi - ali
aḷ = kūrmi, nīrmuḷḷi. aḷ - aḷakam = paṉṟimuḷ. aḷ - aḷi = koṭṭum muḷḷuḷḷatu, tēṉī.  (va.va. 81.)
aṟakkaṭaṉ
oruvaṉ kaṭamiyākac ceyyavēṇṭiya pala viṉikaḷuḷ, aṟamum oṉṟākum, uyartiṇiyic cārnta kāraṇattiṉālum, ulakam kaṭavuḷit talimiyākak koṇṭa oru māperuṅ kuṭumpam eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉālum, ovvoruvaṉum piṟarkku, avaruḷḷum ciṟappāka ēḻi eḷiyavarkkut taṉṉāl iyaṉṟa vari utaviyum naṉmiyum ceytal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ceytaṟkuk kaṭappāṭu eṉṟu peyar.
kimmāṟu vēṇṭā kaṭappāṭu mārimāṭ
ṭeṉṉāṟṟuṅ kollō vulaku  (kuṟaḷ - 211)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
kaṭappāṭu eṉṉum colliṉ mutaṟporuḷ kaṭaṉ eṉpatē. ovvoruvarum aṟañceyya vēṇṭuvatu kaṭaṉ eṉṉum karut tiṉāl, accollukku oppuravoḻukal (upakārañ ceytal) eṉpatu vaḻip poruḷāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu.  (col. 111.)
aṟattoṭunili
kātaliyiṉ melivu kaṇṭu, atip pōkkuvataṟku, avaḷ peṟṟōr kaṭṭuvicci (kuṟikāri) vēlaṉ (mantirakkāraṉ) mutaliyōriṉ tuṇi vēṇṭum pōtō, ataṟku muṉṉatākavō, kātali tāṉākavēṉum taṉ tōḻi vāyilākavēṉum taṉ kātalaṉippaṟṟit terivittal.  (ta.ti.6.)
aṟamum koṭiyum
aṟam: aṟaneṟiyiṟ poruḷīṭṭi apporuḷāl aṟaneṟiyil iṉpan tuykka vēṇṭum eṉpati uṇarttaṟkē, aṟanūlār immipporuḷ mūṉṟiyum aṟam poruḷ iṉpam eṉṉum immuṟippaṭuttik kūṟiṉar. ovvoruvaṉum aṟaneṟiyil taṉtaṉ toḻilic ceyvatē, avaṉavaṉ poruḷīṭṭi iṉpantuykkum vaḻiyākum. aracaṉ toḻilāvatu nāṭukāval. pōrviṉiyum nāṭukāvaluṭpaṭṭatē. ataṉāl akkālattu aracar, perumpālum, tam viṉikaḷilellām aṟattik kaṭippiṭittaṉar.
kuṟaḷarum cintarum, araṇmaṉiyil makaḷir uṟiyum uvaḷaka meykāvalarāka amarttap peṟṟaṉar. tēvāram pāṭum kuruṭar patiṉaṟuvar, tiruvāmāttūrk kōyilil tēvārappāṭakarāka amarttap peṟṟiruntaṉar. iṅṅaṉam cila eccap piṟaviyarkku vāḻkki vacati ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu.
ciṟuvar, meliyār, tūyar mutaliyōrik kollāmiyum; poruniliyaṟṟavaṉ, tōṟṟōṭupavaṉ, mutaliyōroṭu porāmiyum; aṭikkalam pukuntavaṉik kiviṭāmiyum; pōraṟaṅkaḷākap perumpālum kaṭippiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.
paṇṭit tamiḻaracar, tam nāṭṭi allatu kuṭikaḷi āḷum toḻili āṭci eṉṉuñ collāṟ kuṟiyātu, kāval puravu ōmpal mutaliya coṟkaḷālēyē kuṟikka virumpiṉar. avar kāval pūṇṭa iṭam. kuṭi niḻal eṉṟum aṭiniḻal eṉṟum, kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ kuṟṟamaṟṟa kōvalaṉik koṉṟatāl taṉ kōl kōṭiṟṟeṉṟu kaṇṭa noṭiyē uyir tuṟappāṉāyiṉ, avaṉ aṟavuṇarcci ettuṇi aḷaviṟanta tāy iruntiruttal vēṇṭum!
koṭi: akkālattaracar, aṟamnōkkiyum kali vaḷarcci paṟṟiyum ūḻiyap pārāṭṭākavum, viṉiyūkkaṟ poruṭṭum. palvēṟu koṭi nikaḻtti vantaṉar. avi, uṇṭikkoṭi, poṟkoṭi, ūrtikkoṭi, vilaṅkukkoṭi, ciṉṉak koṭi, peyarkkoṭi, nilakkoṭi, makaṟkoṭi, āṭcikkoṭi eṉa oṉpāṉ vakippaṭum.
pārppaṉarkku nāḷtōṟum uṇavaḷikkumpaṭi, āṅkāṅku kōyilkaḷilum maṭaṅkaḷilum ūṭṭuppurikaḷilum nitiyum māṉiyamum viṭṭiruntatu, uṇṭik koṭiyākum. peruñ cōṟṟutiyañcēralātaṉ pāratap pōrppaṭi kaḷiraṇṭiṟkum patiṉeṭṭunāḷum peruñcōṟu vaḻaṅkiyatum, uṇṭik koṭiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.
pulavarkkaḷitta pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa poṟkācum, pāṇark kaḷitta poṟṟāmarippūvum, pāṭiṉiyarkkum viṟaliyarkkum aḷitta poṉṉari māliyum, āṭalpāṭal araṅkēṟiya pāṇarkkum kaṇikiyarkkum aḷitta āyiratteṇkaḻañcu poṉṉum. (muṭṭil pāṇarum āṭiyaṉ makaḷirum eṭṭoṭu puṇarntavāyirampoṉ peṟupa eṉpatu aṭiyārkku nallāruri mēṟkōḷ (cilap. pak. 121) veṟṟiviḻāt toṭar pāka maṟiyōrkkaḷitta tulāpāram (cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ māṭalamaṟiyōṉukkaḷitta tulāpāram 50 tulām (375 pavuṇṭu) poṉṉākum.) eṉṉum āḷniṟip poṉṉum iraṇiya karuppam eṉṉum poṟkuṭamum, poṟkoṭi yākum.
pulavar pāṇar kūttar mutaliyōrkkaḷitta kutiri yāṉi tēr. eṉpavi ūrtikkoṭiyākum.
maṟiyōrk kavvappōtaḷitta āvāyiram (kōcakaram) vilaṅkuk koṭiyākum.
avikkaḷat talimip pulavarkkaḷitta kuṭi koṭi civiki mutaliya virutukaḷum, āṭalpāṭal araṅkēṟiyavarkkaḷitta talikkōlum, vaṇikat talivarkkaḷitta eṭṭippūvum, paṭit talivarkkaḷitta ēṉāti mōtiramum, amiccark kaḷitta kāvitip pūvum. ciṉṉakkoṭiyākum.
uḻuvittuṇṇum vēḷāḷarkkaḷitta vēḷ aracu eṉṉum paṭṭaṅkaḷum, vaṇikar talivarkkaḷitta eṭṭip paṭṭamum, āṭalpāṭal araṅkēṟiyavarkkaḷitta talikkōṟ paṭṭamum, amiccarkkaḷitta kāvitip paṭṭamum, paṭittalivark kaḷitta ēṉātip paṭṭamum,  palavaki araciyalatikārikaṭkaḷitta ariyaṉ (rāyaṉ) māvariyaṉ (mārāyaṉ) pērariyaṉ viḻuppērariyaṉ mūvēnta vēḷāṉ mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷum, ciṟṟaracarkkaḷitta piḷḷi makkaḷ nāyaṉār eṉṉum paṭṭaṅ kaḷum, peyarkkoṭiyākum. iṉi, aracar tam kuṭippeyar kaḷi amiccar paṭittalivar mutaliyōrkkum, tam ciṟappup peyarkaḷic ciṟṟaracarkkum paṭṭappeyarākavum peyaraṭi yākavum iṭuvatumuṇṭu. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ paṭit talivaṉ villavaṉkōti eṉṟum, arimarttaṉa pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ mutalamiccarāṉa māṇikkavācakar teṉṉavaṉ piramarāyaṉ eṉṟum, mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉiṉ ciṟṟaracaruḷ oruvaṉ kulōttuṅkac cōḻap pirutikaṅkaṉ eṉṟum, peyarpeṟṟiruntami kāṇka.
araciyal viṉiñar, porunar (pōr maṟavar), potu nala vūḻiyar, pulavar, kaliñar, vaṇikar, maṟiyōr, aṟaccāli, maṭam, kōyil  mutaliyōrkku aḷitta nilam nilakkoṭi yākum. atu muṟṟūṭṭu, iṟiyili, iṟi nilam, nilaviṟi ākiya nālvaki nilimiyilum; nilam ūr nāṭu eṉṉum mūvakiyaḷavilum koṭukkappaṭṭatu. oru vaki variyumillātatu muṟṟūṭṭu. atu uṟāvari, kācu koḷḷā viṟiyili eṉavum paṭum. araciṟimaṭṭum nīṅkiyatu  iṟiyili. iṟiyiṟukkum nilam iṟinilam. nilattil varum iṟiyimaṭṭum nukarvatu nilaviṟi.
araciyal viṉiñarkku, akkālattil campaḷam umpaḷam eṉ ṉum iruvakiyil vēlippayaṉ aḷikkappaṭṭatu. nellāka vēṉum kācākavēṉum koṭuppatu campaḷam; nilamākak koṭuppatu umpaḷam. umpaḷam māṉiyam eṉappaṭum. atu niliyāṉatu; perumpālum iṟiyiliyāyiruppatu.
māṉiyavaki: araciyal viṉiñaruḷ, ūrttalivaṉukkuk koṭuppatu ampalamāṉiyam; ūrkkaṇakkaṉukkuk koṭuppatu kaṇakkakkāṇi; veṭṭiyāṉukkuk koṭuppatu veṭṭippēṟu.
araciyal viṉiñaruḷ orucārārāṉa porunaruḷ. paṭit talivaṉukkuk koṭuppatu amaranāyakam; pōriṟpaṭṭavaṉ makaṉukku allatu maṉivikkuk koṭuppatu irattakkāṇi (irattak kāṇikki, irattamāṉiyam, utirappaṭṭi).
potunalavūḻiyaruḷ, kuṭimakkaṭkuk koṭuppatu kuṭimakkaḷ māṉiyam; paṟiyaṭippavaṉukkuk koṭuppatu paṟittuṭivi; uvaccaṉukkuk koṭuppatu uvaccakkāṇi; maruttuvaṉukkuk koṭuppatu maruttuvappēṟu; naccumaruttuvaṉukkuk koṭuppatu viṭakara (viṣa hara) pōkam; ēriyuṭippi aṭittavaṉukkuk koṭuppatu ērippaṭṭi. iviyellāvaṟṟiṟkum potuppeyar ūḻiyamāṉiyam eṉpatu. ivaṟṟuṭ pala, ūraviyārāṟ koṭukkappaṭṭaviyēṉum, aracavoppam peṟṟavi.
vaṇikaruṭ ciṟanta toṇṭu ceytavaṉukkuk koṭuppatu eṭṭip puravu.
pulavarkkuk koṭuppatu pulavarmuṟṟūṭṭu.
kaliñaruḷ, naṭṭuvaṉukkuk koṭuppatu naṭṭuvanilikkāṇi; kaṇiyaṉukkuk koṭuppatu kaṇimuṟṟūṭṭu.
maṟiyōr eṉṉum pirāmaṇarukkuk koṭuppatu piramakāyam (piramatēyam). atu tokutippaṭṭavarukkuk koṭuppatum taṉippaṭṭavarkkuk koṭuppatum eṉa iruvaki. akarappaṟṟu, akkiracālippuṟam, akkirakāra vāṭi eṉpaṉa tokutip paṭṭavarkkuriyaṉa. pārppaṉar maṭṭum kuṭiyirukkum iṟiyili nilam akarappaṟṟu. pārppaṉar perumpāṉmiyākavum piṟar ciṟupāṉmiyākavum kuṭiyirukkum iṟiyilinilam akkirakāra vāṭi. pārppaṉari uṇpikkum akkiracālikku viṭappaṭṭa māṉiyam akkiracālippuṟam. vētavirutti, paṭṭa virutti, attiyayaṉavirutti (attiyayaṉāṅkam), tayittiriyak kiṭippuṟam, pāratappuṟam mutaliyaṉa taṉippaṭṭavarkkuriyaṉa.
cattiram, aṭiciṟcāli (ūṭṭuppuri, cōṟṟaṭippu, aṉṉacattiram) mutaliya vaṟṟiṟku viṭum māṉiyam cālippuṟam (cālāpōkam), aṭiciṟcālikku maṭṭum viṭappaṭuvatu aṭiciṟpuṟam. taṇṇīrppantalukku viṭappaṭuvatu taṇṇīrp paṭṭi.
maṭattiṟku viṭappaṭuvatu maṭappuṟam.
kōyiṟku viṭappaṭuvatu tēvatāṉam allatu tēvatāṉappaṟṟu (tēvatāyam, tirunāmattukkāṇi, tiruviṭi yāṭṭam, tiru viṭippaṟṟu). kuṭiyirukkum makkaḷoṭu cērttuk kōyiṟ kaḷikkappaṭum nilam kuṭi nīṅkāt tiruviṭiyāṭṭam eṉap paṭum. piṭārikōyiṟku viṭappaṭum māṉiyam piṭāripaṭṭi allatu piṭāriviḷākam eṉṟum, camaṇaputtap paḷḷikaṭku viṭappaṭuvatu paḷḷiccantam eṉṟum, peyarpeṟum.
aṟamnōkkiyum matam paṟṟiyum viṭappaṭum māṉiyamellām potuvāka aṟappuṟam eṉappeṟum.
pārppaṉarkkum kōyiṟkum oru muḻuvūrittāṉañ ceyyum pōtu, oru peṇyāṉiyi avvūriccuṟṟip pōkaviṭṭu, atupōṉavaḻiyē elliyamittuk kalluṅkaḷḷiyum naṭṭi, nīraṭṭik koṭuttu, perum paṇikkāraṉ eṉṉum atikāri vāyilāy, ceppēṭṭil aracāṇi (āṇatti) poṟippatu marapu. peṇ yāṉiyiviṭṭu elliyamittal piṭicūḻntu paṭāki naṭattal eṉappaṭum. tāṉañ ceyyappaṭum ūriluḷḷa uḻutuṇpār kārāṇmi eṉṉuñ collālum, uḻuvit tuṇpār mīyāṭci eṉṉuñ collālum, kuṟikkap peṟuvar. iṉi, ōr ūri mēṟpārkkuṅ kūṭṭamum mīyāḷuṅ kaṇam eṉappaṭum. nīraṭṭik koṭutta ceppēṭṭut tāṉa mellām aṭṭippēṟu eṉap paṭṭaṉa.
kōyiṟku viṭappaṭum nilamellām iṟiyiliyāka irukka vēṇṭiyi runtamiyiṉ, aracaṉ tāṉāyc ceyyāta tēvatāṉa mellām avaṉuṭiya icivupeṟṟē ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aṅṅaṉañ ceyyu mpōtu, iṟi nīṅkalāl araciyaṟku ēṟpaṭum iḻappiṟku īṭāka, tāṉañ ceypavaṉ allatu ceypavar oru kuṟitta tokiyip paṇṭārattiṟ cērkkumpaṭi ūravi yāriṭam koṭuttu viṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
oru nilam allatu ūr iṟiyiliyākkappaṭum pōtu, ataṉ iṟi nīkkam iṟaṅkal eṉṟum, ati iṟiyilip pativup puttakattil peyartteḻututal nīṅkal allatu nāṭṭu nīṅkal eṉṟum, kuṟikkap peṟum. varikkaṇakkiyum nilaṅkaḷiṉ nili miyiyum entac camayattilum terintukoḷḷaṟ poruṭṭu, palvēṟu kaṇakkukaḷ vikkappaṭṭiruntamiyiṉ, cila camayaṅ kaḷil ōr iṟiyili āṇikku aimpatiṉmarukku mēṟpaṭṭa atikārikaḷ kinnāṭṭiṭa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. iṟutiyil aracaṉ iṭum muṭivāṉa āṇi kaṭiyīṭu eṉap paṭṭatu.
nāṭṭuk kuḻappattiṉāl iḻakkappaṭṭa māṉiyavurimiyi, kuḻappam nīṅkiyapiṉ cāṉṟukāṭṭi mīṇṭum peṟṟukkoḷḷa akkālattu iyalki (cāttiyam) iruntatu. kaḷappāḷar āṭciyil iḻakkappaṭṭa vēḷvikkuṭip piramatēya vurimi, iru nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup  piṉ mīḷa nāṭṭappeṟṟa pāṇṭiya vāṭciyiṉ ēḻām talimuṟiyil putuppikkappeṟṟatu.
cēramāṉ kaṭalōṭṭiya vēlkeḻukuṭṭuvaṉ, paraṇarkkut taṉ makaṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ cēralikkoṭuttatu makaṟkoṭi yākum. makaṟkoṭiyāvatu makaṉi māṇavaṉākak koṭuttal.
takaṭūreṟinta peruñ cēralirumpoṟi, aricil kiḻārkkut taṉ ariyaṇiyik koṭuttatu āṭcik koṭiyākum. āyiṉ, avar ati ēṟkamaṟuttu avaṉiṭam amiccup pūṇṭār.
koṭi maṭam : paṇṭiyaracaruṭ palarkkuk koṭi oru ciṟanta kuṇamā yiruntapōtē, oruvakiyiṟ kuṟṟamākavum iruntatu. atu koṭi maṭam eṉappaṭṭatu.
pēkaṉ ōr aruviṉi yuyarvilik kaliṅkatti oru mayilmēṟ pōrttāṉ. pāri taṉ iḻiyaṇi neṭuntēri oru mullik koṭi paṭara niṟuttiyatoṭu, taṉakkirunta munnūṟūrkaḷiyum paricilark kīntuviṭṭāṉ. imayavarampaṉ neṭuñcēralātaṉ, taṉmēṟ pattuppāṭṭup pāṭiya kumaṭṭūrk kaṇṇaṉārkku, ainnūṟūr piramatāyamum muppatteṭṭiyāṇṭu teṉṉāṭṭuḷ varuvataṉiṟ pākamuṅ koṭuttāṉ. kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇinār muṭiccēral, kāppiyāṟṟuk kāppiyaṉārkku, pattup pāṭṭiṟku nāṟpatilakkam poṉṉum tāṉ āṇṭatiṟ pākamuṅ koṭut tāṉ. iṅṅaṉam, aḵṟiṇiyuyirikaṭkut takāta poruḷ kaḷiyum, aracu keṭumpaṭi paricilarkku nāṭu muḻuvatiyum, pala pulavar paciyāl vāṭa ōriru pulavarkku māperuñ celvat tiyum, aḷittatu koṭi maṭamām.  (pa.ta.ā.)
aṟiva (citta) matam
aṟivaṉ tēyam eṉṟu tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatāl. uruva vaṇakkatti (Idolatry) yoḻitta uyarnta aṟivamatamum paṇṭit tamiḻakattiliruntami yuṇarappaṭum. piṟkālat tilirunta patiṉeṇṇaṟivarum tamiḻarē. kaṭavuḷ eṉṟa peyar ellāvaṟṟiyuṅ kaṭanta muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷik kuṟittal kāṇka.
aṟivu
aṟivu eṉṉum col aṟital (Perception, understanding) aṟinta ceyti (knowledge), ōti (Wisdom) mati (Intelligence) eṉṉum nāṟporuḷ uṇarttum. - ti.ma.250.
aṟutoḻilōr
āpayaṉ kuṉṟum aṟutoḻilōr nūlmaṟappar
kāvalaṉ kālāṉ eṉiṉ.  (kuṟaḷ. 560)
ik kuṟaḷurikku aṭimaṇiyāyiruppatu aṟutoḻilōr eṉṉuñ col. ac coṟkup parimēlaḻakar antaṇar eṉṉuñ collāṟ pirāmaṇar eṉṉum poruḷ kuṟittu. aṟutoḻi lāvaṉa ōtal, ōtuvittal, vēṭṭal, vēṭpittal, ītal, ēṟṟaleṉa ivi. pacukkaḷ pāl kuṉṟiyavaḻi aviyiṉmiyāṉum. atu koṭuttaṟkuriyār mantiraṅ kaṟpameṉpaṉa ōtāmiyāṉum, vēḷvi naṭavātām. ākavē, vāṉam peyalollā teṉpatā yiṟṟu eṉṟu ataṟkēṟpa viḷakkavuriyuṅ kūṟiṉār. ivvuri tavaṟāṉateṉpataṟkuk karaṇiyaṅkaḷāvaṉa:
1. nāṟpālārkkum aṟutoḻiluṇmi
antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷareṉṉum nālvakuppārk kum aṟutoḻil kūṟappaṭuvatāl, avaruḷ oru vakuppāri maṭṭum aṟutoḻilōr eṉṟu kaṭṭuvatu poruntātu.
ōta lōtuvittal vēṭṭal vēṭpittal
ītal ēṟṟal eṉṟiru mūvaki
ātik kālat tantaṇa raṟutoḻil
araca raṟutoḻil ōtal vēṭṭal
puritīr perumpār purattal ītal
kariyaṟu paṭikkalaṅ kaṟṟal vicayam
vaṇika raṟutoḻil ōtal vēṭṭal
ītal uḻavu pacukkāval vāṇikam
vēḷāḷa raṟutoḻil uḻavupacuk kāval
teḷḷitiṉ vāṇikam kuyiluvam kārukaviṉi
oḷḷiya irupiṟap pāḷark kēvalceyal
kuyiluvam vāttiyaṅ koṭṭuta lākum
paruttinūl paṭṭunūl amittāṭi yākkalum
cumattalum piṟavum kāruka viṉittoḻil.
eṉṟu tivākaramum.
ōtalē yōtuvitta luṭaṉvēṭṭal vēṭpittiṭal
ītalē yēṟṟalāṟum ēṟkuman taṇartoḻiṟpēr
ōtalē vēṭṭalītal ulakōmpal paṭipa yiṟṟal
mētaku pōrcey tīṭṭal vēntarcey toḻilka ḷāṟē
paṭikuṭi kūḻamiccup paṟṟiya naṭpiṉōṭu
neṭumati laraciyaṟku nikaḻttiyavāṟu pētam!
iṭiyaṟa vōtal vēṭṭal vēḷāṇmi vāṇikatti
ṉuṭaṉiri kātta lēri yuḻalāṟum vaciyark kāmē
vaciyartan toḻilka ḷāṟuḷ vakuttamut toḻilka ḷāṉa
pacuvōmpal poruḷi yīṭṭal payiriṭal purāṇa māti
vacitavi raṉukū lammām vakittoḻil cūttirarkkām
eṉṟu cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭum. nālvakuppārkkum avvāṟu toḻil kūṟutal kāṇka.
2. parimēlaḻakar aracarkkum aṟutoḻil kūṟal
aṟaṉiḻukkā tallavi nīkki maṟaṉiḻukkā
māṉa muṭiya taracu  (384)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaṭku.
taṉakkōtiya aṟattiṉ vaḻuvātoḻuki, aṟaṉallavi taṉṉāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇum nikaḻāmaṟ kaṭintu, vīrattiṉ vaḻuvāta tāḻviṉmiyiṉi yuṭiyāṉ aracaṉ eṉṟu toṭaruri varintu, ataṉ viḷakkattil, avvaṟamāvatu ōtal, vēṭṭal, ītaleṉṉum potut toḻiliṉum, paṭikkalam payiṟal, palluyirōmpal, pakittiṟan teṟutaleṉṉuñ ciṟapput toḻiliṉum, vaḻuvātu niṟṟal eṉṟu parimēlaḻakarē aracarkkum aṟutoḻil kūṟiyiruttalāl, aṟutoḻilōr eṉṉum potup peyarkkup pirāmaṇar eṉṉum poruḷum. nūlōtutal aracarkkum uriyatātalāl nūl maṟattal eṉpataṟku vēta mōtāmi eṉṉum poruḷum, poruntāmi yuṇarka.
3. tolkāppiyar aracaroḻinta mūvakuppārkku aṟutoḻil  kūṟal 
aṟuvakip paṭṭa pārppaṉap pakkamum
aivaki marapiṉ aracar pakkamum
irumūṉṟu marapiṉ ēṉōr pakkamum  (tol poruḷ puṟat. 20)
eṉṟu, tolkāppiyar antaṇar (pirāmaṇar), vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉṉum mūvakuppārkku aṟutoḻil kūṟiyiruppa tāl, avar  karuttuppaṭiyum aṟutoḻilōr eṉpatu avaruḷ oru vakuppāri maṭṭuñ ciṟappākac cuṭṭalākāmi aṟika. āriya muṟippaṭi nūlōtalurimi vaṇikarkku muṇmiyi nōkkuka.
4. aracarkku aṟutoḻiluṇmi
kōkkaḷik kāttalum māpporu ḷīṭṭalum
ērttoḻil mūṉṟum viciyartan toḻilē
eṉṟu vaṇikarkku muttoḻil kūṟum piṅkalamum.
ōtal porutal ulaku purattal
ītal vēṭṭal paṭipayiṟa laṟutoḻil
eṉṟu aracarkku aṟutoḻil kūṟukiṉṟatu.
vēṭṭal eṉpatu, ēmāṟṟu vakiyil mūvēntariṭip piṟ kālattup pukuttappaṭṭa āriya vaḻakkamātalāl, ataṟkup pakaramākat tamiḻaracarkkuriya toḻilākak koḷḷappaṭuvatu vēṭṭiyāṭalē.
avicorin tāyiram vēṭṭaliṉ oṉṟaṉ
uyircekut tuṇṇāmi naṉṟu  (kuṟaḷ. 259)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvaruṅ kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
iṉi, iṟi (vari) kōṭal eṉpatu aracarkku iṉṟiyamiyāta tātaliṉ, atiyuñ cērppiṉ eḻutoḻilumākum. muṟi ceytal allatu taṇṭañceytal ulaku purattaluḷ aṭaṅkum; āyiṉ, porutal aṭaṅkātu. paḻaviṟal tāyattik kāttuk koḷvataṟku maṭṭumaṉṟip putuviṟal tāyatti uṇṭākkik kōṭaṟkum, pōr iṉṟiyamiyātateṉpatu paṇṭik koḷki.
5. pirāmaṇarkku aṟutoḻiliṉmi
tolkāppiyamum ellā nikaṇṭukaḷum pirāmaṇarkku aṟutoḻil kūṟiyiruppiṉum. aṟuvakippaṭṭa pārppaṉap pakkam eṉṉun tolkāppiyat toṭarukku, āṟu kūṟṟiṉuṭ paṭṭa pārppiyaṟ kūṟu ... avi ōtal ōtuvittal vēṭṭal vēṭpittal koṭuttal kōṭal eṉa āṟām eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urittiruppiṉum, uṇmiyiṟ pirāmaṇark kuriyavi ītaloḻinta aintoḻilē. iti, ōtal mutaliya ēṉi aintoḻiṟkum ciṟappuc ceyyuḷkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭiya nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. ītalukkum ītaṟciṟappiṟkum maṭṭum.
ilaṉeṉṟu mevva muriyāmi yītal
kulaṉuṭiyāṉ kaṇṇē yuḷa  (kuṟaḷ. 223)
īttuvakku miṉpa maṟiyārkol tāmuṭimi
vittiḻakkum vaṉka ṇavar  (kuṟaḷ 228)
eṉṟu potuc ceyyuḷkaḷiyē eṭuttukkāṭṭiyirup patiṉiṉ ṟum,  viruntōmpalum vēḷāṇmiyumillāp pirāmaṇar il vāḻkkiyiṉiṉṟum. muṭṭipukum pārppār eṉṉum kampar pāṭṭiṉiṉ ṟum. aṟintukoḷka.
ōtal vēṭṭa lavipiṟarc ceytal
īta lēṟṟaleṉ ṟāṟupurin toḻukum
aṟampuri yantaṇar vaḻimoḻin toḻuki  (patiṟṟup 24)
eṉpatu, āriya aṭimiyāṉa palyāṉic celkeḻu kuṭṭu   vaṉip pālik kutamaṉ eṉṉum pirāmaṇap pulavaṉ pāṭiyatātalāl. aḷavic cāṉṟākātu. vētamōtuvitta liyē ītaleṉṟu kūṟiṉ atu ōtuvittaleṉṉum oṉṟēyaṉṟi vēṟākāteṉak kūṟiviṭukka.
ākavē, pirāmaṇarkkuriya toḻil aintē yeṉpatu teritaru tēṟṟamām. aracarkku aintum pirāmaṇarkku āṟumākat tol kāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatu. ulakiyaṟku māṟāṉa iṉmi naviṟciyē eṉa aṟika.
6. antaṇar vēṟu, pirāmaṇar vēṟu
antaṇar eṉpavar, irutiṇi yuyirkaḷiṭattum aruḷ kūrntu. tuṟavu mēṟkoṇṭu aimpulaṉaṭakki. iṉamoḻi kulamata iṭa vēṟupāṭiṉṟi ulakap potuvāy uḷḷan tūyarāya uyarnili iṟivaṉaṭiyār.
antaṇar eṉpōr aṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkum
centaṇmi pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ  (kuṟaḷ 30)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. antaṇariṉ piṟaviyalpukaḷit tirukkuṟaṭ pāyira, nīttār perumi yatikārattiṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
pirāmaṇarō, māṇava (piramacariya) nilikkup piṉ il vāḻkki (kirukattam) kāṭuṟivu (vāṉappirattam) eṉṉum irunilikaḷilum iyaṉṟavari iṉpantuyttu, aimpoṟiyun tāmē aṭaṅkiya piṉ tuṟavuk kōlam (canniyācam) pūṇṭu. kulaveṟiyum moḻi veṟiyum nīṅkātu. āṇavamum tannalamum mikka āriya iṉattār.
itaṉāl, tiruvaḷḷuvar kuṟitta antaṇar vēṟu; parimēlaḻakar kuṟitta pirāmaṇar vēṟu; eṉa aṟintu koḷka.
7. nikaṇṭukaḷ āriyamuṟi taḻuviyaṉa
tamiḻ nikaṇṭukaḷellām tamiḻakattil āriyam vērūṉṟiyapiṉ tōṉṟi yaviyātalāl, piṟaviyoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa kulavoḻukkam  paṟṟi avi kūṟupaviyellām āriyac cārpāṉavēyākum. uriccol eṉṉum peyar māṟi nikaṇṭu eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām.
iyaṟṟamiḻp poruḷilakkaṇam. kalvi kāval vaṇikam uḻavu eṉṉum nāṟperun toḻilpaṟṟi akapporuṭ ṭalivari nāl vakuppārāka vakuttatē, antaṇar. aracar. vaṇikar. vēḷāḷar eṉṉum tamiḻp pākupāṭām. illaṟattārāṉa tamiḻp pārppārum, akkālattil aruḷāḷarāy antaṇar eṉṉum peyarukkut takutiyuṭiyārāyiruntaṉar.
ki.mu.7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa tolkāppiyar, tam kālatti lēyē āriyam vērūṉṟiyatik kaṇṭataṉāl, tuṟaviyar pōl vīṭu vācaliṉṟi aṅkumiṅkum alintu tirintu koṇṭirunta pirāmaṇariyum antaṇarākak koṇṭu.
nūlē karakam mukkōl maṇiyē
āyuṅ kāli antaṇark kuriya  (tol. poruḷ mara. 71)
eṉṟu kūṟiṉārāyiṉum, vaṇikarkku vāṇikamum vēḷāḷarkku uḻavumē taṉicciṟapput toḻil eṉṉum uṇmiyi,
vicikaṉ peṟumē vāṇika vāḻkki  (tol. poruḷ mara. 79)
vēḷāṇ māntark kuḻutū ṇallatu
illeṉa moḻiya piṟavaki nikaḻcci   (tol. poruḷ mara. 82)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷāṟ kuṟittār.
iṅṅaṉamiruntum, nikaṇṭukaḷ, antaṇar aracar mutaliya nāl vakuppāṉa tamiḻp pākupāṭṭi, āriyar (pirāmaṇar), pirāmaṇar cattiriyar viciyar cūttirar eṉat tannalattiṟkēṟpa muṟiyē paṭimuṟit tāḻvuṟṟa niliyāṉa nāṟperum piṟavik kulap pākupāṭākat tirittu. pirāmaṇarkku āriya maṟiyāṉa vētamōtu talum vēḷvi ceyvittalum, cattiriyarkkup pōr puritalum vēḷvi ceytalum, viciyarkku vāṇikamum uḻavum ceytalum, cūttirarkkuk kittoḻilum mēl mūvakup pārkkum ēval ceytalum, taṉicciṟapput toḻileṉa vakutta muṟiyip perumpālum piṉpaṟṟiyē, āriyarallātavarum vētamum vēḷviyum vēṇṭātavarumāṉa tamiḻ nālvakup pārkkum avvāṟu toḻil vakuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷami, tamiḻ marapiṟkum tamiḻar uyarvukkum eḷḷaḷavum ēṟkāta iḻi niliyum kaṭippaṭṭa aṭimittaṉamumē kāṭṭum.
8. āriyatti etirppatē tirukkuṟaḷ nōkkam
tiruvaḷḷuvar aṟamporuḷiṉpam eṉṉum muppālpaṟṟi iyaṟṟiyaruḷiya aṟanūl viyakam muḻuvataṟkum payaṉpaṭum vāyuṟi vāḻttē yāyiṉum, tamiḻari āriya aṭimittaṉat tiṉiṉṟu viṭuvippatē ataṉ aṭippaṭi nōkkameṉpatu.
antaṇa reṉpōr aṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkuñ
centaṇmi pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ. (30)
aṟattāṟṟiṉ ilvāḻkki yāṟṟiṉ puṟattāṟṟiṟ
pōoyp peṟuva tevaṉ.  (46)
oḻukka muṭimi kuṭimi yiḻukkam
iḻinta piṟappāy viṭum.  (133)
maṟappiṉu mōttuk koḷavākum pārppāṉ
piṟappoḻukkaṅ kuṉṟak keṭum.  (144)
avicorin tāyiram vēṭṭaliṉ oṉṟaṉ
uyircekut tuṇṇāmi naṉṟu.  (259)
īṉṟāḷ pacikāṇpā ṉāyiṉum ceyyaṟka
cāṉṟōr paḻikkum viṉi.  (666)
piṟappokku mellā vuyirkkuñ ciṟappovvā
ceytoḻil vēṟṟumi yāṉ.  (972)
cuḻaṉṟumērp piṉṉa tulaka mataṉāl
uḻantu muḻavē tali.  (1031)
uḻuvā rulakattārk kāṇiyaḵ tāṟṟātu
eḻuvāri yellām poṟuttu.  (1035)
iravā rirappārkkoṉ ṟīvar karavātu
kiceytūṇ māli yavar.  (1033)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷkaḷālum, ivi pōṉṟa piṟavaṟṟālum aṟiyap paṭum. iṅṅaṉam āriyattik kuṟippākavum veḷippaṭi yākavuṅ kaṇṭippavarum.
tanti makaṟkāṟṟu naṉṟi yaviyattu
munti yiruppac ceyal  (67)
eṉṟu kalviyi ellā makkaṭkum potuvākak kūṟiyavarum.
vilaṅkoṭu makka ḷaṉiyar ilaṅkunūl
kaṟṟārō ṭēṉi yavar  (410)
eṉṟu vētattik kuṟiyātu vēṟu viḻumiya nūlkaḷiyē kuṟitta varumāṉa tiruvaḷḷuvar. pirāmaṇari orupōtum pōṟṟā reṉat  teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
iṅṅaṉam avar tamiḻarkku viḻippuṇartti īrāyiram āṇ ṭiṟku mēlākiyum, iṉṟum avar karuttaṟiyātu. parimēlaḻakar uriyi maṟukkap pāril evaruḷḷār eṉṟu tarukkum tamiḻp pulavarum  uḷar. avar pētik kurittālun tōṉṟā tuṇarvu eṉṉum paḻamoḻik kūṟṟi (93) viḷakkap piṟantavarē yāvar.
iṉi, aṟutoḻilōr yāreṉiṉ, 
uḻavu toḻilē varivu vāṇikam
vicci kaṟpam eṉṟit tiṟattaṟu
toḻil kaṟpa naṭiyatu karumapūmi
eṉṉum tivākara nūṟpāviṟ kuṟitta aṟutoḻilik kaṟṟavarē yeṉka.  - centamiḻc celvi - 1970
aṟippaṭuttal
aṟippaṭuttal eṉiṉum aṟipōkkutal eṉiṉum kīḻaṟuttal eṉiṉum oṉṟē. kiyūṭṭāl kāṭṭik koṭukkac ceytal eṉpatu ivaṟṟiṉ poruḷ.  (kuṟaḷ 747.)
aṉṉam
OE Ganra, OHG ganazzo, RV hamsa - aṉṉam, ekiṉam ōtimam eṉpaṉavē teṉ coṟkaḷ. mēliyāriyac coṟkaḷ muṟiyē peruvāttiyum ataṟkiṉamāṉa oru kaṭaṟpaṟaviyiyum kuṟippaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ tiripāṉa hamsa eṉṉum kīḻiyāriyac collē, ōtimattiṉ peyarāka vaḻaṅkivaruvatākat terikiṉṟatu. Swan eṉpatu āṅkilac col.  (ti.ma.piṉ.)
aṉal
aṉal - aṉala
aṉ - aṉṟu, aṉṟutal = ciṉattal. aṉṟu - kaṉṟu.
kaṉṟutal = ciṉattal, veyilāṟ karukutal.
aṉ - aṉal - kaṉal = neruppu, kaṉali = katiravaṉ.
aṉal = tī, veppam, iṭi. aṉalutal = aḻalutal.
aṉali = neruppu, katiravaṉ.
aḻal, aṉṟu. aṉal eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ mūlam al eṉpatu uyttuṇarappaṭum. atu vaḻakkaṟṟatu. atu el eṉṉum colloṭu toṭarpuḷḷatu. el = katiravaṉ, veyil, oḷi, pakal.
vaṭamoḻiyil aṉ eṉpati mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar. ataṟku uyirttal, iyaṅkutal eṉṉum poruḷē uṇṭu. - va.va.81.

ākkam
ākuvatu ākkam. ākutal - mēṉmēl uyartal.  (ti.ma.55.)
ākkam taruvaṉa
celvam, uṭalnalam, aṟam, pukaḻ, aṟivu, naṭpu mutaliyaṉa.  (kuṟaḷ 831)
ākamam
vētappirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vantavuṭaṉ, matakkoṇ muṭipu, kōyilamippu, kōyil vaḻipāṭu, mantira moḻikaḷ mutaliyaṉapaṟṟiya nūlkaḷi amittuk koṇṭaṉar. avi civa neṟi paṟṟiyavāyiṉ ākamam eṉṟum tirumālneṟi paṟṟiyavāyiṉ samhiti yeṉṟum, cāktampaṟṟiyavāyiṉ tantram eṉṟum, peyar peṟum. ākama = vantatu, tōṉṟiyatu, vaḻivanta nūl.
palvaki nūlkaḷ
pāṇiṉīyam (aṣṭātyāyī) iyaṟṟap peṟṟatu. ki.mu. 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu.
artta cātiram cāṇakkiyarāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟatu. ki.mu. 3 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu.
piṟkālat tarma cātiraṅkaḷ
manutarma cātiram (ki.pi.200); yāñyavalkiya miruti (ki.pi.400), nārata miruti (ki.pi.500).  (va.va.)
ākavam - āhava (i.vē.)
akavutal = aḻittal, pōrukkaḻittal, aṟikūval.
akavu - akavam - ākavam = pōr.
vaṭavar ā.hvē eṉṟu mūlaṅkāṭṭuvar. hvē eṉpatu akavu eṉpataṉ tiripu.
ā eṉpatu poruḷaṟṟa muṉṉoṭṭu.  (va.va.82.)
ākārac cuṭṭu
muntiyal tamiḻar cēymiyik kuṟittaṟku vāyi akalat tiṟanta pōtu, atu ākāra volikkē ēṟṟatāyiruntatiṉāl, av voliyic cēymic cuṭṭākkiṉar.

antā, antō.
cēymik karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟakkūṭiya piṟa karuttukkaḷ nīkkam maṟivu mutaliya oru cilavē yātalālum, aviyellām ūkārac cuṭṭuk karuttukkaḷuḷ ovvoru vakiyil aṭaṅki viṭṭamiyālum, ākārac cuṭṭiṉiṉṟu cēymi yoḻinta vēṟek karuttum paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyila.  (mu.tā.)
ākāraccuṭṭuk karuttu
ākārac cuṭṭu, oru ticiyiyuñ ciṟappāyc cuṭṭātu cēymiyi maṭṭuṅ kuṟittalāṉum, orucāra cēymi kaṇmuṉ tōṉṟum aṇmikkappāṟ paṭṭatātalāṉum, cēymiyiṉ toṭarcciyellām cēymiyēyātalāṉum, cēymik karuttiṉiṉṟu vēṟoru karuttun tōṉṟutaṟkiṉṟu. (mu.tā.)
ākāraccuṭṭu vērccoṟkaḷ
(1) cēymiccuṭṭu (Remoteness) ā = anta (atu, avi).
āṅku - aṅku Cf. A.S. thar, E. there, Sans. tatra, āṅkar > (yāṅkar) > (nāṅkar) > ñāṅkar, āṇṭu, Cf. E. yon. yond, yonder, A.S. geond, Ger. jener, that, root ya.
ātu - atu, atā, atō, atōḷi, atu - ataṉ (te.).
am: antu, anta, antā, o.nō. um > untu.
	ammi = cēymiyiṟ ceṉṟuviṭṭa muṟpiṟappu.
ā - av: (avaḷ), avaṇ, avaḷ = avviṭam.
	āṉ > avaṉ; ām > avam.
	avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, ava - avi.
al:	al + tu = aḵtu, o.nō. pal + tu = paḵtu.
	al + tu = aṉṟu. o.nō. nal + tu = naṉṟu.
lakaravīṟṟuccuṭṭu viṉāvaṭikaḷum uṇṭu eṉpati ala ēlā eṉṉum teluṅkuc coṟkaḷālum alli illi elli eṉṉum karunaṭac coṟkaḷālum uṇarka. Cf. L. ille, he. Ar. al. the.
āṉ - aṉ : aṉṉa, aṉṉā (cuṭṭu); aṉṉa (such) - cuṭṭāc cuṭṭu. aṉṉa (uvamiyurupu), aṉṉatu, aṟṟu.
aṉi (cuṭṭu), aṉi + tu = aṉittu, aṉi, aṉiya (uvami.) 
akam = avvulakam, vīṭu (heaven, bliss), vīṭu pōṉṟa taṅkiṭam, vīṭṭi nukarum uḷḷam, uḷḷiṭam, iṭam.
aruḷillārk kavvulaka milli poruḷillārk
kivvulaka millāki yāṅku.  (kuṟaḷ. 247)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil, avvulakam ivvulakam eṉpaṉa muṟiyē cēymiyum aṇmiyum paṟṟi vīṭṭiyum ñālattiyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka . ēcumīṭpar tam māṇavari nōkkip pēriṉpa vīṭu uṅkaḷ uḷḷattilirukkiṟatu eṉṟār. pēriṉpa vulakiṟkum ivvulaka iruppiṭattiṟkum vīṭu eṉpatu potup peyarā yiruttali nōkkuka.
uṭampiṉukkuḷḷē uṟuporuḷ kaṇṭēṉ
uṭampuḷē uttamaṉ kōyilkoṇṭāṉ
eṉṟār tirumūlar.
appāl > Gk. apo; L. ap. ab, abs, a; Sans. apa; Ger. ab; E. ab, abs, a, off, of, away.
appōtu eṉṉuñ collē appa eṉṟu tiritalāl, appāl eṉpatu appa eṉṟu tirivatu viyappaṉṟu.
(2) paṭarkkip peyar : paṭarkkic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ cēymip poruḷkaḷik kuṟippaṉavātaliṉ, cēymiccuṭṭiṉiṉṟu paṭarkkip peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. āṉ - tāṉ (orumi) = avaṉ, avaḷ, atu. ām - tām (paṉmi) = avar avi.
Cf. Sans. tat; A.S. thoet; E. that; Ger. das, dasz; Gk. te; (sl.) A.S.thas;
E. those (pl.)
tāṉ eṉṉum peyarē taṉ eṉṟu kuṟukip piṉpu āriya moḻikaḷil takaravīṟāyt tirintateṉka.
oppu nōkka : tirumāṉ > srīmat; nuṉi - nuti - tuti. ta - ca pōli. e-ṭu: mātam - mācam; pittaṉ - piccaṉ.
āṅkilattiluḷḷa than eṉṉum oppīṭṭiṭiccol, taṉṉiṉ eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ tiripāyirukkalām. taṉṉiṉ = ataṉiṉ.
kipporuḷ taṉṉiṉ meypporuḷ kalvi eṉpati nōkkuka.
A.S. thanne; Ger. dann; E.than; from stem of The.
akkari eṉṉum col oru nīrniliyiṉ maṟupakkatti allatu etirp pakkattik kāṭṭuvatu pōl, appāl appuṟam eṉṉum coṟkaḷum oru poruḷiṉ maṟu allatu piṉpuṟattikkāṭṭi, ataṉpiṉ kālappiṉmi yuṇarttum iṭic coṟkaḷāyiṉa.
akal = cēymiyiṟ cel, nīṅku, viri.
o.nō. paṭar = cel, viri. akal - āl - ālam (maram).
(3) micimikkaruttu : cēymi oru vakiyil micimi pōlutalālum, mēṭṭiṉaṭiyil niṟkumpōtu cēymi micimiyāyiruttalālum, cēymic cuṭṭil micimik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
aṇ = mēl; aṇṇam = mēlvāy;
aṇṇal = mēlāṉavaṉ, aracaṉ; aṇṇaṉ = mūttōṉ;
aṇṇāvi = āciriyaṉ; aṇṇi = aṇṇaṉ maṉivi;
aṇṇā = mēl nōkku; aṇar = mēṉōkkiccel.
Gk. ana, an, up; Goth, ana; E, on.
(4) celavukkaruttum piṟakaruttum: al - cēymippaṭu, aci, cel, varuntu, curuṅku, taṅku, nīṅku, muṭivaṭi, viri: o.nō. ē - iya - iyaṅku = aci, cel. cellal = tuṉpam.
oruvar cellutavāvatu cēymippaṭutal; celvatiṉāl uṭampiṟku acivum ataṉāl varuttamum piṟakkum; cellac cella uṭampu curuṅkittōṉṟum; irāvāṉavuṭaṉ ōriṭattil taṅkanērum; cellutal ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu nīṅkutalākum; nīkkamē muṭivu; oru poruḷiṉ viḷimpu nīṅkuvatu virital.
allāṭu = ali, varuntu; allā = varuttam; allal = varuttam, tuṉpam; alattal = varuntal; alakkaḻi = varuttu; alam = tuṉpam; alakkaṇ = tuṉpam; alamvā = alamā; alamarutal = varuntutal; alavu = varuntu; al =- alu; aluttal = acital, kaḷittal; alu + al = aluval = varuntic ceyyum vēli, vēli; alu = aluṅku; aluṅkutal = acital; alukku = acippu, iciyacippu (kamakam). al = ala = ali, alital = acital, palaviṭañ cellal, varuntutal; ali = iṭam peyarntu koṇṭēyirukkum nīrttiri. alikkaḻi = varuttu. ali - aci. al - āl. ālutal = āṭutal. ālatti - āḷatti = viḷakki vaṭṭamiṭṭu āṭṭutal, iciyi vaṭṭamiṭṭu āṭṭutalpōl vaḷarttal; āl - ālu - āṭu. āṭutal = acital, viḷiyāṭutal, kūttāṭutal. āṭuvatu āṭi. acittal = acittu uṭuttutal, pulittōli arikkacittu eṉṉun tēvārat toṭari nōkkuka. al - alku. alkutal = curuṅkutal. alku - aḵku. aḵkutal = curuṅkutal, nuṭpamātal, kūrātal.
e-ṭu: aḵkiya i u aḵkiyakaṉṟa aṟiveṉṉām. L.acuo, to sharpen, from root ak. sharp, E. acute, L. acus, a needle, E.L. acumen, sharpness, quickness of perception. E. acme, Gk. akme - ake, a point.
alku - alkul = curuṅkiya iṭi, iṭiyiṉ kīḻppakkam, alku - alaku, kūriya pala poruḷkaḷ alakuppeyar peṟṟaṉa. avi katti, paṟavi mūkku, ili, neṟkatir, nel mutaliyaṉa. paṟavi mūkkiṟkotta kaṉṉamum alakeṉappaṭṭatu. alaku - alakku = ili. alakku - ilakku - ili; alam = kūriya kalappikkoḻu, kalappi.
alku - aḵku - aḵkam - nel, kūlam (tāṉiyam). aḵku - akku akkam = kūlam, viti, maṇi. uruttira + akkam = uruttirākkam = civamaṇi; al - alku; alkutal = taṅkutal; alkunar = taṅkupavar, vacippavar; al - alku = taṅkum irākkālam, irā; al + avaṉ = allavaṉ - alavaṉ = iravil tōṉṟum nilā, iravil veḷiyēṟum naṇṭu. iṉi kuṟaṭṭiṉ alakupōṟ kuvukiṉṟa kāliyuṭiyatu naṇṭu eṉṟumām. alavu - alaku. al - aṟu; aṟutal = nīṅkutal, oṭital; aṟuti = muṭivu. ar eṉṉum olikkuṟippiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya aṟu (cut) eṉṉuñ colvēṟu.
al - alar - malar. cēymippaṭuvatāl virital uṇṭākum. o.nō. akal = cel, viri; paṭar = cel, viri; oru poruḷ muḻutum ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu cellutal nīṅkutalām; ataṉ miyam niṟka maṟṟap pakutikaḷ maṭṭum iṭam peyartal viritalām. malar - mallā, mallāttal = malarntu mēṉōkkippaṭuttal. mallā x kuppuṟu; kuppuṟu = kuvi, kīḻnōkkippaṭu. al - ala - alacu; alacutal = acittal, acittukkaḻuvutal, alital, varuntal, nīṅkiyiruttal; alacaṭi = varuttam; alacu - alacal = iḻi nīṅkiyirukkum āṭi; alavu - alaval = alacal, kaṟpiṉmi (kaṭṭiṉmi); al - ala - alam - alampu. alamputal = acittal, acittuk kaḻuvutal. alampu - alappu: alapputal = vāyacittal, pitaṟṟal. alaṭṭu = aci, varuttu, pitaṟṟu. alati, alakku - nīkkam, vēṟu. o.nō. viḷ - vil - vilu - viṟu - vīṟu - vēṟu = vilakiṉatu, vēṟāṉatu . alati - alāti. alakku - alākku. ittamiḻc coṟkaḷē intiyil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. nāṉ alāti, avaṉi alākkāyt tūkkikkoṇṭu pōyviṭṭāṉ eṉpaṉa ulaka vaḻakku. alavi  - pitaṟṟal, vāyāṭip peṇ, kaṟpiṉmi.  (cu.vi.)
āṅkilarāṭci
āṅkilarāṭci 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṭiyil tōṉṟi, ivvirupa tām nūṟṟāṇṭiṭiyil nīṅkiyatu. araciṉar aluvalakaṅkaḷi lum, peruñcālikaḷilum, potuviṭaṅkaḷilum, puki vaṇṭikaḷilum, miṉvaṇṭikaḷilum, uyarkalvi niliyaṅkaḷilum, vakuppu vēṟṟumi nīkkappaṭṭatu. āṅkilak kalvi ellā vakuppārkkum potuvāyiṟṟu. ellāt tuṟiyilum, poyyum puraṭṭum kaṭṭuṅ katiyumāṉa āriyat tolkati (purāṇa) muṟik kalviyiṉṟi, uṇmiyum akakkaraṇa vāṟṟali vaḷarppatum aṟiyāmiyiyum aṭimittaṉattiyum aṟavē akaṟṟuvatumāṉa, aṟiviyaṟkalvi pukaṭṭappaṭṭatu. kalvit tiṟami mikka aṉivarkkum vakuppu vēṟṟumiyiṉṟip paṭipputavi (Scholar-ship) yaḷikkappaṭṭatu. intiyā muḻutum paṭippaṭiyāka oru pērariya āṭcikkuṭ koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. āṅkilarāṭci yirunta, ilaṅki kāḻakam (parmā) maliyā teṉṉāppirikkā mutaliya pala veḷināṭukaḷilum, intiyar kuṭiyēṟit tamakkēṟṟa toḻilum aluvalum peṟṟu ēntāka vāḻntaṉar. āṅkilavaracu matattuṟiyil taliyiṭavēyilli. kula mata kaṭciyiṉa vēṟu pāṭiṉṟi, eḷiyārkkum valiyārkkum ēḻikaṭkum celvarkkum, orē naṭunili nayaṉmi (nīti) vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, āṅkilarāṭci nērmiyiyum putuccēri kārikkāl teru nērmiyiyum oruṅku nōkki, piriṭṭicu nītiyum pireñcu vītiyum. eṉṟu paḻamoḻiyāka vaḻaṅkumāṟu, potumakkaḷ pukaḻntu pārāṭṭiṉar. kumukāya iṉpa vāḻkkikkum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum muṭṭukkaṭṭiyāyirunta, uṭaṉkaṭṭi yēṟal, ceṭiṟkuttal (Hook-swinging), kuḻanti maṇam, narak kāvu (human sacrifice) mutaliya kuruṭṭup paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum; takkar, piṇṭāriyar, tīvaṭṭik koḷḷikkārar mutaliya koṭiya kayavar kūṭṭaṅkaḷum; aṟavē oḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. arumiyāṉa añcal tuṟiyum,  kuṟinta celavil virintu vaḻiccellum iruppup pātikaḷum , aḻakiya māṭa māḷikikaḷum malinakar kaḷum, intiyāveṅkum amintaṉa. uyirukkum poruṭkum cētamiṉṟi amitiyāka vāḻumāṟu ciṟanta ūr kāvaloḻuṅkum, kalakamum pōruṅ kaṉaviluṅ kāṇāvāṟu māperum paṭi yamippum, ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ellāt tiṇikkaḷaṅkaḷilum (departments), intiyar tattam kalvikkum tiṟamikkum takkavāṟu poṟuppu vāynta patavikaḷil amarttap peṟṟaṉar. intiyā muḻutum oṉṟupaṭṭu nāḷaṭivil taṉṉāṭci peṟumāṟu, intiyat tēciyap pērāyam (Indian National Congress) kiyūm (hume) eṉṉum āṅkilap perumakaṉārāl 1885-il tōṟṟuvikkap peṟṟatu. ataṉ payaṉāka, pērāyat talivarkaḷ, āṅkilar nēraṭiyāṭci maṇṭalaṅkaḷil maṭṭumaṉṟi, ellā uḷnāṭṭu maṉṉar nāṭukaḷuḷḷum urimiyoṭum pātukāppoṭum pukuntu, viṭutalip pōrāṭṭattiṟku vitikaḷi vāriyiṟittu vantaṉar. iṟutiyil, 1947ām āṇṭu muḻu veṟṟiyum peṟṟaṉar. tanti makaṉiṭattiṟ cottiyum, aracaṉ iḷavaracaṉiṭattil nāṭṭiyum, takunta paruvattil oppaṭippatupōl, āṅkilarum pērāyat talivariṭam intiyāvi oppaṭittuviṭṭu, amitiyāy akaṉṟaṉar. ataṉāṟ pireñciyarum pōrttuk kīciyarum caṟṟup pinti intiyāvi viṭṭu nīṅka nērntatu.

āṭcimuṟi
aracavoḷi: paṇṭit tamiḻāṭciyil, aracaṉē nāṭṭiṟkut taṉi nāyakamāyiruntāṉ. perumpālum, avaṉ iṭṭatu caṭṭamum vittatu variciyumāyiruntatu. muḻu niṟivāṉa talimiyum uṭaṉ kollum atikāramum avaṉukkirunta tāl, avaṉ kuṭikaḷ yāvarālum kaṇ kaṇṭa teyvamākavē karutappaṭṭāṉ. ‘âUth¡»‰F vâ®th¡F©nlh?, aracaṉ aṉṟu kollum teyvam niṉṟu kollum eṉṉum paḻamoḻikaḷ aracaṉukkirunta atikāra valiyi yuṇarttum.
aracaṉuk kiṉṟiyamiyātavarum avaṉuk kaṭutta paṭiyāy atikāra muṭiyavarumāṉa amiccar paṭit talivarum, avaṉi mika aṇukātu tīkkāyvārpōl otuṅki yoḻukiṉar. avaṉ muṉṉiliyil, piṟaroṭu pēcāmiyum nakāmiyum cāṭi kāṭṭāmiyum, avaṉic cuṭṭāmiyum, avaṉ piṟaroṭu mellap pēcuvati uṟṟuk kēḷāmiyum, avaṉoṭu payilvār pōṟṟik kākkavēṇṭiya oḻukka neṟikaḷākum. aracaṉiṭattil oṉṟik kūṟuvōr avaṉi vāḻttiyē kūṟuvatum. avaṉik kāṇumpōtum avaṉiviṭṭup piriyumpōtum perumakkaḷ tali vaṇaṅkiyum potumakkaḷ nilattil viḻuntum kumpiṭuvatum vaḻakkam.
aracavuṭimikaḷ teyvavuṭimikaḷ pōl tiruveṉṉum aṭimoḻi peṟṟa peyarālum, aracaviṉikaḷ teyva viṉikaḷ pōl aruḷ eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi koṇṭa viṉiccollālum, kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
aracaṉ amiccaroṭum paṭittalivaroṭum cūḻntu viṉi ceyvatu vaḻakkamāyiruntatēṉum. avaṉ virumpiyati niṟi vēṟṟaṟku etuvun taṭiyāyiruntatilli. talimi amicca ṉukkum paṭit talivaṉukkuṅkūṭa, aracaṉāl viṉavappaṭṭa kāriyattippaṟṟi avar tam karuttit terivikkum urimiyē yaṉṟi, avaṉik kaṭṭuppaṭuttum atikāram iruntilatu. mēlum amiccar paṭittalivaroṭu cūḻntē etuvuñ ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṉum yāppuṟavum aracaṉukkirunta tilli. āriyappaṭi kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, kōvalaṉik kolvit tatum, putiyalurimi eṭuttavarkkē eṉṟu paṟi cāṟṟuvit tatum, itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷām.
aracaṉ ceyal - aracaṉ taṉ tuṇiyatikārikaḷum pulavaruṅ kūṭiya aviyuṭaṉ ariyaṇiyil vīṟṟiruttal, aracu vīṟ ṟirukki eṉṟum makiḻirukki eṉṟum olakkamirukki eṉṟum kolu virukki eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. atu perum pālum kāliyil toṭaṅkumātalāl, ati nāḷavi nāṇ makiḻurukki nāḷōlakkam eṉak kūṟuvatuṇṭu. aracaṉ aracu vīṟṟirukkum maṇṭapam, vēttiyaṉ maṇṭapam, ōlakkamaṇṭa pam, kolu maṇṭapam, attāṇi maṇṭapam, capāmaṇṭapam eṉṉum peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum. am maṇṭapat tiṟkuc ciṟappup peyariṭuvatumuṇṭu. caṭāvarmaṉ kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ ōlakkamaṇṭapam pukaḻāparaṇam eṉap peyar peṟṟiruntatu.
aracaṉ ovvoru kāriyattiyum ataṟkuriya ciṟappuk kuḻuvuṭaṉ allatu atikāriyuṭaṉ kalantu eṇṇuvāṉāyiṉum, aṉittuk kāriyaṅkaḷiyum talimiyamiccaṉōṭu kūṭic cūḻnta piṉṉarē oru muṭiviṟku varuvaṉ.
aracirukkiyilum cūḻviṉiyilum araciyum uṭaṉiruppatu vaḻakkam.
aracaṉ ekkāriyattic ceytālum, kaṇiyaṉ kuṟitta naṉṉāḷilum maṅkala vēḷiyilumē ceyvaṉ.
ūrccapiyār, nāṭṭatikārikaḷ, kōyiṟ kaṇkāṇippāḷar, potumakkaḷ mutaliyavaruḷ, taṉippaṭṭavarō oru kuḻuvārō oru kāriyattiṟku aracaṉuṭiya oppattiyēṉum tīrppi yēṉum peṟavēṇṭi avaṉiṭam variṉ, avar tāmē nērilāvatu uṭaṉ kūṭṭattatikārikaḷuḷ oruvar vāyilākavāvatu aracaṉukku atit terivippar. aracaṉukku mika vēṇṭiyavarāyiruntā loḻiya nēril terivikka muṭiyātu. aracaṉ akkāriyatti ataṟkuriyavaruṭaṉellām cūḻntu. taṉ kaṭṭaḷiyi nēri lāvatu uṭaṉ kūṭṭattatikārikaḷuḷ oruvar vāyilāka vāvatu piṟappippaṉ.
aracaṉuṭiya āṇikaḷi ōliyi leḻutumpōtum piṉṉar avaṟṟuḷ mukkiyamāṉavaṟṟik kallil veṭṭumpōtum, avi piṟanta iṭattiyum amayattiyum piṉvarumāṟu kuṟippatu vaḻakkam.
	kaṅki koṇṭa cōḻapurattuk kōyiluḷḷāl, tiru mañcaṉa cāliyi leḻuntaruḷiyiruntu utakam paṇṇi yaruḷā niṟka.
	srī rājarāja tēvar tañcāvūrp periya ceṇṭu vāyiṟ cittira kūṭattut teṟkil kallūriyil eḻuntaruḷi.
	kāñcīpurattuk kōyiluḷḷāl aṭṭattu veḷmēli maṇṭapam rājēntira cōḻaṉil coṭṭiyil eḻuntaruḷi yiruntu.
	puraci nāṭṭuc civapurattup pakalirukkiyil tiruvamutu ceytaruḷā viruntu. (cōḻavamica carittirac curukkam, pak.49).
vēṟṟaracarum māṟṟaracarum viṭutta tūtar, vāyiṟ kāvalaṉ vāyilāy aracaṉukkut tam varavit terivittu, uttaravu peṟṟapiṉ ōlakka maṇṭapam pukuntu, tam aracar tirumukatti nīṭṭi, atu paṟṟiya viṉākkaṭkellām añcātu viṭi kūṟuvar. aracaṉ taṉ aviyuṭaṉ naṉṟāka āyntu ataṟkut takka viṭi viṭuppaṉ. avvavvaracar tarattiṟkum naṭpaḷaviṟkum takkapaṭi, avar viṭutta tūtaric cila pala nāṭkaḷ aracaṉ kākka vippatu muṇṭu.
talinakarilirunta aṟaṅkūṟaviyattil, potu vakiyāṉa vaḻakkukkaḷiyellām aviyattārē tīrppar.

cāṉṟōr, iruvar colvaḻakku mēṟkoṇ ṭaṉuvatit
tiraṇṭu nōkki

taṉapati vaṇikar tāmē yivareṉac cāṟṟa lōṭum
maṉavalit tāyat tārtam vaḻakkiḻuk kaṭinta tītu
naṉivaḻi vēmpaṉ tēriṟ ṟaṇṭikku nammi peṉṉā 				(māmaṉākavantu vaḻakkuritta paṭalam. 28,30,33.)
eṉṟu parañcōtimuṉivar, taṉapatic ceṭṭiyār vaḻakki maturi yaṟaṅkūṟaviyattārē tīrttatākak kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. ciṟappāṉatum aracaṉoṭu toṭar puṟṟatumāṉa vaḻakkāyiṉ. aracaṉē maṉṟattiruntu tīrppaṉ. cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟuttut tuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ. maliyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāri makkaḷip paṟṟiya ceytiyi. uṟanti maṉṟattiliruntu āyntami kāṇka. īrūrārkkum, irukulattārkkum, iru matattārkkum, iru kōyilārkkum, īratikārikaṭkum, iru ciṟṟaracarkkum iṭip paṭṭa vaḻakkāyiṉ; avvaḻakkāḷikaḷ talinakarkku vanta viṭattu aracaṉē tīrttuvippatum, avar varātu aracaṉukkaṟi vitta (allatu avaṉiṭam muṟiyiṭṭa) viṭattu atit tīrkkum paṭi avaṉ ōr atikāriyi aṉuppuvatum vaḻakkam. vaṉkoli yum muṟikēṭāṉa kolit taṇṭamum nikaḻnta viṭattuk koliyuṇṭāriṉ āruyirk kēḷir aracaṉiṭam muṟiyiṭu tal poruṭṭuc cōḻar araṇmaṉip puṟavāyilil ārāycci maṇi kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. pāṇṭi nāṭṭil aḵtilliyā yiṉum, pāṇṭiyar kāṭcik keḷiyarāyiruntu attakiya muṟi yīṭukaḷik kēṭṭu muṟi vaḻaṅki vantaṉar eṉpatu, kaṇṇaki muṟiyīṭṭā laṟiyappaṭum. iṉi, kaṭiṉamāṉa vaḻakkiṉ uṇmi kāṇṭaṟku iṟivaṉ tirumuṉ pāṭukiṭappatum māṟukōlam pūṇṭu ceṉṟu potumakkaḷ kūṟṟik kavaṉippatum, pāṇṭiyar vaḻakkam.
aracaṉ taṉ nāṭṭu nilimiyip paṟṟi amiccar vāyi lākavum, piṟar nāṭṭu nilimiyip paṟṟit tūtar vāyilākavum, iraṇṭiyum paṟṟi oṟṟar vāyilākavum, aṭikkaṭi aṟintu varuvaṉ. oṟṟaruḷ oruvaṉ kūṟṟi avaṉaṟiyātu iṉṉoruvaṉ kūṟṟoṭu oppunōkki, mūvar kūṟṟu ottaviṭattē ati aracaṉ nampuvāṉ.
taṉ nāṭṭu nilimiyiyum taṉ nakara nilimiyiyum tāṉē aṟivāṉvēṇṭi, iṭiyiṭi nāṭukāvaṟ cuṟṟuppōkkuc celvatum, māṟukōlam pūṇṭu nakara nōṭṭañ ceyvatum aracaṉ vaḻakkam. vikkiramac cōḻaṉ 1122 il kuṭantikkaru kiluḷḷa paḻiyāṟiyilum, 1123 il ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭat tic cērnta kuḷivaḷanallūril oru kuḷakkari maṇṭapattilum, 1124 il teṉṉārkkāṭṭu māvaṭṭattic cērnta vīra nārāyaṇac caturvēti maṅkalam eṉṉum kāṭṭu maṉṉār kōyilil uḷḷa araṇmaṉiyilum, 1130 il tillik karukil ōr araṇmaṉiyilum, iruntatākak kalveṭṭuk kūṟuvatāl, avaṉ aṭikkaṭi nāṭukāvaṟ cuṟṟup pōkkuc ceṉṟami aṟiyappaṭum, maturiyil koṟkippāṇṭiyaṉ māṟukōlam pūṇṭu nakara nōṭṭam ceytu, kīranti ūrilillātapōtu avaṉ vīṭṭiṟkuk kāvalāyiruntu vantatu nūṟpukaḻpeṟṟa ceytiyākum.
aracaṉ taṉ nāṭukāvaṟ cuṟṟuppōkkil, āṅkāṅkuḷḷa atikārikaḷ tam kaṭamikaḷic cevvaṉē niṟivēṟṟukiṉṟār kaḷāveṉak kavaṉippatum, ūrc capiyārum aṟaṅkūṟaviyat tārum tīrkkamuṭiyāta vaḻakkukkaḷit tīrttuvippatum, kuṟṟañceytavarit taṇṭippatum, takkārkkut tāṉañcey vatum, kuṭikaḷ muṟiyīṭukaḷik kēṭṭu avarkaḷ kuṟikaḷi nīkkuvatum, cīrtiruttamum makkaḷ nalamum paṟṟiya āṇikaḷip piṟappippatum, vaḻakkam.
iyaṟkiyākavum ceyaṟkiyākavum nāṭṭiṟkup peruntuṉpam nērnta kālaṅkaḷil, aracaṉ attuṉpaṅkaḷi iyaṉṟavari nīkkuvatum, attuṉpa nīkkattiṟkākat toṇṭu ceytavarkku māṉiyam aḷippatum uṇṭu, veḷḷakkālattil karikaṭṭi uṭippaṭippatum, aḻinta nilatti mīḷa aḷantu avvāṇṭu iṟinīkkik  koṭuppatum, pañcakālattil vari nīkkip paḻanel viṟpatum, puyaṟkālattil iṭinta kōyilkaḷip piṉṉark kaṭṭuvatum, kalakaṅkaḷip paṭikoṇṭu aṭakkuvatum, koḷḷi nōyk kālattil (teyvakōpan taṇittaṟ poruṭṭup) paliyiṭṭu viḻaveṭuppatum, kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷ nāṭṭil vantu cētañceyyiṉ avaṟṟi vēṭṭiyāṭik kolvatum, kaṭalkōḷ nērntaviṭattup piṟanāṭṭup pakuti kaḷi veṉṟu avaṟṟil taṉ kuṭikaḷik kuṭiyēṟṟuvatum, tuṉpa nīkkac ceyalkaḷām.
ciṟṟaracar ceyal: ciṟṟaraca nāṭukaṭkellām taṉṉāṭci (Self Government) aḷikkappaṭṭiruntatu. tiṟiyaḷattal allatu paṭiyutavip pōrāṟṟal ciṟṟaracar ceyalām.
aracaṉ valikuṉṟiyapōtu, aṉpaṟṟavarum atikāra viruppiṉaru māṉa ciṟṟaracar, aracaṉukkaṭaṅkātu taṉiyaracākiviṭuvatum avaṉuṭiya pakiyaracaroṭu toṭarpu koḷvatum uṇṭu. attakiya camiyaṅkaḷil aṉpum uṇmiyum aṭakkamumuḷḷa ciṟṟaracar oṉṟu cērntu, aṭaṅkāc ciṟṟaracarkku māṟākat tamakkuḷ oppantañ ceytukoḷvatu muṇṭu. atu nilimit tīṭṭu eṉappaṭum.
3 ām kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉiṉ 27 ām āṇṭil, kīḻkkuṟitta ciṟṟaracar patiṉmarum, aracaṉ cārpākavum avaṉukkaṭaṅkāta ciṟṟaracarkku māṟākavum tammuḷ oppantañ ceytu koṇṭaṉar.
(1)	pāṇṭināṭu koṇṭāṉāṉa campuvarāyaṉ.
(2)	ceṅkēṇi attimallaṉ vīrāṇṭāṉāṉa etirilic cōḻa campuvarāyaṉ.
(3)	kiḷiyūr maliyamāṉ periyuṭiyāṉāṉa irājarājac cētirāyaṉ.
(4)	kiḷiyūr maliyamāṉ ākāracūraṉāṉa irāja kampīrac cētirāyaṉ.
(5)	kuntaṉ nampūralāṉa irājarāja nīlakaṅkariyaṉ.
(6)	ammiyappaṉ marutaṉāṉa irājarāja mūvēntariyaṉ.
(7)	pāvantīrttāṉāṉa irājēntira cōḻac campuvarāyaṉ.
(8)	naraciṅka vaṉmaṉāṉa karikālcōḻa āṭiyūr
nāṭāḻvāṉ.
(9)	cōmaṉ tiruvaṇṇāmaliyuṭiyāṉāṉa kulōttuṅka cōḻa pirutikaṅkaṉ.
(10)	cōmaṉ varantaruvāṉāṉa cōḷēntira ciṅkappiruti kaṅkaṉ. (mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉ (irāmac cantira tīṭcitar) pak.87-88).
nāṭṭatikārikaḷ ceyal: nāṭṭatikārikaḷ tam nāṭṭic cuṟṟippārttu, atiluḷḷa ūrccapiyār kōyil kaṇkāṇip pāḷar aṟaniliyap pātukāppāḷar mutaliyōr tattaṅ kaṭamikaḷic cevvaṉē niṟivēṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉarāveṉak kavaṉippatum, ūrkkaṇakku kōyiṟkaṇakku aṟaniliyak kaṇakku mutaliyavaṟṟit taṇikkiyiṭuvatum, kuṟṟaṅkaṇṭa viṭattut taṇṭippatum, āṅkāṅkuḷḷa vaḻakkukaḷit tīrttu vippatum, aracāṇikaḷi niṟivēṟṟuvatum, ūrccapit tērtali naṭattivippatum, ceyvar.
ciṟṟūrkaḷ nāṭṭatikārikaḷiṉ kaṇkāṇippilum pērūrāṉa nakarkaḷ aracaṉatu nēraṭiyāṉa kaṇkāṇippilum, iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
ūrccapiyār, tam kuṟṟatti maṟittaṟporuṭṭum tammēṟ kuṟṟatti ēṟṟāmipporuṭṭum, tammik kaṇkāṇikkum nāṭṭatikārikaṭkum tammi viṉavavarum vēṟatikārikaṭkum ki yūṭṭum koṭuppatuṇṭeṉpatu; caṭāvarmaṉ cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ kālattil, ōr ūrccapiyār, vārappaṟṟiliruntum, kaṭamip paṟṟiliruntum varum vārattiyum kaṭamiyiyum, cuntarapāṇṭi yaṉuṭiya atikārikaḷiṉ naṭpip peṟac celaviṭṭati liruntu aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. (Pandyan Kingdom p. 218-9).
ūrccapiyār ceyal : ūrccapiyāruḷ, ovvoru vāriyat tārum tattam kaṭamiyi āṟṟivantaṉar. avaruḷ ūr vāriyat tiṉarkku, urimi (Civil) vaḻakku, kuṟṟa (Criminal) vaḻakku ākiya iruvaki vaḻakkiyun tīrkkavum kuṟṟavāḷikaḷit taṇṭikkavum, muḻu atikāramiruntatu. avarāl tīrkkamuṭiyāta vaḻakkum, avar tīrtta vaḻakkiṉ mēṉ muṟiyīṭum (Appeal) aracaṉiṭam koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa.
orē vāriyattiṟkuriya ceytiyi avvavvāriyattārum, pala vāriyaṅkaṭkuriya ceytiyi ellā vāriyattārum, kūṭic cūḻntu muṭivuceytaṉar. ūrccapik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ kāḷamūtiyum paṟicāṟṟiyum kūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. kūṭṭaṅ kūṭuvataṟku ūr maṇṭapam allatu māḷiki iruntatu. aḵtillā viṭattuk kōyil maṇṭapattil kūṭṭam naṭipeṟṟatu.
avvappōtu varum aracāṇikaḷit talimēṟkoṇṭu avaṟṟi niṟivēṟṟivippatum; avaṟṟuḷ mukkiyamāṉa vaṟṟiyum ūrāṭciyil tām ceytuḷḷa putumiyāṉa ēṟpāṭu kaḷiyum, kallil veṭṭuvittuc capi maṇṭapattilāvatu kōyil maṇṭapattilāvatu patippippatum; ūrccapiyār kaṭamiyākum.
ūrk kuṭiyiruppiyum, veḷḷāṇmiyiyum iraviṟ kāval ceyvatu pāṭikāvalaṉ vēli.
ūraviyāriṉ ūrāṭci, ūrāṇmiyeṉṟum ūrami yeṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.
ciṟṟūrt talivaṉ ceyal: āḷnilap pirivāṉa oru kūṭṭūriṉ pakutiyāy. orē kulattār allatu oru kulattār perumpāṉ miyarāka irukkum ciṟṟūril, avvakkulattiṟkum iṭattiṟku mēṟpa, ampalakāraṉ, ūrāḷi, kavuṇṭaṉ, kiḻavaṉ, kuṭumpaṉ, cērvi (cērvikāraṉ) talimakaṉ, talivaṉ, nāṭaṉ (nāṭāṉ, nāṭāḷvi), nāṭṭāṉ, nāṭṭampalam, nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉ, paṭṭakkāraṉ, paṭṭaṅkaṭṭi, paṭṭattuppiḷḷi, paṇṇāṭi, periyataṉakkāraṉ, maṇiyakkāraṉ, maṉṟāṭi (maṇṇāṭi) mantiri. mūppaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟip pūṇṭu. ūrārāl tērnteṭukkappaṭāmal tantiyiṉiṉṟu makaṉukku varum vaḻimuṟi yurimippaṭi ūrttalivaṉāyiruppavaṉ, taṉ ūr vaḻakkukkaḷil koliyoḻinta piṟavaṟṟiyellām tāṉē tīrttuvantatākat terikiṉṟatu. (Hindu Administrative Institutions in South India. p.262).
ciṟṟūriliruntu aracaṉukku varavēṇṭiya varikaḷellām oḻuṅkāy vantu, ūrār ūrttalivaṉōṭu muṟṟum ottup pōyk kāriyaṅkaḷ amitiyāy naṭantu vantatiṉāl, aracaṉum nāṭṭatikāriyum kūṭṭūrccapiyārum ciṟṟūr vaḻakkut tīrppil perumpālum taliyiṭavilli. kūṭṭūric cērnta ellāvūr kaṭkum potuvāṉa ceytikaḷiyum, ciṟṟūrc ceytikaḷil ūrttalivaṉ atikārattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭavaṟṟiyumē, ūrc capiyār kavaṉittu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu.
uttamaccōḻaṉ kālattil, toṇṭi nāṭṭic cērnta ciṟṟiyāṟṟūril piramatēyamākak koṭukkappaṭṭa oru nilattip paṟṟiya araca āṇi yōliyil, aṭikaḷ nakkaṉ eṉṉum palvattiri kiḻavaṉ (pavvattiri yūrttalivaṉ) uṭaṉ kūṭṭattati kārikaḷuṭaṉ cērntu kiyeḻuttiṭṭatālum, ūrttalivaṉu ṭiya ūḻiyattiṟkāka ampalamāṉiyam eṉṉum iṟiyili nilam viṭappaṭṭiruntatiṉālum, ciṟṟūrt talivaṉ patavi aracaṉāl oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭiruntati aṟiyalām.
ciṟṟūrt talivaṉukku ūrāṭciyil utavuvataṟku, cilavūrkaḷil, tuṇittalivaṉoruvaṉum karumamāṟṟuvōṉoruvaṉum cēvakaṉoruvaṉumāka mūvar iruntaṉar. ovvōr ūrkkum, nīrppāycci yoruvaṉum kuṭikkāval eṉṉum ūrkāvalaṉ oruvaṉum iruntaṉar.
ūrvaḻakkukkaḷit tīrppataṟkum ūril caṭṭatiṭṭaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttaṟkum ūrttalivaṉ kūṭṭum kūṭṭam, ūrmaṇṭa pattilāvatu, oru marattaṭiyilāvatu, veḷḷiṭiyilāvatu naṭipeṟum.
āvaṇakkaḷariyār ceyal: āḷnila ūrtoṟum āvaṇak kaḷariyiruntatu. oru nilatti viṟpavaṉum koḷpavaṉum, allatu aṭimāṉam vippavaṉum vāṅkupavaṉum, allatu kuttakikku viṭupavaṉum eṭuppavaṉum, tammuḷ icintu eḻutiya āvaṇatti āvaṇakkaḷarikkeṭuttuk koṇṭu pōy, kaḷariyār muṉ tām iruvarum icintu koṇṭatāka uṟutimoḻi kūṟiyapiṉ āvaṇam kaḷariyārāl kāppiṭap peṟum. kāppiṭutal eṉpatu aracu muttiriyiṭṭup pativuceytal.
āvaṇattiṟku, ēṭu, ōli, karaṇam, kalam, cīṭṭu, ceyki, tīṭṭu, naṟukku, paṭṭiki, paṭṭayam, muṟi mutaliya piṟa coṟkaḷum, avvav viṉikkum eḻutappaṭṭa karuvikkum ēṟpa vaḻaṅkiṉa. āyiṉum, avaṟṟiyellām taḻuvuvatum, atikāra eḻuttuc cāṉṟic ciṟappākak kuṟippatum, āvaṇam eṉṉum collē pattiram piramāṇam eṉṉum vaṭacol vaḻakku piṟkālatta tākum. ōli cīṭṭu naṟukku muṟi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ paṉiyōliyil eḻutap paṭṭavaṟṟiyum, paṭṭiki paṭṭam eṉpaṉa ceppēṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭavaṟṟiyum, piṟa iraṇṭiyum, kuṟikkum.
nilaviṟpaṉi yāvaṇattiṟku viliyāvaṇam, viliyōli, vilit tīṭṭu eṉṉum peyarkaḷum: āḷviṟpaṉi yāvaṇattiṟku āḷvilittīṭṭu, āḷvilippiramāṇa icivuttīṭṭu, aṭimiyōli eṉṉum peyarkaḷum; vaḻaṅkiṉa. aṭimāṉa āvaṇam, oṟṟikkalam oṟṟikkaraṇam oṟṟinaṟukku aṭiyōli eṉṟum; aṭimāṉam vāṅkupavaṉ eḻutikkoṭutta ōli, etiraṭiyōli yeṉṟum; peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. nukarcciyōṭu kūṭiya oṟṟikku iṟaṅkoṟṟi allatu aṭippu (Usufructuary mortgage) eṉṟum niliyāṉa oṟṟikku uṟāvoṟṟi (Irredeemable mortgage) eṉṟum peyar. tīṭṭukkāraṉāl (Notary) eḻutappaṭāta allatu pativu ceyyappaṭāta oṟṟikkalam kiyoṟṟi eṉappaṭṭatu. kuttakit tavaṇi muṭintavuṭaṉ ataṉ tīrvupaṟṟi eḻutappaṭuvataṟku aṟutimuṟi (Cancelled lease-deed) eṉṟum, oru poruḷiṉ urimi kuṟippiṭṭa oruvark kaṉṟip piṟarukkilliyeṉṟu kuṟikkum eḻuttīṭṭiṟku viṭutīṭṭu allatu tīrvumuṟi (Release-deed) eṉṟum, peyar. oruvar oṉṟaṟ kuṭampaṭṭu eḻutittarum cīṭṭu, icivut tīṭṭu iciyōli oppumuṟi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṟ kuṟikkappeṟṟatu. oruvar oṉṟip peṟṟukkoṇṭatāka eḻutittarum, cīṭṭiṟkup paṟṟumuṟi (Receipt) eṉṟu peyar. nilavārac cākupaṭi yāvaṇam (Deed of lease for cultivation) kāṇip piṭipāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. 
viliyāvaṇam oḻinta piṟavaṟṟuḷ perumpālaṉa, iṉṟiyami yātu āvaṇakkaḷariyiṟ pativuceyyappaṭa vēṇṭun tiṟattaṉavalla.
potuvakiyāṉa āvaṇaṅkaḷ ōliyilum, ciṟappāṉavi ceppēṭṭilum, eḻutappeṟum. ūrārukkut teriyavēṇṭiya āvaṇamāyiṉ, kallil veṭṭappeṟum.
aṟaṅkūṟaviyattār ceyal : nakaraṅkaḷilirunta aṟamaṉṟattār, vaḻakkāḷi (plintiff) etir vaḻakkāḷi (Defendant) ākiya iruvariṉ colliyum palamuṟi kēṭṭu, kariyum (witness) cāṉṟum (Evidence) iruppiṉ avaṟṟik koṇṭum, aviyillāviṭiṉ nūlutti paṭṭaṟivu (karutiyuktiyaṉupavam) koṇṭum, naṭunili miyāyt tīrttu muṟiceytu vantaṉar.
iruvartam colliyum eḻutaraṅ kēṭṭē
eṉṟu ativīrarāma pāṇṭiyaṉum.
iruvar colvaḻakku nōkki yaṉuvatit tiraṇṭunōkki
eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivarum, kūṟiyiruppatāl, vaḻakki naṉṟāy aṟintukoḷḷaṟkum, vaḻakkāḷariṉ meymmi kāṇṭaṟkum, maṉṟattār vaḻakkip palamuṟi colvittami aṟiyappaṭum.
kariyuñ cāṉṟumillāta vaḻakkukkaḷil, paḻukkak kāycciya irumpip piṭittal, pāmpuk kuṭattil kiyiṭutal mutaliya teyvaccāṉṟu vāyilākat tam meymmiyi nāṭṭumpaṭi, vaḻakkāḷari maṉṟattār kēṭpatu muṇṭu. attakiya cāṉṟu pala vaḻakkukaḷil uṇmikku māṟāyiruntateṉpatu. pāḻntaṉicu vēṇṭip pāmpukkuṭam peṟṟāṉ eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyālum.
kiviṭṭa oṇporuḷ kivara villeṉpār
meyppaṭṭa vāṟē uṇarntārāl - meyyā
maṭampaṭṭa māṉōkkiṉ māmayi laṉṉāy!
kaṭampeṟṟāṉ peṟṟāṉ kuṭam
eṉṉum paḻamoḻic ceyyuḷālum (57) aṟiyappaṭum. immuṟi piṟkālattuk kiviṭappaṭṭatu.
vaḻakkukkaḷ, urimi vaḻakku (Civil case) kuṟṟa vaḻakku (Criminal case) eṉa ikkālattiṟ pirikkappaṭṭiruppatupōl ak kālattiṟ  pirikkappaṭavilli. iru vaki vaḻakkukkaḷum ellā aṟamaṉṟaṅ kaḷilum tīrkkappaṭṭaṉa.
paṭittalivaṉ ceyal : āṅkāṅku avvappōtu naṭakkuṅ kalakaṅkaḷiyum koḷḷikaḷiyum, tāṉākavum taṉ tuṇi varik koṇṭum aṭakkivippatu, paṭittalivaṉ ceya lākum.  (pa.ta.ā.)
āṭṭi
oru viḷiyāṭṭi oru muṟi āṭi muṭippatu ōr āṭṭi eṉappaṭum.  (ta.vi.3.)

āṭu-(ēṭu) ēṭa
muḻuttal = tiraḷutal. muḻu-muḻā = tiraṇṭamuracu, muḻā-muḻavu - muḻavam.
muḻā-miḻā = tiraṇṭa āṇmāṉ.
o.nō. muṭukku - miṭukku. muṇṭu - miṇṭu, muṟi - miṟi = vaḷivu.
miḻā - mēḻam = tiraṇṭa cemmaṟikkaṭā.
mēḻam - mēḻakam = cemmaṟikkaṭā.
vempari mēḻakamēṟṟi  (cīvaka. 522).
mēḻam - mēṭam = cemmaṟikkaṭā, mēṭavōri (piṅ.).
mēḻakam - mēṭakam = cemmaṟikkaṭā, mēṭavōri.

āṭu aṭittal
pulāl uṇpavar āṭu kōḻi mutaliya uyir (pirāṇi)kaḷik kolvati āṭu kollutal kōḻi kollutal eṉṉātu āṭaṭittal kōḻiyaṭittal eṉak kūṟum vaḻakku, makkaḷ kuṟiñci niliyil vilaṅkukaḷiyum paṟavikaḷiyum kallālum vaṇari (vaḷitaṭi) yālum aṭittuk koṉṟati niṉippūṭṭum.  (col. 19.)
āṇ peṇ iyalpu
āṇmiyuḷḷavaṉ āṇ; peṇmiyuḷḷavaḷ peṇ. āṇmi - maṟam ; peṇmi virumpappaṭum taṉmi. neṟippaṭak karutiya karuttukkaḷiyellām veḷiyiṭutalum, iruḷukkum pēykkum añcāmiyum, uyirkkiṟuti variṉum oḻukkam tappāmiyum, paṟṟalarik kaṇṭāl paṇiyātu porutiṟattalum, iṭukkaṇ varuṅkāl nakutalum, āṇmiyām; mukattaḻakōṭu akattaḻakum uṭimiyum āṇavamiṉṟi yamintiruttalum, kalviyaṟivu nirampiyiruttalum, nalla kaṇavaṉukku aṭaṅki naṭattalum peṇmiyām.  (col 105.)
āṇmi
āṇmi āḷuntaṉmi. āḷutal payaṉpaṭuttutal.  (kuṟaḷ 614.)
āṇavam - āṇava
āṇ - āṇavam = āṇmi, cerukku.
māṉiyar villiyamcu akaramutali, āṇava eṉṉum colli aṇu eṉpatiṉiṉṟu tirittu nuṇmi allatu mikac ciṟumi eṉṟu mūlapporuḷ kūṟukiṉṟatu.  (va.va.83.)
āṇi2
āṇi - āṇi (i.vē.)
āḻ - āḻi - āṇi = āḻntiṟaṅkuvatu, iruppāṇi, maravāṇi, āṇi pōl kūrāṉatu.
āṇikkuruttu = aṭikkuruttu, āṇivēr = aṭivēr.
āṇi = aṭippaṭi, nilikkaḷam, tāṅkal.
āṇiyā yulakuk kellām  (kamparā. kaṭaṟāvu. 27).
āṇikkoḷḷutal = ūṉṟik koḷḷutal.
ḻa - ṇa, pōli. o.nō. taḻal - taṇal, niḻal - niṇal.
kālṭuvelār poruntutal eṉṉum aṭippaṭip poruḷ koṇṭu, aṇ eṉṉuñ colli mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar.
vaṭamoḻiyār, ūcimuṉi, kaḻumuṉi, accāṇi, mukakkōṟ kuccu, vīṭṭumūli, elli eṉṟu vaṭamoḻi yakaramutaliyiṟ poruḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa aṇi eṉṉuñ colli mūlamākak kāṭṭa virumpuvar. atu āṇi eṉpataṉ kuṟukkamē.
muṉipporuḷ koḷḷiṉum, aḷ (kūrmi) eṉṉum teṉ collē āṇivērām. - va.va.84.
āttirēliya moḻikaṭkum tamiḻukkum uḷḷa oppumi
(1)	cakara vēṟupāṭukaḷum mūccolikaḷu millāmi.
(2)	piṉṉoṭṭuc coṟkaḷālēyē perumpālum putuccoṟkaḷ ākkappaṭal.
(3)	āttirēliyā moḻikaḷil uyartiṇip peyarkaḷum aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷum vēṟupaṭuttappaṭāmi. mutupaḻan tamiḻilum iṅṅaṉamē yiruntatu.
	kā : maṇveṭṭi, viṟakuveṭṭi, calippāṉ (callaṭi).
(4)	ar paṉmiyīṟā yiruttal.
tamiḻar, muṇṭar, nākar, āttirēliyar eṉpavar paṇṭu ōriṉat tārāyiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
ilaṅkiyil paṇṭu vaḻaṅkiyatu tamiḻeṉṟum, īḻanāṭṭara carkku muṭinākar eṉṟa pēr iruntateṉṟum, muttuttampip piḷḷiyavarkaḷ centamiḻc cuvaṭikiyil (Magazine) eḻuti yiruppatu poruttamāṉatē.
āttirēliyar tirāviṭarip palavakiyil ottiruppatāka māntaṉūlār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  (o.mo.)
āti
āti eṉpatu toṭakkam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu. peṇpāṟ peyarāyiṉ ātaṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl vaṭivām. atu tūya teṉcol.  (ti.ma.piṉ.)

ām2 - āp
