āmaṇakku
āmaṇakku - āmaṇṭa
āmaṇakku naṭṭāl āccā vākumā? eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. ciṟṟāmaṇakku, kāṭṭāmaṇakku (ātaḷi) eṉpaṉa āmaṇakku, vakikaḷ .
vaṭacol amaṇṭa, maṇṭa eṉṉum vaṭivuṅ koḷḷum.  (va.va.84.)
āytam
āyta eḻuttu vakimi
eḻutteṉap paṭupa
akara mutala ṉakara viṟuvāy
muppaḵ teṉpa
cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē
avitām
kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
āytam eṉṟa
muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttō raṉṉa  (tol. nūṉmarapu 1, 2)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷāl mutaleḻuttu muppa teṉpatum, cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟeṉpatum, ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉi yavē ciṟantaṉaveṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṟ cārntu varaṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē eḻutteṉappaṭupa muppaḵteṉpa eṉavē, cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟumē ciṟantaṉa; ēṉiya muppatum avvāṟu ciṟantila veṉavum poruṭantu niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉi vilakkic ciṟanta muppatu eḻuttōṭu iviyum oppa vaḻaṅku meṉṟaṟku, eḻuttōraṉṉa eṉṟār eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟutal kāṇka.
iṉi, ip peyarkaḷē peyar, im muṟiyē muṟi; tokiyum mūṉṟē eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tēṟṟēkāramiṭṭuk kūṟu vatālum, naṉṉūlār cārpeṉak kūṟiya pattu eḻuttukkaḷuḷ ēṉiya vēḻum tolkāppiyar kālattilum iruntamiyālum, tolkāppiyarkkum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarkkum naṉṉūlār koḷki yiruntatilli yeṉpatu teḷivām.
āytap peyarum oliyum
tolkāppiyar āytattik kuṟikkumpōtē muppāṟpuḷḷi eṉa ataṟku maṟṟumoru peyar koṭuttār. piṟkālat tāciriyar vēṟuñ cila peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar. āytam eṉpatu aḵkēṉam, aḵkēṉameṉiṉum āyta meṉiṉum taṉinili yeṉiṉum puḷḷi yeṉiṉum oṟṟeṉiṉum okkum, eṉṉi?
aḵkēṉa māytan taṉinili puḷḷi
yoṟṟip pāla vintu mitaṟkē
eṉṟār mayēccurar eṉṟu kārikiyuriyāciriyarāṉa kuṇacā kararum, āytam eṉiṉum, akkēṉam eṉiṉum, taṉinili eṉiṉum, puḷḷi eṉiṉum, oṟṟu eṉiṉum okkum, eṉṉi?
akkēṉa māytan taṉinili puḷḷi
yoṟṟip pāla vintu mitaṟkē
eṉṟār avinayaṉār eṉṟu yāpparuṅkala viruttikārarum kūṟuvatu kāṇka.
avinaya nūṟpāvil akkēṉameṉpatu ēṭṭuppiḻiyā yiruttal vēṇṭum. paḻiya tiṇṇippaḷḷip pāṭamuṟiyil āytappeyar  ataṉ oli vaṭivoṭṭi aḵkaṉṉā eṉa vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
āytavoli
ēṉiyiru cārpeḻuttup pōṉṟē. āytamum oru mutaleḻuttiṉ tiripeṉpatu. itaṟku (āytattiṟku) vaṭivu kūṟiṉār. ēṉi yoṟṟukaḷ pōla uyirēṟātu ōci vikāramāy niṟpatoṉ ṟākaliṉ, eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyālum,
cārntuvari ṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat
tērntuveḷip paṭutta ēṉi mūṉṟum
tattañ cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi
yotta kāṭciyiṟ ṟammiyal piyalum  (piṟappiyal, 19)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvālum, cārpeḻuttu eṉṉum peyarālum, aṟiyappaṭum. āyiṉ, evveḻuttiṉ tiripeṉiṉ, kakarattiṉ tiripām eṉka.
āytam toṉṟutoṭṭu melliya kakaramākavē olikkappaṭṭu varuvatālum, ēṉac cāriyi peṟṟakkāl aḵkēṉam eṉak kakarattōṭiyintē vaḻaṅku vatālum, paṇṭiyēṭṭariccuvaṭi kaḷil ka - ḵ eṉṟu eḻutap peṟṟamiyālum uyirukkum meykkum iṭiyil vikkappaṭṭiruppiṉum kakaratti yaṭuttirup patālum, pōppiyar tam tamiḻilakkaṇattil ‘the guttural’ eṉṉum, kālṭuvel tam oppiyalilakkaṇattil “guttural h” eṉṟum kuṟittiruppatālum, ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyil ataṉ amippu muṟiyi viḷakkumiṭattu “the aspirate aytam” eṉṟu kuṟittiruppiṉum, varipeyarppil (transliteration) k eṉṉum kuṟiyi āḷvatālum āytaveḻuttu kakarattiṉ tiripēyeṉat teṟṟeṉa vaṟika.
āriya vaṉmi cāṉṟa valleḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻukkiṉmiyiṉ, āytatti vaṭamoḻi hakaram (h) pōṉṟa mūccoliyākak koḷvatu muṟṟum tavaṟām. iṉi vaṭamoḻi vicarkkam: pōṉṟa oliyeṉak koḷḷiṉ, oliyaḷavil vicarkkam āytattiyot tiruppiṉum, atil akarak kūṟum kalantiruppatāl, atuvum poruntāteṉka. āytam evvakiyiṉum uyirēṟappeṟā eḻuttām kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi eṉṉum nūṟpā vuriyil, āytamāṉa puḷḷi, āytappuḷḷi eṉṟār. itaṉiyum oṟṟiṉpāṟ cārttutaṟ keṉka. oṟṟēl uyirēṟap peṟal vēṇṭumeṉiṉ. cārpeḻuttātaliṉ uyirēṟap peṟāteṉak koḷka eṉa mayilinātar urittiruppatik kāṇka.
āytam aṟṟālaḷavaṟin tuṇka vaḵtuṭampu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uyirpōla alaku peṟṟum, tōṉṟiṟ pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟuka vaḵtilār mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil meypōla alaku peṟāmalum uyirmeykaḷi (oru puṭiyottu) uyirumākāmal meyyumā kāmal (alipōlat) taṉiniṟṟalāl, taṉinili eṉappaṭṭatu eṉṟār naṉṉūṟ kāṇṭiki yurikārarākiya caṭakōpa rāmāṉucāc cāriyār. aṟṟā laḷavaṟin tuṇka vaḵtuṭampu eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku āytam alaku peṟal vēṇṭāmi yāṉum, avvāccāriyārē uyirum meyyum tammiṟ kalattal pōla āytam ōreḻuttōṭuṅ kalattaliṉṟit taṉi niṟṟalāl taṉinili yeṉappaṭṭa teṉṟalu moṉṟu. eṉak kūṟiyirutta lāṉum avvuri poruntāteṉka. āytam kakarattiṉpāṟ paṭṭa oru nuṇṇiya voli yāyiruttal paṟṟiyē appeyar peṟṟateṉ ṟaṟika.
ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam  (uriyiyal. 32)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. āytap peyarkkāraṇaṅ kūṟumiṭattu, “that which is subtle, minute” eṉṟār kālṭuvelum.
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻakarāti, tāṉ koṇṭa kuṟikkōṭkoppa, tamiḻc coṟkaḷit tamiḻvaḻi ārāyātu, āytam eṉṉum teṉcollic cārntirukkiyeṉṟu poruḷpaṭum āciritam eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ ṟiripā yirukkalā meṉak karuti mayaṅkukiṉṟatu. kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟṟiyalikaram āytam eṉac cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟāyirukka, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiṟku maṭṭum cārntirukkip poruṭcol eṅṅaṉam peyarā yamintiruttal kūṭum? vaṭamoḻiyit tamiḻukkaṭippaṭiyāy vittu, olimuṟic colliyal (Sound Etymology) paṟṟit teṉ coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟi vaṭacolleṉak kūṟuvatu, vaṭamoḻi yāḷarkkut toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkamāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, āytam eṉṉum teṉcolli āyutam eṉṉum vaṭa colloṭu mayakki, aṭuppukkūṭṭum kēṭakam mūvilic cūlam ākiya paṭikkalamum pōl varivaṭivu koṇṭiruppatāl āytam eṉap peyar peṟṟatu eṉak kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭuvārum uḷar. āytam eṉṉum col oriṭattum āyutam eṉa eḻutap paṭāmiyiyum, āyutam eṉṉum collum āytam eṉa eḻutappaṭāmi yiyum, nōkkuka. āriyar nāvalan tēyattiṟku varumuṉṉarē, āyta veḻuttum ataṉ peyarum kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉṉum uṇmiyiyum aṟintu koḷka.
iṉi, yāpparuṅkala viruttiyurikārar uyirē meyyē eṉṉum nūṟpāvuriyil,
cārpiṟ ṟōṉṟum taṉmiya eṉṟu mikuttuc colliya vataṉāl, akarattoṭu yakaravoṟṟu vantum āytam vantum aikārattiṉ payattavāy niṟkum ...
āytamum yavvu mavvoṭu variṉē
aiyeṉe ḻuttoṭu meypeṟat tōṉṟum
eṉṟār avinayaṉār.
varalāṟu ... kaḵcu, kaḵtam, kaḵcam eṉa akarattoṭu āytam vantu kicu, kitam, kicam eṉa aikārattiṉ payattavāyiṉavāṟu ... eṉa urittiruppatu. āytattip poṟuttamaṭṭil uttikkup poruntuvataṉṟu. kaḵcu mutaliya mūṉṟum kicu mutaliya mūṉ ṟākat tirintirukkalāmēyaṉṟi, āytam yakaramey yot tolit tiruttal muṭiyātu. orukāl, kaḷ eṉpatu kaḵcu mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ aṭiyākaliṉ, atu kay eṉat tirintirukkalām. oppu nōkka : toḷ - toy, poḷ - poy.
āytap piṟappu
cārntuvari ṉallatu eṉṉum cārpeḻuttup piṟappu nūṟpāliṉ āṟṟoḻuk kik koṇṭukūṭṭākac citittu, iru kūṟākki, cila eḻuttukaḷiccārntu tōṉṟiṉallatu tamakkeṉat tōṉṟutaṟku ōriya pilaveṉṟu, ārāyntu veḷippaṭukkappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ tammuṭiya piṟappiyalpu mūṉṟiṉiyuṅ kūṟuṅkāl, tattamakku uriya cārpākiya meykaḷatu ciṟappup piṟappiṭattē piṟattalōṭu porunti naṭakkum eṉṟum, oḻinta āytan tamakkup poruntiṉa neñcu vaḷiyāṟ piṟakkum eṉṟum urittaṉar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
in nūṟpā muḻuvatiyum orē kūṟṟākak koṇṭu, cila eḻuttukaḷic cārntuvariṉallatu tamakkeṉat taṉiyiyal pillātaṉaveṉa ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭa (kuṟṟiyalu karamum kuṟṟiyalikaramum āytamumākiya) maṟṟa mūṉṟu eḻuttu kaḷum, tattam mutaleḻuttukaḷiṉ piṟappoṭu porunti avaṟṟō ṭorupuṭi yotta tōṟṟattiṉavāyt tattam ciṟapput taṉmiyuṭaṉ naṭakkum eṉṟurippatē poruttamām.
iṉi āytan talivaḷiyāṉum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉum piṟakku meṉpārum uḷar eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
āytac cāriyi
kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi
nērat tōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyi  (puṇariyal, 32)
eṉṉum eḻuttuccāriyi nūṟpāvil āytaveḻuttiṟkuriya cāriyiyi āciriyar kūṟāviṭiṉum, nērattōṉṟum eṉavē nērat tōṉṟātaṉavum uḷavāyiṉa. avi āṉam, ēṉam, ōṉam eṉka. ivi citinta vaḻakkēṉuṅ kaṭiyalākāvāyiṉa eṉṟu uriyāciriyar (nacciṉārkkiṉiyar) kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. tolkāppiya nūlil kūṟappaṭāta cila ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ uri yāciriyarāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ āytaveḻuttuc cāriyiyum oṉṟu. itu uriyiṟ kōṭal eṉṉum utti paṟṟiyatu.
āytac cārpu
āytam, kuṟilukku muṉṉum valliṉa vuyirmeykkup piṉṉumāka avviraṇṭiṟkumiṭiyil varuvatē iyalpām.
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
uyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micittē  (moḻi marapu. 5)
eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār.
āytan tāṉē kuṟiyataṉ kīḻttāy
valiyataṉ mēlvan tiyalu meṉpa
eṉṟār kiyaṉār.
iv vitikku, eḵku kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu, aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, aḵkam, veḵkā, eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu eṉa yāpparuṅkala viruttiyuri yāciriyarum, eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu eṉak kuṇa cākararum, eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu eṉa mayili nātarum eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉar. ivi taṉic coṟkaḷ. aḵṟiṇi, muḵṭītu, aḵkakūṭiya eṉpaṉa toṭarccoṟkaḷ.
vilaḵḵki līṅkiru ḷōṭṭumē māta
rivaḵḵku mutti ṉiṉam
eṉa ivvāṟu kuṟiliṇik kīḻum āytam varumālōveṉiṉ, aṉṉa iyalpākavē āytamāy niṟpaṉavalla. ōci niṟittaṟ poruṭṭu oṟṟiṉ vaḻi oṟṟākkutaliṉ, īṇṭu āytamum oṟṟāy varumeṉak koḷka eṉṟu mayilinātar kūṟiyiruppatu iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
laḷaḵkāṉ (nūṉmarapu. 24) ṇaṉaḵkāṉ (nūṉmarapu. 26) eṉat tol kāppiyattil vantiruppavi ummit toṭarkaḷāta liṉ, kuṟiliṇikkīḻ vantaṉavākā. āyiṉ, iḷaḵkumē eṉac cintāmaṇiyil (149) vantiruppatu vitikku māṟātaliṉ, itaṉi yum oṟṟil vaḻiyoṟṟeṉavē koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
āytam tōṉṟum vaki
lakarattiripu. vakarattiripu, ḷakarattiripu, oṟṟil vaḻiyoṟṟu, cāriyip puṇarppu eṉa āytam tōṉṟum vaki aintām.
takaram varuvaḻi yāyta niliyalum
pukariṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulami yōrē  (puḷḷimayaṅkiyal 74)
eṉpatu lakarattiripu. kaḵṟītu, kaṟṟītu eṉpaṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu. ivi uṟaḻcci, aḵṟiṇi paḵṟuḷi eṉpaṉa uṟaḻcciyillātaṉa.
vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi yāyta mākum  (puḷḷimayaṅkiyal 84)
eṉpatu vakarattiripu. aḵkaṭiya, iḵkaṭiya, uḵkaṭiya, ciṟiya, tīya, periya eṉpaṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu.
āyta niliyalum varinili yiṉṟē
takaram varūuṅ kāli yāṉa  (puḷḷimayaṅkiyal 104)
eṉpatu ḷakarattiripu. muḵṭītu. muṭṭītu eṉpaṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu. ivi uṟaḻcci.
oṟṟil vaḻiyoṟṟu muṉṉark kāṭṭappeṟṟatu.
aḵkāṉ, maḵkāṉ, vaḵkāṉ eṉpaṉa cāriyip puṇarppu.
aḵtu eṉpataṉ kuṟukkamē atu eṉat teritaliṉ. ceyvaḵtu eṉpatu ceyvatu eṉpataṉ virittal tiripeṉak koḷvatu cariyaṉṟu.
vakarattiripum cāriyip puṇarppum ottiyalamipu (assimilation) eṉṉum neṟimuṟi paṟṟiyaṉa.
āytam varum taṉic coṟkaḷ
āytac cārpiyum āytan tōṉṟum vakiyiyum tuṇik koṇṭu āytam varum taṉic coṟkaḷi āyntu pārppiṉ, avaṟṟuṭ perumpālāṉa lakara ḷakarat tiripākavē pōtarum. it tiripu kumarināṭṭiṉ oru maruṅku tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
e.kā. aḵku
alkutal = curuṅkutal, kuṉṟutal, nuṇukutal.
alku - aḵku.
aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu, eḵtu.
ivi, al + tu, il + tu, ul + tu, el + tu eṉṉum puṇarcciyākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. aṉṟu iṉṟu eṉṟu eṉṉum kālac cuṭṭu viṉāc coṟkaḷiṉ aṭikaḷum, al il el eṉpaṉavē. ivaṟṟi, alli illi elli eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭa iṭaccuṭṭu viṉāc coṟkaḷoṭum, aṉṟu (al + tu) iṉṟu (il+tu) naṉṟu (nal+tu) eṉṉum oṉṟaṉpāṟ kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟuc coṟkaḷoṭum, oppunōkkuka.
al+tu = aḵtu - attu - atu. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉiyavum.
attoṭu niṉṟatu aliccal, koṭṭoṭu niṉṟatu kuliccal, ettāl vāḻalām ottāl vāḻalām eṉpaṉa paḻamoḻikaḷ.
paḵtu pal + tu = paḵtu - pattu - patu.
pal - paṉ = pattu. o.nō. āl - āṉ. vel - veṉ.
paṉ + iraṇṭu = paṉṉiraṇṭu.
paṉ - pāṉ = pattu. irupāṉ muppāṉ mutaliya eṇṇup peyarkaḷi nōkkuka.
mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiya oṉṟu mutaṟ pattu varippaṭṭa eṇṇup peyarkaḷuḷ, oṉpatiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa eṇṇip pala eṉṉum karuttuppaṟṟip pal eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu amittatākat terikiṉṟatu.
eḵku
iḷ (- iḷa - iḷaku) - eḷ - eḷku - eḵku = urukku. atāvatu iḷakiṉa tātu (ulōkam). eḵku - eḵkam = urukkiṉāṟ ceyta vēl.
paḵṟi
pal + ti = paḵṟi (paṉṟi) = paṉṟi vaṭivāṉa oruvakip paṭaku. itil carakkēṟṟic celvati iṉṟum kūvam mutaliya āṟukaḷil pārkkalām.
veḵku
piḷ - piṇ - piṇā - piṇavu - piṇaval. piṇā - piṇi.
piḷ - peḷ - peṇ - pēṇ - pēṭu - pēṭi
peḷ - peṭṭi - peṭi.
peḷ - peṭpu = viruppam.
peḷ - veḷ - vey - veymmi - vemmi = vēṇṭal.
veḷ - vēḷ - vēṇ - vēṇṭu.
veḷ - veḷku - veḵku.
veḵkutal = piṟaṉ poruḷi virumputal.
kaḵṟu
kal - kāl = karumi.
kāltōy mēṉik kaṇṭakar (kamparā. vāṉara. 21)
kal - kaḷ - kāḷ - kāḷam = karumi.
kaḷ + tu = kaḵṟu (karumi)
kaḵḵṟeṉṉuṅ kallatarattam  (tol. eḻut. 40. uri)
kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu mutaliya piṟa āytac coṟkaḷiṉ vērpporuḷkaḷ maṟintu pōyiṟṟeṉka.
āyta varivaṭivu
āytavoli, aṭuppil neruppi aṇikkumpōtu uṇṭākum ōciyi orupuṭi ottiruppatāl, orukāl, ataṉ varivaṭivu aṭuppuk kūṭṭuppōl muppuḷḷi vaṭivāy amik kappaṭṭirukkalām.
muṭipu
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl. āytam tamiḻeḻutteṉṟum. atu lakara ḷakara vakarat tiripāṉa nuṇ kakara meyyeṉṟum, oṉṟā yiruttalum uyirēṟāmiyumpaṟṟit taṉinili yeṉappaṭṭa teṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka. iṉi āytaveḻuttit tamiḻeḻuttu kaḷiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum iṭṭu. ja. ṣa. F,Z mutaliya piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukkaḷi ikkālattuc cilar kuṟippatu. tamiḻiyalpiṟku māṟāṉatum tollāciriyar kaṭṭaḷikku muraṇāṉatumāku meṉṟum, piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyellām, avvāṟākkiṉ tamiḻ nāḷaṭivil vēṟu moḻiyāka māṟiviṭumeṉṟum, aṟintu kaṭippiṭikka. mēlum, āytavolikku ayaṉ moḻi yolikaḷik kuṟikkum āṟṟalilliyeṉṟum, āyta varivaṭi veḻutuvatum ayaṉ moḻi varivaṭiveḻutuvatum oṉṟē yeṉṟum eḻutteṉpatu uṇmiyil oliyēyaṉṟi varivaṭivaṉ ṟeṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.  (kaḻakattiṉ 1008āvatu veḷiyīṭṭu viḻā malar.)
āyam
āyam - āya (i.vē.)
tāyam - āyam = urimi, kaṭami, kaṭattakkūli, tāya āṭṭakkāy, tāya eṇ allatu paṇiyam.
uruḷāyam ōvātu kūṟiṟ poruḷāyam
pōoyp puṟamē paṭum.  (kuṟaḷ. 933).
āyattiṟ kañci āṟṟil nīntiṉatupōl.
eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
irukku vētattil āya eṉṉum col vantucērki eṉṉum poruḷil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu (ii, 38: 20). pāṇiṉīyam, maṉumiruti, makāpāratam mutaliya piṉṉūlkaḷ varuvāy, vari, ūtiyam mutaliya poruḷkaḷil āḷkiṉṟaṉa. āya eṉṉum coṟku ā-i eṉṉum mūlaṅ kāṭṭuvar. i eṉpatu iy eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ citivām. iy-iyal, iyaṅku.  (va.va. : 84.)
āyam : (ka) tāy - tāyam = tāyvaḻiyurimi. mutaṟkālattiṟ kuṭumpat talimi tāṅkiyatu tāyē. tāyam - āyam = urimi. tāyam - tāya (va.).
āyam : (u) vā - ā - āyam = varuvāy, vari, kaṭṭaṇam. ōṭivā eṉpatu kocci vaḻakkil ōṭiyā eṉṟu collappaṭutal kāṇka. vā eṉṉuñ collē āv eṉṟu muṉpiṉṉāka muṟi māṟi intiyil vaḻaṅkum. āyam - āya (va.).  (ti.ma. 737.)
āyirattāḻi
kōlap pāṉiyi āyiraṅkaṇṇup pāṉiyeṉṟum, aracāṇip pāṉiyeṉṟum āyirattāḻiyeṉṟuñ colva tuṇṭu.  (ta.ti.22.)
āyiram
āyiram - sakara (i.vē.)
ayir = nuṇmaṇal. ayir - ayiram - āyiram.
āṟṟu maṇalum kaṭaṟkari maṇalum ērāḷamāyiruppatāl, maṇaṟ peyar oru peruntokip peyarāyiṟṟu.
vāḻiya ... naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē.  (puṟam 9).
nī nīṭuvāḻiya ... vaṭuvāḻ ekkar maṇaliṉum palavē  (puṟam. 55).
ma. āyiram, ku. āyire, ka. cāvira, tu. cāvira, tuṭa. cōver (f), kō. cāvrm.
i. hacār (haza#r), pe. hacār (haza#r), ki khilloi for khesloi.
vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamilli. akaramutal cakara mutalāyt tirivatum yakaram vakaramāy māṟuvatum iyalpātalāl, kaṉṉaṭattil āyiram eṉpatu cāvira eṉat tirintatu.
o.nō. iḷi - ciḷi, uti - cuti, uvaṇam - cuvaṇam, ēṇ cēṇ. nīyir - nīvir, cēyaṭi - cēvaṭi.
kaṉṉaṭac colli yoṭṭiyē tuḷuvac collum, kaṉṉaṭat tiripāṉa paṭakac colli yoṭṭiyē tuṭavac collum kōttac collum, ivaṟṟi yoṭṭiyē vaṭacollum, cakara mutalāyt tirintuḷḷaṉa. iti aṟiyātu, pērā. parō vaṭa colliyē teṉcoṟku mūlamākat tam akaramutaliyiṟ kāṭṭiyiruppatu, tamiḻip paṟṟiya tam tavaṟṟuk karuttiṉālēyē.  (va.va. 84-85.)
ār
ār - ara (i.vē.) 
ārtal = poruntutal. ār = cakkarak kuṟaṭṭilum vaṭṭi yilum poruntum caṭṭam (spoke, radius).
ārkeḻu kuṟaṭucūṭṭāḻi pōṉṟu  (cīvaka. 828).
vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṭṭappeṟum vēr ru (ri) eṉpatē. aṟu iri eṉṉum teṉ colliṉ tiripākum.  (va.va.85.)
ārppāṭṭam
ārppāṭṭam - ārapāṭṭa (bh).
ārttal = āravārittal. ārppu + āṭṭam - ārppāṭṭam = āravāram. te. ārpāṭṭamu (bh).  (va.va.85.)
āram
āram1 - āra
ār - āram. ārañ cūḻnta ayilvāy nēmi (ciṟupāṇ. 253). va.va.85.
āram2 - hāra
ārtal = poruntutal. ār - āram = pala malarkaḷ allatu maṇikaḷ poruntiya māli.
o.nō. toṭu - toṭi.  (va.va. 84.)
